Though Thou didst descend into the tomb as dead, O Giver of life, yet Thou didst tear asunder the power of Hades, O Christ, and didst raise with Thyself the dead whom it had formerly swallowed; and as God Thou didst grant resurrection unto all who magnify Thee in faith and love!

*Troparion:*

Let creation rejoice, and let all the earth-born be glad; for Hades, the enemy, has been despoiled! Christ is risen, and the first-formed arose with Him in glory, being delivered from corruption; unto the Myrrh-bearing women [f. 103A] the Angel cries: Let creation rejoice, and let it blossom like a lily! For Christ is risen from the dead as God; where is thy victory, O Hades? He has cast thee down to the earth, having exalted our horn, as He is compassionate!

*Theotokion:*

Creation rejoices with thy childbirth, O Virgin; for Bethlehem opened Eden unto us, and enjoying the Tree of Life in faith, we cry fervently: Fulfill our petitions, O Lady!

*Another Canon to the Theotokos. Heirmos: It is not possible for man to see God...*

*Troparion:*

A Star shining with the rays of the Godhead from Jacob shone forth unto those held by darkness; for through thee, O all-holy one, Christ God the Word was incarnate, by Whom being enlightened, with the heavenly hosts we call thee blessed!

*Troparion:*

Strengthened by thy power and grace, I have fervently offered a hymn from the heart unto thee; do thou receive it, O pure Virgin, granting in return thy radiant grace from incorruptible treasures, O divinely-blessed one!

*Troparion:*

Thou didst appear openly as a divinely-woven loom, whereon the Word wove the vesture of the flesh, deifying my form, O Virgin; and clothed therein, He saved all who with a pure mind magnify thee!

[f. 104A] *Theotokion:* [^122]

Resurrection has now been granted unto the dead by thine ineffable and unspeakable childbirth, O all-venerable Mother of God; for Life, clothed in the flesh from thee, has shone forth unto all, and openly destroyed the infirmity of death!

*On Sunday at the Praises, Resurrectional Stichera, Tone 6:*

*Sticheron:*

Thy Cross, O Lord, is life and resurrection unto Thy people; and hoping thereon, we sing unto Thee, our risen God: Have mercy upon us!

*Sticheron:*

Thy burial, O Master, opened Paradise unto mankind, and being delivered from corruption, we sing unto Thee, our risen God: Have mercy upon us!

*Sticheron:*

With the Father and the Spirit let us hymn Christ Who is risen from the dead, and cry unto Him: Thou art our life and resurrection, have mercy upon us!

*Sticheron:*

On the third day Thou didst arise from the tomb, O Christ, as it is written, and didst raise our Forefather with Thyself; therefore the human race glorifies Thee, and hymns Thy Resurrection!

*Other Stichera, Anatolian, Same Tone:*

*Sticheron:*

O Lord, great and fearful is the mystery of Thy Resurrection; for in such manner didst Thou come forth from the tomb [f. 104A] like a Bridegroom from His chamber, having shattered death by death that Thou mightest set Adam free; therefore in heaven the Angels dance, and on earth mankind glorifies Thy tender mercy shown unto us, O Lover of mankind!

*Verse: I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart; I will speak of all Thy marvelous works.*

O lawless Judeans, where are the seals and the silver coins that ye gave unto the soldiers? The treasure was not stolen, but is risen as mighty; ye yourselves were put to shame, having rejected Christ, the Lord of glory, Who suffered and was buried, and is risen from the dead: worship Him!

*Sticheron:*

Though the tomb was sealed, how were ye robbed, O Judeans, having set a guard and placed signs? While the doors were shut, the King came forth: either produce Him as dead, or worship Him as God, singing with us: Glory to Thy Cross, O Lord, and to Thy Resurrection!

*Verse: Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor unto the end.*

Reaching Thy life-receiving tomb early in the morning with weeping, O Lord, and bearing sweet spices, the Myrrh-bearing women sought to anoint Thy most pure body; but they found a radiant Angel sitting upon the stone, and speaking unto them [^123] [f. 105A] saying: Why weep ye for Him Who poured forth life unto the world from His side? Why seek ye the Immortal One in the tomb as dead? Run rather, announce unto His disciples His glorious Resurrection and worldwide joy; through which do Thou Who hast enlightened us also grant cleansing and great mercy!

## SUNDAY LITURGY: THE BEATITUDES (TONE 6)

*At the Divine Liturgy, the Beatitudes, Tone 6:*

*Troparion:*

Remember me, O God my Savior, when Thou comest into Thy Kingdom, and save me as the only Lover of mankind!

*Troparion:*

Adam was deceived by a tree, but by the Tree of the Cross Thou didst again save the Thief who cried: Remember me, O Lord, in Thy Kingdom!

*Troparion:*

Let us hymn Christ our God, Who was willingly crucified and buried, and arose in glory on the third day; Who shattered the gates and bars of Hades, O Giver of life, and raised up all who were in Hades, crying: O compassionate Savior, glory to Thy rising!

*Troparion:*

[He Who arose from the tomb on the third day, having foretold His rising] before the Passion, [...] let us all harmoniously sing praise [unto Him]!

[f. 105A] *Troparion:*

Having appeared as the light of the world, the Apostles drove away all the darkness of deception by the divine rays of their teachings!

*Glory:*

We doxologize the Father, and the Son, and the Holy Spirit, saying: O Holy Trinity, save our souls!

*Both now. Theotokion:*

Rejoice, gate opened unto God, Who ineffably took up His abode in thee and came forth from thee, O Ever-Virgin!

*End of the Sixth Tone.*

---

# TONE 7

## TONE 7 [^124]

## SATURDAY EVENING: VESPERS (TONE 7)

[f. 106A] *On Saturday Evening at Great Vespers, on "Lord, I have cried," Resurrectional Stichera, Tone 7:*

*Sticheron:*

Come, let us rejoice in the Lord, Who shattered the dominion of death and enlightened the human race, crying with the bodiless hosts: O our Creator and Savior, glory to Thee!

*Sticheron:*

Thou didst endure the Crucifixion and burial for our sake, O Savior; and by death as God Thou didst slay death. Therefore we worship Thy third-day Resurrection: O Lord, glory to Thee!

*Sticheron:*

Beholding the Resurrection of the Creator, the Apostles marveled, singing angelic praise: this is the glory of the Church, this is the wealth of the Kingdom! O Thou Who didst suffer for our sake: O Lord, glory to Thee!

*Other Stichera, Anatolian, Same Tone:*

*Sticheron:*

Though Thou wast seized, O Christ, by lawless men, yet Thou art my God, and I am not ashamed [f. 106A] I was scourged on the back, and I do not deny it; I was nailed to the Cross, and I do not conceal it; I boast in Thy Resurrection. For Thy death is my life, O almighty Lover of mankind: O Lord, glory to Thee!

*Sticheron:*

Fulfilling David's prophecy, O Christ, Thou didst reveal Thy majesty in Zion unto Thy disciples, showing Thyself praiseworthy and ever glorified together with the Father and the Holy Spirit: first indeed bodiless as the Word, but later incarnate for our sake as man, and risen with power as the Lover of mankind!

*Sticheron:*

Thou didst descend into Hades, O Christ, as Thou didst will; and Thou didst overthrow death as God and Master, and didst arise on the third day, raising Adam with Thyself from the bonds and corruption of Hades, as he cried out, saying: Glory to Thy Resurrection, O only Lover of mankind!

*Sticheron:*

Thou wast laid in the tomb as sleeping, O Lord; and Thou didst arise on the third day as mighty in strength, raising Adam with Thyself from the corruption of mortals, as almighty God!

*Glory. Both now. Theotokion:*

[f. 107A] Thou wast known as a Mother above nature, O Mother of God, and didst remain a Virgin above word and understanding; and the tongue cannot tell the wonder of thy childbirth! Glorious indeed is thy conception, O pure one, and incomprehensible is the manner of thy giving birth; for where God wills, the order of nature is overcome! Therefore, knowing thee all as the Mother of God, we pray thee fervently: intercede that our souls may be saved! [^125]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 7:*

*Sticheron:*

Risen from the tomb, O Savior of the world, Thou didst raise man with Thyself together with Thy flesh: O Lord, glory to Thee!

*Verse: The Lord is King, He is robed in majesty.*

Come, let us worship Him Who arose from the dead and enlightened all things; for from the tyranny of Hades He set us free by His third-day Resurrection, and granted us life and great mercy!

*Verse: For He hath established the world, so that it cannot be moved.*

Descending into Hades, O Christ, Thou didst despoil death, and rising on the third day, Thou didst raise us with Thyself, who glorify Thine almighty rising: O Lord, Lover of mankind!

*Verse: Holiness becomes Thine house, O Lord, unto length of days.*

Fearful didst Thou appear, O Lord, lying in the tomb as sleeping; but rising on the third day as mighty, Thou didst raise Adam with Thyself, who cried: Glory to Thy Resurrection [f. 107A] unto Thy Resurrection, O only Lover of mankind! Glory. Both now.

*Theotokion:*

Fleeing under thy protection, O Lady, all of us earth-born cry aloud unto thee: O Mother of God, our hope, deliver us from our boundless transgressions, and save our souls! *Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart. Trisagion through Our Father.*

*Resurrectional Troparion, Tone 7:*

Thou didst destroy death by Thy Cross, and didst open Paradise unto the Thief; Thou didst transform the lamentation of the Myrrh-bearing women, and didst command the Apostles to preach that Thou art risen, O Christ God, granting great mercy unto the world!

*Glory. Both now. Theotokion:*

As the treasure of our Resurrection, O all-hymned one, lead up from the pit and depth of transgressions those who trust in thee; for having given birth to our Salvation, thou hast saved those guilty of sin: who before childbirth wast a Virgin, and in childbirth a Virgin, and after childbirth art still a Virgin!

## SUNDAY MIDNIGHT OFFICE (TONE 7)

*On Sunday at the Midnight Office, Canon to the Holy and Life-Originating Trinity.*

*Ode 1, Tone 7. Heirmos: By Thy nodding upon earth, the form was transformed...*

*Troparion:*

Open my intelligible lips of the heart and material lips unto Thy praise, O thrice-radiant single Godhead of all, that I may sing a light-bestowing hymn of thanksgiving unto Thee!

[f. 108A] *Troparion:* [^126]

That Thou mightest show the exceeding abundance of Thy goodness, Thou didst create man, O Trinity of unassailable power, only as an earthen image of Thy primordial Masterhood, O Creator!

*Troparion:*

The unoriginate Father having begotten the co-everlasting Word, and the co-unoriginate Spirit having shone forth: grant us to worship the one God, identical in essence and three-hypostatic!

*Theotokion:*

Thou didst appear unto Moses at the bush, O Word of God, as a cleansing fire that consumed it not at all, prefiguring Thy fleshly incarnation from the Virgin, wherewith Thou didst re-fashion mankind!

*Ode 3. Heirmos: In the beginning, the heavens by Thine almighty word...*

*Troparion:*

Hymning Thee, the Master, as single, thrice-shining, and all-creating, we ask deliverance from sins and trials, O God of unassailable power: do not overlook those who glorify Thy goodness in faith!

*Troparion:*

A shoot sprouted forth from the Father, as from an unoriginate root, God the Word, equal in power with the co-sprouting [f. 108A] and the divine Spirit; and therefore we the faithful glorify the Trinity in Persons, the one Lordship!

*Troparion:*

Equally glorified and co-sprouting, we the faithful all doxologize Thee, the three-hypostatic Nature indivisibly and divisibly, the monarchical Trinity; and bowing down, we ask forgiveness of transgressions!

*Theotokion:*

Inalterably Thou wast made like unto men in all things, O Word of God, openly coming forth from the pure Maiden; and Thou didst show unto all the thrice-shining and single Godhead, of inalterable Hypostases in essence!

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 7:*

Have mercy upon Thy servants who have sinned, O Holy Trinity, and receive those who repent unto Thee, O Compassionate One, and vouchsafe forgiveness!

*Glory. Both now. Theotokion:*

Assuage our souls afflicted with sins, O all-holy Mother of God; deliver from transgressions those who hymn thee, O Bride of God!

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

All-holding, thrice-radiant Unity, divine Principle and Savior of all: protect now Thy singers, and save them from sorrow, passions, and every affliction!

*Troparion:*

Through symbolic sayings of Thine incomprehensible [^127] [f. 109A] thrice-radiant Godhead, at a loss we sing unto Thee, O Lover of mankind, O Lord, and we glorify Thy power!

*Troparion:*

On earth as in heaven, together with the bodiless choirs, O Unity and Trinity, we divide Thee indivisibly, and with love we glorify Thee, as doing good unto all existing things!

*Theotokion:*

Without departing from the Father's glory, Thou didst willingly descend unto our lowliness: incarnate, O supersubstantial One, Thou didst raise us up unto divine glory, as Thou art compassionate!

*Ode 5. Heirmos: Night is without light unto the unbelieving, O...*

*Troparion:*

O light-originating Nature, Trinity in hypostatic forms, and Unity in counsel, glory, and honor: establish us in Thy love!

*Troparion:*

Glorifying the Mind, the Word, and the Spirit, one divine-principled and thrice-solar Nature, we pray to be snatched away from trials and all afflictions!

*Theotokion:*

Taking form in human nature from the holy Virgin, O Word of God, Thou didst teach us to hymn the Trinity in Unity, of one form and co-enthroned!

*Ode 6. Heirmos: Sailing in the surge of the billows of...*

*Troparion:*

The co-glorified dominion, and the single-principled [f. 109A] divine Principle: existing in three Hypostases, without confusion with one another we glorify, possessing difference only in the manner of subsistence of each personal property!

*Troparion:*

The intelligible ranks of angelic splendors praise Thee, the thrice-solar divine Principle; with whom also we with earthen lips, as the Creator of all things, hymn and faithfully glorify as one!

*Theotokion:*

By an incomprehensible word, the Word begotten of the Father the Sun, another Sun before the ages, shone forth at the end of times from the Virgin, and proclaimed the one incomprehensible God in three Persons!

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 7:*

O consubstantial Trinity, three-hypostatic Unity: have mercy upon those whom Thou didst create, O Immortal One, burning up the malice of sinners, and enlightening the hearts of those who hymn Thy compassion: O our God, glory to Thee!

*Glory. Both now. Theotokion:*

Brighter than fire, more efficacious than light is the mercy of thy goodness, O Lady, burning up the sins of mankind, and bedewing the thoughts of those who praise thy wonders, O most immaculate Mother of God!

*Ode 7. Heirmos: The Children of old showed the fiery furnace...*

[f. 110A] *Troparion:* [^128]

Ever shining with light-beaming flashes, O thrice-radiant God, unapproachable and supersubstantial Unity: save those who piously believe in Thee, O Master, and bow down before Thee!

*Troparion:*

Obeying the sayings of the divine Prophets, Thee alone as God we glorify in every way in three Persons, crying thus: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers!

*Troparion:*

With earthen lips together with the immaterial ranks we sing hymns unto Thee, O Holy Trinity, in unity of essence, crying: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers!

*Theotokion:*

That He Who fashioned Adam might renew him again, He openly became man from thee, bringing mankind to life, who cry thus: Blessed, O all-pure one, is the fruit of thy womb!

*Ode 8. Heirmos: Unconsumed by fire, communicating at Sinai...*

*Troparion:*

Vouchsafe the hearts of Thy singers to be enlightened with the light-bestowing rays of the thrice-shining Sun, and grant now to all who hymn Thy majesty in similar faith, ever to behold Thy beauty as far as is possible, O Unity-Trinity, unto all ages!

*Troparion:*

Thou holdest all things, O Trinitarian and single [f. 110A] unoriginate Lordship; and Thou rulest both heaven and earth. Therefore, drawn ever by Thy love, preserve Thy singers to sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; hymn and supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

*Troparion:*

By Thy grace make me a temple of Thy thrice-radiant dawn, O Lover of mankind; and show me unapproachable in participation and communion unto invisible enemies and fleshly passions, O Master, my single-principled God and Lord of glory, to hymn Thee, O God, unto all ages!

*Theotokion:*

The divine-principled Light, shining forth from thy womb, O most pure Mother of God, illumined the whole world with thrice-solar light, and revealed the earth as another heaven, singing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; hymn and supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

*Ode 9. Heirmos: A Mother unto God and a Virgin, giving birth and remaining a virgin...*

*Troparion:*

We earthen ones are unable worthily to praise and hymn Thee in elevated words, Whom the Seraphim on high unceasingly hymn; yet daring in every way as our Master, we magnify Thee as our most loving God!

*Troparion:*

Deliver from bodily diseases and spiritual attach- [^129] [f. 111A] -ments Thy faithful singers, O single, indivisible Trinity, and grant them to be preserved unharmed, delivering them from every worldly calamity!

*Troparion:*

O equal in power, divine-principled, thrice-radiant, almighty Dominion, inalterable beauty, substantial Goodness: grant remission of transgressions unto Thy servants, and deliverance from trials and passions!

*Theotokion:*

Having taken mind and soul and fleshly commingling from thy most pure womb, O Mother of God, God the Word in truth appeared as man, and openly revealed man as a partaker of the divine nature!

## SUNDAY MATINS (TONE 7)

*On Sunday at Matins, on "God is the Lord," Troparion, Tone 7: Thou didst destroy [death] by Thy Cross... Glory. Both now. Theotokion.*

*After the First Stichology, Sessional Hymn, Tone 7:*

While the tomb was sealed, Thou didst shine forth Life from the tomb, O Christ God; and while the doors were shut, Thou didst stand before the disciples, renewing an upright Spirit in us through them according to Thy great mercy!

*Glory:*

He Who by His three-day burial despoiled death, and by His life-bearing rising raised up corrupt man, O Christ God, as [f. 111A] the Lover of mankind: glory to Thee!

*Both now. Theotokion:*

As thou hast tender mercy for our help, look upon those on earth, upon helpless people; have mercy, O blessed Mother of God, remain praying that we perish not grievously; as thou art compassionate, pray the merciful God that our souls may be saved, O most holy Virgin!

*After the Second Stichology, Sessional Hymn, Tone 7:*

Life lay in the tomb, and seals lay upon the stone; as a sleeping King soldiers watched Christ, and Angels glorified Him as immortal God; and the women cried aloud: The Lord is risen, granting great mercy unto the world!

*Glory:*

Unto the tomb ran the women with tears, bearing sweet spices; and while soldiers were guarding the King of all, they said among themselves: Who will roll away the stone for us? The Angel of Great Counsel is risen, trampling down death: O almighty Lord, glory to Thee!

*Both now. Theotokion:*

Unceasingly pray Him Who was crucified for our sake, Christ our God, and Who cast down the dominion of death, O Virgin Mother of God, that He may save our souls!

*Hypakoe, Tone 7:*

[f. 112A] Thou Who didst take our form and endure the Crucifixion in the flesh: save us by Thy Resurrection, O Christ God, as the Lover of mankind! [^130]

*The Hymns of Degrees, Tone 7. Antiphon 1:*

*Troparion:*

Turning back the captivity of Zion from deception, quicken me also, O Savior, delivering me from the slavery of passions!

*Troparion:*

They that sow in the south with tears the sorrows of fasting, shall reap joyous sheaves of ever-living nourishment!

*Troparion:*

Through the Holy Spirit is the fountain of divine treasures, from Whom comes wisdom, understanding, and fear: unto Him be praise and glory, honor and dominion!

*Antiphon 2:*

*Troparion:*

Except the Lord build the house of the soul, in vain do we labor; for without Him neither deed nor word is accomplished!

*Troparion:*

Of the fruit of the womb, the saints, moved by the Spirit, sprout forth paternal traditions of adoption!

*Troparion:*

Through the Holy Spirit all things have their being; for before all He is God, the Lordship of all, unapproachable Light, and the Life of all!

*Antiphon 3:*

[f. 112A] *Troparion:*

They that fear the Lord, having found the way of life, now and ever are blessed with incorruptible glory!

*Troparion:*

Beholding thy offspring round about thy table like shoots, rejoice and be glad, bringing them unto Christ, the Chief Shepherd!

*Troparion:*

Through the Holy Spirit is a great depth of gifts, the wealth of glory, a great depth of judgments; for He is equally glorified with the Father and the Son, and co-worshipped!

*Prokeimenon, Tone 7: Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up, forget not Thy poor unto the end. Verse: I will confess Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart.*

*Let every breath praise the Lord. Matins Resurrection Gospel. Psalm 50.*

*Resurrection Canon, Tone 7.*

*Ode 1. Heirmos:*

By Thy nodding upon earth, the form was transformed, the nature of water which formerly flowed freely, O Lord; whence Israel passed dry-shod, and sings a song of victory unto Thee!

*Troparion:*

The tyranny of death was condemned by the tree, when Thou, O Lord, wast condemned to an unjust death; whence the prince of darkness, failing to prevail against Thee, was justly cast out!

[f. 113A] *Troparion:* [^131]

Hades drew near unto Thee, and not being able to crush Thy body with its teeth, shattered its own jaws; and Thou, O Savior, having loosed the pangs of death, didst arise on the third day!

*Theotokion:*

The sorrows of our foremother Eve were loosed; for having escaped pangs, without knowing man thou didst give birth; whence knowing thee openly as the Mother of God, O all-pure one, we all glorify thee!

*Another Canon, of the Cross and Resurrection, Same Tone.*

*Ode 1. Heirmos: In the deep He overwhelmed Pharaoh...*

*Troparion:*

Two life-bearing streams the Savior poured forth unto us upon the Cross from His pierced side; let us sing unto Him, for He is glorified!

*Troparion:*

Having lodged in the tomb, and risen on the third day, Christ granted hope of incorruption unto mortals; let us sing unto Him, for He is glorified!

*Theotokion:*

Thou didst appear as the only Virgin even after childbirth; for thou didst bear incarnate the Creator of the world: Rejoice! we all cry unto thee.

*Another Canon to the Mother of God.*

*Ode 1. Heirmos: By Thy nod upon earth is fashioned the...*

*Troparion:*

O Virgin who didst bear the Abyss of compassion, enlighten my soul with thy light-bearing radiance, that I may worthily hymn the abyss of thy wonders!

*Troparion:*

Beholding those wounded by the arrow of sin, the Word [f. 113A] beholding them, as Benefactor had compassion; whence ineffably the all-divine One was united, O all-pure one, to the flesh taken from thee!

*Troparion:*

Mankind's nature became subject unto death, corruptible and mortal, O Lady; but having conceived Life, thou didst lead it up from corruption unto life!

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

In the beginning having established the heavens by Thine almighty Word, O Lord Savior, and all their host by the all-creating and divine Spirit: establish me upon the immovable rock of Thy confession!

*Troparion:*

Ascending the tree, Thou didst willingly sorrow for our sake, O compassionate Savior, and didst endure wounding that intercedes for reconciliation and salvation unto the faithful; whereby, O Merciful One, we were all reconciled unto Thy Father!

*Troparion:*

Cleansing me from the wound, who was wounded in soul by the serpent's bite, O Christ, Thou didst show light unto me who of old lay in darkness and corruption; for descending into Hades by the Cross, Thou didst raise me with Thyself!

*Theotokion:*

By the prayers of Thy Mother who knew not man, grant peace unto the world [^132] [f. 114A] O Savior, and give victory unto the king over opposing enemies, and vouchsafe those who glorify Thee to partake of Thine ineffable glory!

*Another Canon. Heirmos:*

The flaming sword Thou didst quench with the blood that flowed from Thy side, O Lord, when lawless people nailed Thee to the tree of the Cross; whereby the Thief found Paradise, and I boast in hope of the Cross, that receiving remission of sins for its sake, O Christ, I may hymn Thee!

*Troparion:*

Risen from Thy tomb, O Christ, Thou didst first appear unto the women, and didst loose the sorrows of Eve; and Adam fallen into corruption Thou didst raise up with Thyself as compassionate God: therefore we all hymn Thy rising!

*Theotokion:*

The Lord Whom thou didst receive in a virginal womb, and Whom thou didst bear in the flesh, O pure one: through thy maternal prayers grant Him to be propitious unto us, His servants, as we know thee alone as a haven of compassion towards God!

*Another Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos: The serpent creeping out of Eden...*

*Troparion:*

The serpent creeping out of Eden, having deceived me through desire of deification, cast me down unto the earth; but He Who is merciful by nature and compassionate, had mercy [f. 114A] having compassion, He made me godlike, taking up His abode in thy womb, and became like unto me, O Mother Virgin!

*Troparion:*

Blessed is the fruit of thy womb, O Virgin Mother of God, joy of all; for thou didst bear joy and true gladness unto all the world, which dispels the sorrow of sins, O Bride of God!

*Troparion:*

Thou didst bear eternal Life and Light and Peace unto us, O Mother of God, Virgin, calming through faith and confession of thy grace the ancient warfare of men towards God and the Godhead!

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Without leaving the Father's bosom, and descending unto earth, O Christ God, I have heard the mystery of Thy dispensation, and I have glorified Thee, O only Lover of mankind!

*Troparion:*

Offering His back unto wounds, He Who was incarnate from the Virgin, the guiltless Master, is scourged for the servant who sinned, loosing my transgressions!

*Troparion:*

Before the judgment seat, the lawless judge holds Him guilty as the condemned; and He is struck with an earthen hand, Who created man as God, and judges the earth in righteousness!

*Theotokion:*

As truly the Mother of God, pray Thy Creator and Son [^133] [f. 115A] O all-immaculate one, to direct me unto the saving haven of His glorious will!

*Another Canon. Heirmos: The Prophet, beholding the last things...*

*Troparion:*

Knowing no sin, yet for this cause Thou becamest, O Lord, what Thou wast not, taking what was alien unto Thyself, that Thou mightest save the world and slay the tyrant by deceit!

*Troparion:*

Thou wast lifted up upon the Cross, and didst loose the sin of Forefather Adam; on account of which I have heard of Thy power, that Thou didst come to save all Thine anointed ones!

*Theotokion:*

Proceeding from the mountain of the Pharan division, the Virgin was enrolled with the servants, delivering all from slavery, ruling unto the ages: therefore we glorify thee who gavest birth!

*Another Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos: Without leaving the Father's bosom...*

*Troparion:*

Appearing all-elect and fair unto God before creation by thy brightness, O all-pure one, enlighten with light-bestowing grace those who hymn thee!

*Troparion:*

Thou didst bear God for mankind, O pure one, incarnate of thy pure blood, delivering from many transgressions those who glorify and honor thee with love, O Virgin Mother!

*Troparion:*

The rational nature celebrates a sacred ministry: shining [f. 115A] forth from thee, O all-hymned one, having now learned the ineffable mystery of thy childbirth, O most blessed one!

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Night without light unto the faithless, O Christ, but unto the faithful enlightenment in enjoyment of Thy words; therefore early in the morning do I rise unto Thee, and hymn Thy Godhead!

*Troparion:*

For the sake of Thy servants Thou wast sold, O Christ, and Thou didst endure buffeting upon the cheek, interceding for freedom unto those who sing: Early in the morning do I rise unto Thee, and hymn Thy Godhead!

*Troparion:*

By Thy divine power, O Christ, through fleshly weakness Thou didst cast down the strong one; and Thou didst reveal Him as the conqueror of death, O Savior, for the sake of the Resurrection!

*Theotokion:*

Thou didst bear God, O pure Mother, divinely incarnate from thee, O all-hymned one; since thou knewest not male seed, but didst give birth from the Holy Spirit!

*Another Canon. Heirmos: O Lord my God, from the night I rise early unto Thee...*

*Troparion:*

When Thou wast reckoned among the lawless, being lifted up upon the Place of the Skull, the luminaries hid themselves, the earth quaked, and the temple veil was rent in twain, revealing the falling away of the Hebrews!

*Troparion:*

Thee Who didst destroy all the power of the tyrant by the might of Thine incomprehensible Godhead, and didst raise the dead [^134] [f. 116A] by Thy Resurrection, we glorify with hymns!

*Theotokion:*

O Mother of the King and God, all-hymned Mother of God: through thy prayers send down cleansing of transgressions unto those who ever praise thee in faith and love with hymns!

*Another Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos: Night without light unto the faithless...*

*Troparion:*

Beholding the ladder fixed on high, Jacob was taught the figure of thee who knewest not wedlock; for through thee, O all-venerable Lady, God communicated with mankind!

*Troparion:*

Having now found eternal deliverance through thee, O Virgin, fervently we cry unto thee: Rejoice, O Bride of God! And having rejoiced in thy light, O all-pure one, with hymns we magnify thee!

*Troparion:*

Finding thee alone as a lily among thorns, shining with the brightness of purity and with the lights of virginity, O all-hymned one, the Bridegroom brought thee as a bride!

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Sailing in the surge of worldly cares, sinking with the ship through sins, and cast before the soul-destroying beast, like Jonah I cry unto Thee, O Christ: Lead me up from the deadly deep!

[f. 116A] *Troparion:*

The imprisoned in Hades remembered Thee, the souls of the righteous who were left; and they prayed for salvation from Thee, which Thou, O Christ, hast granted by the Cross unto those in the nethermost parts, having come as the Tender-hearted!

*Troparion:*

Looking upon Thy living and not-made-with-hands temple, which was destroyed by sufferings, of old the choir of Apostles despaired; yet beyond hope having worshipped, they proclaimed Thy Resurrection everywhere!

*Theotokion:*

Who among men can declare the image of thine ineffable childbirth, O all-immaculate Virgin, Bride of God, for our sake? For God the uncircumscribed Word was united unto me, and became flesh from thee!

*Another Canon. Heirmos: I cried unto the Lord in my affliction...*

*Troparion:*

Being crucified, the Master shook all the earth, and as good healed its grievous afflictions!

*Troparion:*

Death was destroyed, and Hades was despoiled; for Christ is risen, making a path unto mankind by His Resurrection!

*Theotokion:*

O most pure Lady, certain Hope: preserve those who in love hymn thee as the true Mother of God!

*Another Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos: Sailing in the surge...*

*Troparion:*

The types of the Law and the prophecies of the Prophets clearly foretold thee, who wast to give birth [^135] [f. 117A] unto the Benefactor of all creation, O pure one, Who in many parts and in diverse manners hath shown goodness unto those who faithfully hymn thee!

*Troparion:*

Adam the first-formed, who became alienated of old by the counsel of the murderer of man, thou hast led back, O Bride of God, into the divine delights of Paradise, having given birth unto Him Who snatched us from transgression!

*Troparion:*

He Who by divine light and creative power framed and upholds all things, came forth from thy womb, O pure one, and shone with divine-originating rays upon those sitting in the darkness of death!

*Kontakion, Tone 7:*

No longer can the dominion of death hold mankind; for Christ came down, shattering and destroying its powers! Hades is bound; the Prophets rejoice with one accord, saying: The Savior hath appeared unto those in faith; come forth, ye faithful, unto the Resurrection!

*Ikos:*

The nethermost parts below trembled today, Hades and death, before the One of the Trinity; the earth shook, and the gatekeepers of Hades, seeing Thee [f. 117A] were terrified! And all creation, rejoicing with the Prophets, sings unto Thee a song of victory, unto our Deliverer God, Who hath now destroyed the power of death! Let us shout aloud and cry out unto Adam and unto those born of Adam: The Tree hath led him in again; come forth, ye faithful, unto the Resurrection!

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

Of old the Children showed the fiery furnace dew-bearing, hymning the one God and saying: O God of our fathers, supremely exalted, blessed art Thou!

*Troparion:*

By the tree Adam is put to death, having willingly committed disobedience; but by Christ's obedience he is renewed again; for for my sake the Son of God, the supremely glorified, is crucified!

*Troparion:*

All creation hymns Thee, O Christ, risen from the tomb; for Thou, the supremely glorified Light, hast blossomed forth as Life unto those in Hades, the Resurrection of the dead, and unto those in darkness!

*Theotokion:*

Rejoice, daughter of corruptible Adam; rejoice, only Bride of God; rejoice, through whom corruption was driven away, for thou didst bear God, Whom implore [^136] [f. 118A] O pure one, that we all may be saved!

*Another Canon. Heirmos: The Children of old showed the fiery furnace...*

*Troparion:*

O Thou Who on the tree of the Cross didst blunt the sting of sin, and didst tear up the handwriting of Adam's transgression by the spear of Thy side: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers!

*Troparion:*

O Thou Who wast pierced in the side, and by the sprinkling of divine blood didst cleanse the earth defiled by the blood of idols: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers!

*Trinity Hymn:*

Orthodoxly we theologize the indivisible Nature: God the unoriginate Father, and the Word and Spirit of the same dominion, crying: Blessed art Thou, O consubstantial Trinity!

*Another Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos: The Children did not burn in the furnace...*

*Troparion:*

Having thee as an adorned and gilded vessel, O Virgin, Thy Creator and Lord loved thee: the God of our fathers, supremely exalted and supremely glorified!

*Troparion:*

Isaiah of old was cleansed, O Maiden, having received the coal, seeing in figure and symbolically thy divine childbirth, [hymning] the God of our fathers, supremely exalted and supremely glorified!

*Troparion:*

Symbolic types of old [f. 118A] of thy divine childbirth beholding, the divine Prophets, joyfully singing, cried aloud: O God of our fathers, supremely exalted and supremely glorified!

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Communing with the fire on Sinai without burning, the bush revealed God unto slow-tongued and heavy-voiced Moses; and the zeal of God showed the three Children unharmed singers in the fire: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto the ages!

*Troparion:*

Slain for the world, the all-pure spiritual Lamb brought to an end the sacrifices offered according to the Law, cleansing transgressions by His blood as God, of those who ever cry aloud: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto the ages!

*Troparion:*

Our flesh, which was assumed by the Creator, being incorruptible before the Passion, was made after the Passion and Resurrection untouched by corruption, and renews mortal man, who cries: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto the ages!

*Theotokion:*

Thy precious and all-immaculate [giving birth], O Virgin, cleansed the foul and abominable defilement of the world, and became the cause of our reconciliation unto God, O all-pure one; therefore [^137] [f. 119A] thee, O Virgin, do we supremely exalt unto all ages!

*Another Canon. Heirmos: The only unoriginate...*

*Troparion:*

Him Who willingly endured the Passion, and by His own desire was nailed unto the Cross, and shattered the power of Hades: praise Him, ye priests; supremely exalt Him, ye people, unto the ages!

*Troparion:*

Him Who abolished the dominion of death, and rose with glory from the tomb, and saved the human race: praise Him, ye priests; supremely exalt Him, ye people, unto the ages!

*Troparion:*

The only Tender-hearted, the pre-eternal Word, Who in the last times was born of a Virgin, and loosed the ancient curse: praise Him, ye priests; supremely exalt Him, ye people, unto the ages!

*Another Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos: Communing with the fire without burning...*

*Troparion:*

With the light of thy childbirth, in marvelous splendor thou didst enlighten the universe, O Birth-giver of God; thou bearest in thine arms the existing God, Who sanctifies the faithful who ever cry: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto the ages!

*Troparion:*

Piously thy womb hymns thee, O pure one, which held God ineffably incarnate, Who grants unto all the faithful the illumination of the knowledge of God, unto those who ever cry aloud: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto the ages!

[f. 119A] By the flashings of thy light, make those who hymn thee light-bearing, O Mother Virgin who gavest birth to the Light; for thou wast shown to be the dwelling of Light, shining with light upon those who cry: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto the ages!

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Having given birth without experience of corruption, and having lent flesh unto the all-craftsman Word, O Mother who knewest not man, Virgin Mother of God, vessel of the Uncontainable, dwelling-place of thine uncircumscribed Creator: thee do we magnify!

*Troparion:*

Let all the strange-minded who ascribe suffering unto the Godhead be put to shame; for the Lord of glory was crucified in the flesh, yet was not crucified in His divine nature, as in two natures we magnify the One!

*Troparion:*

Let those who do not believe in the resurrection of bodies go unto the tomb of Christ, unto the Teacher: for He was put to death, and rose again, the flesh of the Life-giver, in assurance of the final resurrection, the hope whereof we hold!

*Trinity Hymn:*

Honoring the Trinity not in Godhead, but in Persons; nor the Unity in Persons, but in Godhead: we cut off those who divide It, and we refute again those who dare [^138] [f. 120A] confusion concerning It: Whom we magnify!

*Another Canon. Heirmos: A Mother unto God and a Virgin, giving birth...*

*Troparion:*

Beholding in Christ the fleshly sufferings and the might of the Godhead, let them be ashamed who imagine one single nature; for as man indeed He dies, and as Creator of all He arises!

*Troparion:*

Myrrh unto the dead, but praise unto the Living; tears unto the dying, but a hymn unto the Life of all did ye women bring; and the herald of the Rising cried aloud, announcing the good tidings of Christ's Resurrection!

*Troparion:*

Light from Light, the Father's radiance of glory, shone forth ineffably; as in darkness unto human life Christ dawned, chasing away the gloom: Whom unceasingly we faithful magnify!

*Theotokion:*

I know no other God beside Thee, the Church cries unto Thee, Who hast chosen Thy Bride from the unbelieving nations; grant therefore salvation unto the faithful, O Word, through the prayers of her who bore Thee, as Thou art tender-hearted!

*Another Canon to the Mother of God. Same Heirmos.*

*Troparion:*

As the Mediatress of eternal joy and gladness hast thou ever appeared unto us, O Virgin Maiden, having borne the Deliverer, unto those who honor Him in truth and the divine Spirit, as God Who delivers!

[f. 120A] *Troparion:*

Singing, David thy forefather, O all-pure one, calls thee the holy divine Ark, which contained God beyond nature: Who sits in the Father's bosom; Whom unceasingly we faithful magnify!

*Troparion:*

Truly thou art higher than all creation, O Maiden, for bodily thou didst bear the Creator of all; therefore as the Mother of the only Master, thou bearest sovereign victory against all!

*At the Praises, Resurrection Stichera, Tone 7:*

*Sticheron:*

Christ is risen from the dead, having broken the bonds of death; announce, O earth, great joy: hymn the glory of God, ye heavens!

*Sticheron:*

Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ, let us worship the holy Lord Jesus, the only sinless One!

*Sticheron:*

We cease not worshipping Christ's Resurrection; for He hath saved us from our iniquities: holy is the Lord Jesus, Who revealed the Resurrection unto the world!

*Sticheron:*

What shall we render unto the Lord for all that He hath rendered unto us? For our sake God is among men; for corrupted nature the Word became flesh and dwelt among us; unto the ungrateful the Benefactor, unto the captives the Liberator, unto those sitting in darkness the Sun of righteousness, upon the Cross the Immortal [^139] [f. 121A] One, in Hades the Light, in death the Life, the Resurrection for the sake of the fallen: unto Whom let us cry aloud: O our God, glory to Thee!

*Other Stichera, Eastern:*

*Sticheron:*

The gates of Hades Thou didst shatter, O Lord, and abolish the dominion of death by Thy mighty power; and Thou didst raise up the dead who slept from the ages in darkness, by Thy divine and glorious Resurrection: O our God, glory to Thee!

*Sticheron:*

Come, let us rejoice in the Lord, and let us be glad in His Resurrection; for He raised the dead together with Himself from the unbreakable bonds of Hades, and as God granted unto the world eternal life and great mercy!

*Verse: Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor unto the end.*

*Sticheron:*

Shining brightly, the Angel sat upon the stone of Thy life-receiving tomb, and announced good tidings unto the myrrh-bearing women, saying: The Lord is risen, as He told you before; announce unto His disciples that He goes before you into Galilee, and grants unto the world eternal life and great mercy!

*Verse: I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart; I will speak of all Thy marvelous works.*

*Sticheron:*

Why have ye set at nought the Chief Cornerstone, O law-transgressing Jews? Behold, this is [f. 121A] He Whom God placed in Zion: Who poured forth water from the rock in the wilderness, and pours forth immortality unto us from His side; this is the Stone that was cut without male desire from the virginal mountain: the Son of Man coming upon the heavenly clouds unto the Ancient of Days, as Daniel said, and His Kingdom is eternal!

## SUNDAY LITURGY: THE BEATITUDES (TONE 7)

*At the Liturgy, The Beatitudes, Tone 7:*

*Troparion:*

Fair was the fruit and good for food that slew me; Christ is the Tree of Life, eating of which I die not, but cry with the Thief: Remember me, O Lord, in Thy Kingdom!

*Troparion:*

Thou wast lifted up upon the Cross, O Compassionate One, and didst blot out the handwriting of Adam's ancient sin, and saved all the human race from deception; therefore we hymn Thee, O Benefactor Lord!

*Troparion:*

Laid in the tomb as dead, O Christ, Life of all: Thou didst shatter the bars of Hades, and rising in glory on the third day as mighty, Thou didst enlighten all: glory to Thy rising!

*Troparion:*

By Thy rising Thou didst raise up Adam with Thyself [^140] [f. 122A] O Christ Giver of life, granting the ancient incorruption that was in Paradise; therefore I cry with the Thief: Remember me, O Lord, when Thou comest into Thy Kingdom!

*Troparion:*

Risen on the third day from the tomb, the Savior granted life, as He said, peace unto His disciples; and having blessed them, He sent them forth, saying: Bring all into My Kingdom!

*Troparion:*

The evil serpent once poured venom into the ears of Eve; but Christ, on the sharp wood of the Cross, poured forth unto the world the abundance of sweetness; therefore we cry: Remember me who cry out in Thy Kingdom!

*Glory. Trinity Hymn:*

The Father is Light, the Son and Word is Light, the Holy Spirit is Light; yet the three are one Light; for God is one in three Persons indeed, yet in one Nature and Principle, indivisible and unconfused, abiding pre-eternal!

*Both now. Theotokion:*

Thou didst bear the Son and the Father's Word in the flesh for our sake, as He Himself knows, O Mother of God; therefore, O Virgin Mother, having been made godlike through thee, we cry: Rejoice! unto thee, the boast of Christians!

---

# TONE 8

## SATURDAY EVENING: VESPERS (TONE 8)

[f. 122A] *On Saturday Evening, at Great Vespers, on "Lord, I have cried," Resurrection Stichera, Tone 8:*

*Sticheron:*

An evening hymn and rational service do we offer unto Thee, O Christ: for Thou wast well pleased to have mercy upon us by the Resurrection!

*Sticheron:*

O Lord, Lord, cast us not away from Thy presence: but be well pleased to have mercy upon us by the Resurrection!

*Sticheron:*

Rejoice, O holy Zion, Mother of the Churches, dwelling-place of God: for thou wast the first to receive remission of sins by the Resurrection!

*Other Stichera, Eastern, Same Tone:*

*Sticheron:*

The Word born of God the Father before the ages, and in the last times the Same incarnate of her who knew not wedlock, of His own will enduring the death of the Cross, and saved man who of old was slain, by His Resurrection!

[f. 123A] *Sticheron:* [^141]

Thy Resurrection from the dead do we glorify, O Christ, whereby Thou didst free the race of Adam from the tyranny of Hades, and as God didst grant unto the world eternal life and great mercy!

*Sticheron:*

Glory to Thee, O Christ Savior, only-begotten Son of God, Who wast nailed to the Cross and didst rise from the tomb on the third day!

*Sticheron:*

Thee do we glorify, O Lord, Who for our sake didst willingly endure the Crucifixion; and Thee do we worship, O almighty Savior: cast us not away from Thy presence, but give ear and save us, for the sake of Thy Resurrection, O Lover of mankind!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion [Dogmatikon]:*

The King of heaven, through love for mankind, appeared upon earth and lived among men; for taking flesh from the pure Virgin and coming forth from her with the assumption [of our nature], He is one Son, twofold in nature, but not in Person; therefore, truly proclaiming Him as perfect God and perfect man, we confess Christ our God: Whom implore, O unwedded Mother, that our souls may have mercy!

[f. 123A] *At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 8:*

*Sticheron:*

Thou didst ascend the Cross, O Jesus, Who camest down from heaven; Thou camest unto death, O immortal Life; unto those in darkness the true Light, unto the fallen the Resurrection of all and Enlightenment: O our Savior, glory to Thee!

*Other Stichera, Alphabetical:*

*Verse: The Lord is King, He is robed in majesty.*

*Sticheron:*

Let us glorify Christ, Who rose from the dead: for having assumed soul and body, by His Passion He severed them one from the other; His all-pure soul indeed descending into Hades, which He also despoiled, while in the tomb His holy body saw not corruption: the Deliverer of our souls!

*Verse: For He hath established the world, so that it cannot be moved.*

*Sticheron:*

With psalms and hymns do we glorify Thy Resurrection from the dead, O Christ, whereby Thou didst free us from the tyranny of Hades, and as God didst grant eternal life and great mercy!

*Verse: Holiness becomes Thine house, O Lord, unto length of days.*

*Sticheron:*

O incomprehensible Master of all, Creator of heaven and earth, by the sufferings of the Cross Thou didst pour forth passionlessness unto me; and having accepted burial, and risen in glory, Thou didst raise up Adam with Thyself by Thine [^142] [f. 124A] all-powerful hand! Glory to Thy three-day rising, whereby Thou hast granted unto us eternal life and cleansing of sins, as Thou alone art tender-hearted!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*

O unwedded Virgin, who didst ineffably conceive God in the flesh, Mother of God the Most High: receive the entreaties of thy servants, O all-immaculate one, who bestowest upon all cleansing of transgressions, now receiving our supplications: pray that we all may be saved!

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart. Trisagion. And after Our Father.*

*Resurrectional Troparion, Tone 8:*

Thou didst descend from on high, O Compassionate One; Thou didst accept three-day burial, that Thou mightest free us from passions: O our Life and Resurrection, glory to Thee!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*

O Good One, Who for our sake wast born of a Virgin and didst endure the Crucifixion, Who by death didst despoil death, and as God didst manifest the Resurrection: despise not those whom Thou didst create with Thine own hand; show forth Thy love for mankind, O Merciful One; accept the Mother of God who bore Thee, who intercedes for us; and save, O our Savior, a despairing people!

## SUNDAY MIDNIGHT OFFICE (TONE 8)

[f. 124A] *On Sunday, at the Midnight Office, Canon to the Holy and Life-Originating Trinity, of which the acrostic is: "O Unity, [preserve] the powers of Thy servant." Work of Metrophanes.*

*Ode 1, Tone 8. Heirmos: He submerged the armed Pharaoh, working wonders...*

*Troparion:*

Unto the thrice-sunlike King, and Builder, and Provider in every way, being good alone in nature, and possessing the unique glory of the Godhead, unto God of single principle we fall down, singing the thrice-holy hymn!

*Troparion:*

Divine utterances and prophecies from on high, fulfilling clearly, glorify the divine-originating single Nature, ever-existing, co-unoriginate, in three Persons: Father, and Son, and Spirit: all-powerful, creative!

*Troparion:*

Abraham, having become an initiate of sacred mysteries, sacredly of old received the Creator of all, and God, and Lord, in three figures indeed, rejoicing; and in three Hypostases he recognized the single Dominion!

*Theotokion:*

O thou who knewest not wedlock, thou didst bear Christ, Who for our sake assumed our [^143] [f. 125A] nature, O all-pure one, and remained inalterable in both [natures]: Whom implore unceasingly to grant me remission of sins and deliverance from trials!

*Ode 3. Heirmos: In the beginning, having established the heavens with understanding...*

*Troparion:*

Isaiah, beholding Thee, the unapproachable God and King of glory, upon a high throne, and the Cherubim and Seraphim glorifying Thee with unceasing hymns: [glorified Thee as] the single tri-hypostatic God!

*Troparion:*

Having attained by the wind of understanding and the teachings of the Scriptures the one Word born of the Father as from the Mind, and the Spirit proceeding ineffably: we venerate the one thrice-radiant God!

*Troparion:*

The unbegotten Father, having incorruptibly begotten the Son, the radiance of His essence, Light from Light, appropriately brings forth the co-sprouted Light of the Spirit: all-creating and equally honored!

*Theotokion:*

A pure temple wast thou shown to be, O Virgin Mother Mary, unto Christ Who all-powerfully and all-wisely fashioned all things, and set them in order and upholds them: Whom make propitious unto me by thy maternal prayers!

[f. 125A] *Sessional Hymn, Tone 8.*

*Podoben: "That which was commanded mystically" (Повеленное таинственно (Повеленное)):*

Ye faithful, let us now hymn the power of the thrice-sunlike and precious Godhead, how by a nod alone He framed all things, the supreme angelic choirs, and the lower ecclesiastical hierarchies, to cry: Holy, Holy, Holy art Thou, O all-good God; glory and song be unto Thy dominion!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*

O thou who didst bear the inalterable God: my heart, ever growing slothful through sin and indolence by the suggestions of the deceitful one, as good strengthen by thy maternal prayers, that I also may gratefully glorify thee, O Mary Birthgiver of God: have mercy on thy flock which thou hast acquired, O all-immaculate one!

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Thou art my Lord, O Christ, Thou art my strength, Thou art my God; having appeared as the dawn of the Godhead, He dispersed all the gloomy darkness of passions, and the Sun of righteousness shone forth: simple in essence, yet thrice-radiant in Persons: Whom we ever hymn and glorify!

*Troparion:*

Hymned by the mouths of the Seraphim, with earthen lips do we glorify the single and Trinity Lord of glory, crying out in nature and hypostasis: O holy [^144] [f. 126A] King, grant unto Thy servants remission of their diverse transgressions!

*Troparion:*

All-holding unto all existing things, invisible, all-bountiful, tender-hearted, loving mankind, O holy and divine-originating Trinity: forget not me Thy servant unto the end, nor make void the covenant which Thou hast covenanted with Thy servants, for the sake of Thine ineffable mercy!

*Theotokion:*

Finding thee alone, O all-pure one, fair from all ages, the beauty of Jacob, the pre-unoriginate Word dwelt in thee for the sake of tender mercy, and renewed human nature: Whom implore unceasingly to deliver me from every affliction!

*Ode 5. Heirmos: Why hast Thou cast me off from Thy...*

*Troparion:*

We glorify the three co-ever-existing Persons, and one Lord, Thee the divine Nature, simply distinguishing and uniting, and faithfully we cry aloud: O holy Trinity of single origin, deliver Thy servants from afflictions!

*Troparion:*

I weep bitterly for the weakness of my mind: how unwillingly I suffer; an involuntary change in truth! Therefore I cry: O holy, life-originating Trinity, establish me in the state of good things!

*Troparion:*

Weighed down by the slumber of my sin, and having need [f. 126A] unto the sleep of death: as Thou art loving mankind, and all-good, and all-merciful, O holy, divine-originating Trinity, show tender mercy and raise me up!

*Theotokion:*

O Virgin Maiden, all-pure, all-immaculate Mother, full of divine grace: by thy prayers make Thy Son, God, and Lord propitious unto me, and quickly deliver thy servant from passions and transgressions!

*Ode 6. Heirmos: Cleanse me, O Savior, for many are mine...*

*Troparion:*

Imitating the hierarchies of the heavenly minds, O Trinity transcendent in essence, of single origin over all: with thrice-holy hymns do we glorify Thee with our earthen lips!

*Troparion:*

Unto Him Who created man according to His own image, and all-wisely fashioned all things from non-existence, unto God in three Persons do I bow down, and I honor, sing, and magnify Him!

*Troparion:*

O Almighty God, and only Uncircumscribed One: dwell in me for the sake of Thine ineffable mercy, O thrice-sunlike Master; and enlighten me and grant me understanding, as Thou art tender-hearted!

*Theotokion:*

Thou wast shown to be a temple unto the uncontainable God, O all-pure one: show me also to be a temple unto Him [^145] [f. 127A] of divine graces, O all-holy Mother of God, by thy supplications, and preserve me unharmed!

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 8.*

*Podoben: "That which was commanded mystically" (Повеленное таинственно (Повеленное)):*

Ye faithful, let us truly hymn with our mouths the unoriginate Father, the co-unoriginate Son, and the divine Spirit, unconfusedly, inalterably, and immutably: the simple, holy, and co-sprouted Trinity, crying aloud with the Angels: Holy art Thou, O Father and Son, with the Spirit, simple and precious: have mercy on those whom Thou hast created according to Thine image, O Master!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*

We ever bless thee, O Mother of God, and we magnify thee, and we worship thee, hymning thy birthgiving, O full of grace, crying out unceasingly: Save us, O all-merciful Virgin, as thou art good, in the hour of examination, that we thy servants may not be put to shame!

*Ode 7. Heirmos: The fire was ashamed before the condescension of...*

*Troparion:*

By Thine ineffable wisdom and the abyss of goodness, show Thy servant to be freely shown mercy; and now, as of old, deliver me from wickedness, O God, Trinity in Unity, from sins and passions!

*Troparion:*

The Mind is the unbegotten Father, and the Word begotten of Him [f. 127A] and the divine Spirit, proceeding ineffably: O God of single origin, thrice-sunlike, I sing unto Thee: Blessed art Thou, the God of our fathers!

*Theotokion:*

I have been slain, O all-pure one, made to drink of the poison of sin; and with faith I flee unto thee who didst bear the Author of life: by thy prayers give life unto thy servant, and deliver me from temptations and passions, O thou who alone art pure!

*Ode 8. Heirmos: Sevenfold the furnace the Chaldean tyrant fiercely kindled...*

*Troparion:*

Being unwaning Light, thrice-radiant and thrice-sunlike, and of single origin, sovereign, most simple, incomprehensible God, and sovereign Lord: now enlighten my dark and darkened heart, and show it to be radiant and light-bearing, to sing and glorify Thee faithfully unto all ages!

*Troparion:*

With most fiery, most divine wings the Seraphim reverently veil their faces and feet, unable to endure the glory of Thine incomprehensible goodness: O divine-originating, divine-originating, self-originating, all-holy Trinity; yet we also dare to hymn Thee and glorify Thee unto all ages!

[f. 128A] *Troparion:* [^146]

O Lordship without beginning, all-powerful and all-good, perfectly unoriginate, beneficent, and supreme; the Cause having no cause, creative, pre-existing, provident, and saving unto all, Unity in essence and Trinity in Persons: I glorify Thee, my God, faithfully unto ages!

*Theotokion:*

Upon earth shone forth the unwaning Sun through thy virginal birthgiving, O all-pure Lady, and delivered earthly ones from idolatrous and deceitful dark gloom; therefore now also enlighten me with rays of that divine origin, and preserve thy servant!

*Ode 9. Heirmos: The heaven was amazed at this, and the...*

*Troparion:*

The Nature reigning over all and all-creating, pre-originate, timeless, life-originating, tender-hearted, loving mankind, good, Trinity of single origin: Thee do we now glorify, asking remission of sins, peace for the world, and concord for the Churches!

*Troparion:*

One Lordship and thrice-radiant, unique divine origin, thrice-sunlike: accept Thy singers fittingly, and deliver from transgressions and [f. 128A] afflictions, and hardships; and quickly grant peace, in Thy love for mankind, and unity unto the Churches!

*Theotokion:*

Having entered into the virginal womb, O Christ my Savior, Thou wast manifested unto Thy world, as God-man, inalterable, unconfused in truth; and Thou didst promise to be manifestly ever with Thy servants: therefore, by the prayers of her who bore Thee, establish peace unto all Thy flock!

## SUNDAY MATINS (TONE 8)

*On Sunday at Matins.*

*"God is the Lord..." Troparion, Tone 8: "Thou didst descend from on high, O Compassionate One..." Glory... Both now... Theotokion.*

*After the first Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 8:*

*Sessional Hymn:*

Thou didst rise from the dead, O Life of all; and a radiant Angel cried unto the women: Cease from tears, announce good tidings unto the Apostles, cry aloud singing: That Christ the Lord is risen, Who was well pleased as God to save the human race!

*Glory... Podoben: "The disciple of wisdom" (Премудрости):*

Having truly risen from the tomb, Thou didst command the venerable women to proclaim the rising unto the Apostles, as it is written; and running swiftly [^147] [f. 129A] Peter stood before the tomb, and seeing light in the tomb, he was astonished; therefore also seeing the linen cloths lying alone, which none could see lying in the night, having believed, he cried aloud: Glory to Thee, O Christ God, for Thou savest all, O our Savior, for Thou art the radiance of the Father! Both now...

*Theotokion:*

Let us hymn the heavenly Gate and Tabernacle, the all-holy Mountain, the radiant Cloud, the heavenly Ladder, the rational Paradise, the deliverance of Eve, the great treasure of the whole world: for salvation was wrought in it for the world, and remission of ancient transgressions; therefore we cry unto thee: Pray to thy Son and God to grant remission of transgressions unto those who piously worship thine all-holy birthgiving!

*After the second Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 8:*

*Sessional Hymn:*

Men sealed Thy tomb, O Savior, and an Angel rolled away the stone from the doors; and the women, beholding Thee risen from the dead, announced good tidings unto Thy disciples in Zion: That Thou art risen, O Life of all, and the bonds of death are loosed: O Lord, glory to Thee!

*Glory...*

Having brought [f. 129A] burial myrrh, the women heard an angelic voice from the tomb: Cease from tears, and in place of sorrow bring joy; cry aloud singing: That the Lord is risen, Who was well pleased as God to save the human race! Both now...

*Theotokion:*

All creation rejoices in thee, O full of grace: the angelic assembly and the race of man; hallowed temple and rational paradise, virginal boast, of whom God became incarnate and became a Child, Who is our pre-eternal God; for He made thy womb a throne, and made thy body more spacious than the heavens; all creation rejoices in thee, O full of grace: glory to thee!

*Hypakoe, Tone 8:*

The Myrrh-bearers, standing before the life-giving tomb, sought the immortal Master among the dead; and having received the joy of good tidings from the Angel, they announced unto the Apostles: That Christ God is risen, granting great mercy unto the world!

*Hymns of Ascent, Tone 8. Antiphon 1:*

From my youth the enemy tempts me, and with pleasures [^148] [f. 130A] burns me; but hoping in Thee, O Lord, I vanquish the enemy!

Let them that hate Zion become as grass before it is plucked up; for Christ shall cut off their necks with the cutting of torments!

*Glory... Both now...*

By the Holy Spirit all things have life: Light from Light, great God; we sing unto Him with the Father and with the Word!

*Antiphon 2:*

I cried unto Thee, O Lord: give ear unto me, incline Thine ear unto me who cry, and purify me before Thou takest me hence!

He that hath hope in the Lord shall not be terrified then, when He shall judge all things with fire and torment!

*Glory... Both now...*

By the Holy Spirit is theology, the Trinity in Unity; for the Father is unoriginate, from Whom the Son sprouted timelessly, and the Spirit, co-enthroned, co-formed, shone forth together from the Father!

*Antiphon 3:*

Let my heart be covered with Thy fear, thinking humbly, lest being lifted up it fall away from Thee, O All-compassionate One!

Whosoever departs [f. 130A] unto his mother earth is straightway dissolved, to receive either torments or honors for what he has lived!

*Glory... Both now...*

By the Holy Spirit, whosoever is godlike beholds and foretells; he works high wonders, singing of one God in three Persons; for though it be thrice-radiant, the Godhead reigns as one Principle!

*Antiphon 4:*

Behold now, what is so good, or what is so fair, as for brethren to dwell together in unity? For concerning this the Lord promised eternal life!

Concerning his clothing, He Who adorns the lilies of the field commands that we ought not to take thought!

*Glory... Both now...*

By the Holy Spirit, through a single Cause, all things are held together in peace-giving manner; for He is God, truly consubstantial with the Father and the Son!

*Prokeimenon, Tone 8:*

The Lord shall reign unto eternity; thy God, O Zion, unto generation and generation!

*Verse: Praise the Lord, O my soul!*

*Let every breath... Praise God in His saints... The Resurrectional Gospel. Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ... Psalm 50, and the rest.*

[f. 131A] *Canon of the Resurrection.* [^149]

*Ode 1, Tone 8. Heirmos: He submerged the armed Pharaoh, the rod of Moses working wonders of old, striking in the form of a cross and dividing the sea; but it saved Israel fleeing on foot as a traveler, singing a hymn unto God!*

*Troparion:*

At the all-powerful Godhead of Christ how shall we not marvel? From passions pouring forth passionlessness and incorruption unto all the faithful, and from His holy side trickling an immortal fountain, and life from the tomb unto ever-existing ones!

*Troparion:*

How comely appeared the Angel now unto the women, bearing bright natural images of immaterial purity, and by his appearance proclaiming the light of the Resurrection, crying aloud: The Lord is risen!

*Theotokion:*

Most glorious things were spoken concerning thee in generation of generations, who didst contain the Word of God in thy womb, yet remained pure, O Mary Mother of God; therefore do we all honor thee, our protectress after God!

*Another Canon, of the Cross and Resurrection.*

*Heirmos: Having traversed the water as dry land...*

The gates [f. 131A] of sorrow were lifted up, and the gatekeepers of Hades were terrified, beholding in the nether regions Him Who in the highest is above all nature!

*Troparion:*

The angelic ranks marvelled, beholding fallen human nature, which had been shut up in the nether parts of the earth, seated upon the divine throne!

*Theotokion:*

The angelic and human ranks unceasingly praise thee, O unwedded Mother; for thou didst carry in thine arms as an infant the Creator of these!

*Another Canon, to the Mother of God. Heirmos: Let us sing unto the Lord...*

*Troparion:*

O all-pure Mother of God, who didst bear beyond nature the incarnate ever-existing and transcendent Word: we hymn thee!

*Troparion:*

A life-bearing cluster of grapes did the Virgin bear, O Christ: dripping the sweetness of salvation upon all the world!

*Troparion:*

The race of Adam, having been led up through thee unto beatitude that is beyond understanding, O Mother of God, worthily glorifies thee!

*Ode 3. Heirmos: In the beginning, having established the heavens with understanding...*

[f. 132A] ...and founded the earth upon the waters: establish me, O Christ, upon the rock of Thy Church; for there is none holy save Thee, O Lover of mankind! [^150]

*Troparion:*

Adam having been condemned through the tasting of sin, by the saving suffering of Thy flesh Thou didst justify, O Christ; for Thou Thyself, being sinless, wast not liable to the trial of death!

*Troparion:*

The light of the Resurrection shone forth unto those sitting in the darkness and shadow of death, even Jesus my God; and having bound the strong one by His Godhead, He despoiled his vessels!

*Theotokion:*

Thou wast shown to be higher than the Cherubim and Seraphim, O Mother of God; for thou alone didst receive the uncontainable God in thy womb, O undefiled one; therefore all we faithful bless thee with hymns, O pure one!

*Another Heirmos: O Lord, Maker of the celestial vault...*

*Troparion:*

Him who at first disobeyed the commandment of the Lord Who truly made me, Thou didst banish, in which he was formed; and having taught obedience by Thyself, Thou didst renew him by the Crucifixion!

*Troparion:*

O Lord, Who by wisdom didst foreknow all things, and by Thy understanding didst establish the nether regions: by Thy descent, O Word of God, Thou didst deign to raise up him who was made according to Thine image!

*Theotokion:*

Having dwelt [f. 132A] bodily in the Virgin, O Lord, Thou wast manifested unto man as it was fitting to see Thee; and her Thou didst show forth as true Queen and helper unto the faithful, O Thou Who alone lovest mankind!

*Another Heirmos: Thou art the establishment of those that flee unto Thee, O Lord...*

*Troparion:*

Grant us help by thy prayers, O all-pure one, repelling the assaults of grievous afflictions; thou wast the setting-aright of our foremother Eve, having borne for the world the Author of life, O Mother of God!

*Troparion:*

Gird me with power, O all-pure one, who in truth didst bear God in the flesh: the hypostatic Power of the Father!

*Ode 4. Heirmos: Thou art my strength, O Lord, Thou art my power; Thou art my God, Thou art my rejoicing; Who, without leaving the bosom of the Father, hast visited our poverty; therefore with the Prophet Habakkuk I cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lover of mankind!*

*Troparion:*

When I was at enmity, Thou didst love me exceedingly; by a strange emptying Thou didst descend upon earth, O tender-hearted Savior, not rejecting the reproach of my lowliness; and abiding on the height of Thine all-pure glory, Thou didst glorify him who before was dishonored!

[f. 133A] Who, beholding now, O Master, is not astonished? Sufferings having destroyed death, by the Cross corruption is put to flight, and by death the riches of Hades are despoiled by the divine power of Thee Who wast crucified: a terrifying work, O Lover of mankind! [^151]

*Theotokion:*

Thou art the boast of the faithful, O unwedded one; thou art the protectress, thou art the refuge of Christians, a rampart and a harbor; for unto thy Son thou bearest supplications, O all-immaculate one, and dost save from perils those who with faith and love know thee as the pure Mother of God!

*Another Heirmos: I have heard, O Lord, the mystery of Thy dispensation...*

*Troparion:*

The children of the lawless nailed Thee to the Cross, O Christ God, whereby Thou didst save, as Thou art compassionate, those who glorify Thy sufferings!

*Troparion:*

Having risen from the tomb, Thou didst raise up with Thyself all the dead in Hades, and didst enlighten, as Thou art tender-hearted, those who glorify Thy Resurrection!

*Theotokion:*

God Whom thou didst bear, O all-pure Mary: Him implore to grant remission of transgressions unto thy servants!

*Another Heirmos, the same:*

*Troparion:*

As the unplowed furrow that sprouted the life-creating Ear of wheat, Who grants life unto the world, O Mother of God, save those who hymn thee!

[f. 133A] *Troparion:*

Enlightened, we all know thee to be the bush, O all-pure one, for thou didst bear the Sun of righteousness, O Ever-Virgin!

*Troparion:*

Grant cleansing unto our negligences, as Thou art sinless, and give peace unto Thy world, O God, by the prayers of her who bore Thee!

*Ode 5. Heirmos: Why hast Thou cast me off from Thy presence, O unwaning Light, and a strange darkness hath covered me, the wretched one? But turn me back, and unto the light of Thy commandments direct my ways, I pray Thee!*

*Troparion:*

Mocked, Thou didst endure to be clothed in a purple robe before Thy Passion, O Savior, healing the shameless nakedness of the first-created man; and naked to be nailed to the Cross, stripping off the garment of slaying, O Christ!

*Troparion:*

From the dust of death, rising, Thou didst rebuild my fallen nature; and Thou didst make it unaging, O Christ, showing it forth again, like a royal image, gleaming with the light of incorruption!

*Theotokion:*

Having maternal boldness toward thy Son, O all-pure one, the kindred care for us [^152] [f. 134A] despise not, we pray, for thee alone do we Christians mercifully offer as cleansing unto the Mother of God!

*Another Heirmos: Enlighten us by Thy commandments, O Lord...*

*Troparion:*

Guide us by the power of Thy Cross, O Christ: for by it we fall down before Thee; grant us Thy peace, O Lover of mankind!

*Troparion:*

Govern our life now, O all-good Christ our God, who hymn Thy rising, and grant us peace, O Lover of mankind!

*Theotokion:*

Implore thy Son and our God, O pure one, unwedded Mary all-pure, to send down great mercy upon us, the faithful!

*Another Heirmos: Rising at dawn we cry to Thee, O Lord...*

*Troparion:*

Calm the unstable tempest of my passions, O Virgin, who didst bear God, the Helmsman and Lord!

*Troparion:*

Unto thy birthgiving minister the angelic ranks and human assemblies, O all-pure Mother of God!

*Troparion:*

O Mary, unwedded Mother of God: bring to light the hopes of the enemies, and gladden those who hymn thee!

*Ode 6. Heirmos: Cleanse me, O Savior, for many are mine...*

[f. 134A] ...and give ear unto me, O God of my salvation!

*Troparion:*

By the tree the author of evil mightily cast me down; but Thou, having been lifted up upon the Tree, didst mightily cast him down, having exposed him by the Cross, and didst raise up the fallen one!

*Troparion:*

Thou didst have compassion on Zion, having shone forth from the tomb; Thou didst make it new instead of old, as Thou art tender-hearted, by Thy divine Blood; and now Thou reignest in it unto the ages, O Christ!

*Theotokion:*

May we be delivered from grievous transgressions by thy prayers, O pure Mother of God; and may we obtain, O all-pure one, the divine radiance of the Son of God Who was ineffably incarnate of thee!

*Another Heirmos: I will pour out my prayer unto the Lord...*

*Troparion:*

Thou didst stretch out Thy palms upon the Cross, healing the incontinently outstretched hand of the first-created man in Eden; and of Thine own will having tasted gall, O Christ, Thou didst save as mighty those who glorify Thy sufferings!

*Troparion:*

The Deliverer tasted death of the initial decree, that He might destroy the kingdom of corruption; and having descended into the nether regions, Christ arose [^153] [f. 135A] and saved as mighty those who sing His Resurrection!

*Theotokion:*

Cease not to pray for us, O all-pure Mother of God and Virgin, for thou art the establishment of the faithful, and by hope in thee are we strengthened, and with love we ineffably glorify Him Who became incarnate of thee!

*Another Heirmos: Grant me a tunic of light...*

*Troparion:*

A temple of God, and an Ark, and an animate bridal chamber, and heavenly gates do the faithful proclaim thee to be, O Mother of God!

*Troparion:*

As God, the destroyer of heathen temples, Thy birthgiving, O Mary Bride of God, is worshipped with the Father and the Spirit!

*Troparion:*

The Word of God showed thee unto earthly ones as the heavenly Ladder, O Mother of God: for through thee He descended unto us!

*Kontakion, Tone 8:*

Having risen from the tomb, Thou didst raise up the dead, and didst resurrect Adam, and Eve dances in Thy Resurrection, and the ends of the world celebrate in Thy rising from the dead, O Many-merciful One!

*Ikos:*

Having despoiled the realms of Hades, and raised up the dead, O Long-suffering One, Thou didst meet the Myrrh-bearing women, bringing joy in place of sorrow; and unto Thine Apostles [f. 135A] Thou didst announce tokens of victory, O my Savior and Giver of life; and Thou dost enlighten creation, O Lover of mankind; therefore also the world together rejoices in Thy rising from the dead, O Many-merciful One!

*Ode 7. Heirmos: At the condescension of God, the fire was ashamed in Babylon of old; therefore the children in the furnace, with dancing foot, rejoiced as in a bridal chamber, singing: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!*

*Troparion:*

The glorious emptying, the divine wealth of Thy poverty, O Christ, astonishes the Angels, beholding Thee nailed to the Cross in the flesh, to save those who cry with faith: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Troparion:*

By Thy divine descent the nether regions were filled with light, and the darkness that formerly pursued was driven away; therefore the prisoners from the ages arose, crying aloud: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Trinity Hymn:*

The Lord of all indeed, the Father of the only-begotten Son alone, we orthodoxly proclaim, theologically declaring Thee; and we know Thy single upright Spirit, proceeding forth, consubstantial and co-ever-existing!

[f. 136A] *Another Heirmos: He Who from Judea of old...* [^154]

*Troparion:*

Thou hast wrought salvation in the midst of the earth, we prophetically cry aloud: for having been lifted up upon the Tree, Thou didst call all who cry with faith: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Troparion:*

Risen from the tomb as from sleep, O Compassionate One, Thou didst raise up the world with Thyself; creation was assured by the Apostles who proclaimed the Resurrection: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Trinity Hymn:*

We worship the Father, and honor the Son, and the Holy Spirit, acknowledging three consubstantial Persons, and calling upon the one God and Lord: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Another Heirmos, the same:*

*Theotokion:*

Incarnate of a virginal womb, Thou wast manifested for our salvation; therefore knowing Thy Mother to be the Mother of God, we orthodoxly cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Theotokion:*

Thou didst bud forth as a rod from the root of Jesse, O Virgin, bearing the all-blessed fruit, the saving Flower, crying with faith unto thy Son: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Theotokion:*

Fill all with [f. 136A] [Wisdom] of divine essence, which came to be for the sake of the Mother of God, unto those who sing unto Thee with faith: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Ode 8. Heirmos: The Chaldean tyrant furiously burned the furnace sevenfold for the godly; but having seen them saved by a better power, he cried unto the Creator and Deliverer: Ye children, bless; ye priests, hymn; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages!*

*Troparion:*

The divine, transcendent power of Jesus has divinely shone forth in us: for entering bodily into the tabernacle of death's strength for the sake of all, He destroyed the strength of Hades; Whom ye children unceasingly bless, ye priests hymn, ye people supremely exalt unto all ages!

*Troparion:*

The Crucified One is risen, the boastful one is fallen; he who had fallen and been crushed is set upright; corruption has been overthrown, and incorruption has blossomed forth; for that which was mortal has been swallowed up by life! Ye children, bless; ye priests, hymn; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

*Trinity Hymn:*

The thrice-radiant Godhead, shining forth a single ray from the one nature in three Persons: the unoriginate Father, and the co-sprouted Word of the...

(see fol. 137)[^155]

...Father, and the consubstantial Spirit reigning together: ye children, bless; ye priests, hymn; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

*Another Heirmos: Conquerors of the tyrant...*

*Troparion:*

Him Who stretched out His hands upon the Tree unto me who was naked, calling me to warm myself with His noble nakedness: bless, ye priests, hymn, and ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

*Troparion:*

Him Who raised me up, the fallen one, from the nethermost parts of Hades, and honored me with the enthroned glory of the Father on high: bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, hymn, and supremely exalt Him unto ages!

*Trinity Hymn:*

The Godhead portrayed in three Persons, undivided, in three Hypostases, in one essence and glory: bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, hymn, and supremely exalt Him unto ages!

*Another Heirmos: The Heavenly King...*

*Troparion:*

Quench the kindled and fiery arrows of the adversary that are against us, that we may hymn thee, honoring thee unto all ages!

*Theotokion:*

Beyond nature hast thou borne God the Word, the Creator and Savior, O Virgin: Whom we supremely exalt unto all ages!

*Theotokion:*

Thee, the illuminating and golden-beaming lamp, the inaccessible Light that dwelt [f. 136A] in thee, O Virgin, the inaccessible Light showed thee forth as a lamp unto all ages!
