*Ode 9. Heirmos: Heaven was astonished at this, and the ends of the earth were amazed, that God was manifested unto men in the flesh, and thy womb became more spacious than the heavens; therefore, O Mother of God, the hierarchies of Angels and men magnify thee!*

*Troparion:*

Being simple in nature, divine and unoriginate, Thou wast compounded by the assumption of the flesh, having hypostasized this within Thyself, O Word of God; and Thou didst suffer as man, yet remained free from passions as God: therefore in two natures, indivisibly and unconfusedly, do we magnify Thee!

*Troparion:*

[Glorifying] the Father according to His divine essence, as Thou didst become man in nature: being the transcendent God, Thou didst descend unto servants; and having risen from the tomb by the grace of the Father, Thou didst establish Him as God and Master according to nature unto the earthborn: with Him all of us magnify Thee!

*Theotokion:*

Thou wast seen, O Virgin, Mother of God, having borne beyond nature in the flesh the good Word, Whom the Father poured forth from His heart before...

(see fol. 138)[^156]

...all ages, as He is good; Whom now also we understand to be transcendent over bodies, even though He clothed Himself in a body!

*Another Heirmos: Every hearing was affrighted...*

*Troparion:*

Knowing the Son of God by nature, conceived in the womb of the Mother of God, and become man for our sake, and beholding Thee upon the Cross, suffering indeed in human nature, but remaining immortal as God!

*Troparion:*

The murky darkness was dispersed, for from Hades shone forth Christ, the Sun of righteousness, enlightening all the ends of the earth, beaming with the rays of the Godhead: heavenly Man, earthly God; Whom in two natures we magnify!

*Troparion:*

Direct, and save, and reign, O Son of the Mother of God, subduing the Ishmaelite peoples that war against us unto the Christ-loving king; for as an invincible weapon He granted the Cross with the lance unto those who approach Thee!

*Another Heirmos: Truly the Mother of God...*

*Troparion:*

Full of joy and gladness is thy memory, pouring forth healings unto those who draw near and piously proclaim thee to be the Mother of God!

*Troparion:*

With psalms do we hymn thee, O full of grace, and unceasingly we cry unto thee: Rejoice! For thou [f. 136A] hast poured forth rejoicing unto all!

*Theotokion:*

Comely did thy fruit bud forth, O Mother of God, mediating incorruption and life unto those who partake thereof, who magnify thee with faith!

*At the Praises, Resurrectional Stichera, Tone 8:*

*Sticheron 1:*

O Lord, though Thou didst stand at the judgment seat judged by Pilate, yet Thou didst not withdraw from the throne, seated with the Father; and having risen from the dead, Thou hast freed the world from the bondage of the stranger, as Thou art compassionate and the Lover of mankind!

*Sticheron 2:*

O Lord, Thou hast given us Thy Cross as a weapon against the devil: for he trembles and shakes, unable to bear looking upon its power; for it raises the dead and has abolished death: therefore do we worship Thy burial and Resurrection!

*Sticheron 3:*

O Lord, though the Jews laid Thee in the tomb as one dead, yet soldiers watched Thee as a sleeping King, and sealed Thee with a seal as the treasure of life; but Thou didst rise and hast granted incorruption unto our souls!

*Sticheron 4:*

Thine Angel, O Lord, who proclaimed the Resurrection...

(see fol. 139)[^157]

...terrified indeed the guards, and crying out unto the women, he said: Why seek ye the Living One among the dead? He is risen, being God, and has granted life unto the universe!

*Other Stichera, Anatolian, Tone 8:*

*Sticheron 5:*

Thou didst suffer by the Cross, being immortal in Godhead; Thou didst accept three-day burial, that Thou mightest free us from the bondage of the enemy, and having made us immortal, mightest quicken us, O Christ God, through Thy Resurrection, O Lover of mankind!

*Sticheron 6:*

I worship, and glorify, and hymn, O Christ, Thy Resurrection from the tomb, for the sake of which Thou hast freed us from the insoluble bonds of Hades, and granted unto the world, as God, eternal life and great mercy!

*Verse: Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor unto the end.*

*Sticheron 7:*

While the lawless were watching Thy life-receiving tomb, having sealed it with a custody then, Thou, as immortal God and almighty, didst rise on the third day!

*Verse: I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart; I will speak of all Thy marvelous works.*

*Sticheron 8:*

When Thou didst come to the gates of Hades, O Lord, and break them down, the captives cried thus: Who is this [f. 136A] that He is not condemned to the nether parts of the earth, but has destroyed the prison-house of death as a shadow? I received Him as one dead, and I tremble at Him as God: O Almighty One, have mercy on us!

*Glory... Evangelical Sticheron. Both now... Theotokion: "Most blessed art thou..." Great Doxology, and Dismissal.*

## SUNDAY LITURGY: THE BEATITUDES (TONE 8)

*At the Liturgy, The Beatitudes, Tone 8:*

*Troparion 1:*

Remember us, O Christ, Savior of the world, even as Thou didst remember the thief upon the Tree; and make all of us worthy of Thy heavenly kingdom, O only Compassionate One!

*Troparion 2:*

Hear, O Adam, and rejoice with Eve, for he who formerly stripped you both naked, and through deceit took you captive, has been abolished by the Cross of Christ!

*Troparion 3:*

Nailed to the Tree of Thine own will, O our Savior, Thou didst deliver Adam from the curse of the tree, restoring unto him, as Thou art compassionate, the dwelling in Paradise that is according to Thine image!

*Troparion 4:*

Today Christ is risen from the tomb, granting incorruption unto all the faithful, and renewing joy unto the Myrrh-bearing women after His Passion and Resurrection!

(see fol. 140)[^158]

Even as the multitude of Angels in heaven, so also upon earth the human race celebrates Thy holy Resurrection, [praising] Thy goodness, O Lord!

*Troparion 5:*

Raise us up from the tomb of sin, O Savior, who are slain by many passions; and by Thy rising destroy the tyranny of death, as Thou art the Lover of mankind! Glory...

*Trinity Hymn:*

Unoriginate Trinity, undivided Essence, co-enthroned Unity, equal in honor of glory, supreme Nature and Kingdom: save those who hymn Thee with faith! Both now...

*Theotokion:*

Rejoice from us, O holy Mother of God! Rejoice, thou who didst bear joy unto the world! Rejoice, only protectress of mankind, and salvation of our souls!

*There the Prokeimenon, according to the Tone.*

*Epistle to the Romans, Section 88:*

Brethren: Having been justified by faith, we have peace with God through our Lord Jesus Christ, through Whom also we have obtained access by faith into this grace wherein we stand, and boast in hope of the glory of God. And not only so, but we boast also in afflictions, knowing that affliction [f. 136A] produces endurance, and endurance approved character, and approved character hope; and hope puts not to shame, because the love of God has been poured out in our hearts through the Holy Spirit Who was given unto us. For while we were yet weak, in due time Christ died for the ungodly. For scarcely for a righteous man will one die; though perhaps for a good man someone might even dare to die. But God proves His love toward us, in that while we were yet sinners, Christ died for us. Much more then, having now been justified by His Blood, shall we be saved by Him from wrath. For if, being enemies, we were reconciled to God by the death of His Son, much more, having been reconciled, shall we be saved by His life.

*The Gospel according to Matthew, Section 18:*

The Lord said: The lamp of the body is the eye: if therefore thine eye be simple, thy whole body shall be full of light. But if thine eye be evil, thy whole body shall be full of darkness. If therefore the light that is in thee be darkness, how great...

(see fol. 141)[^159]

...is that darkness! No man can serve two masters: for either he will hate the one and love the other, or else he will hold to the one and despise the other. Ye cannot serve God and mammon. Therefore I say unto you: Take no thought for your life, what ye shall eat, or what ye shall drink; nor yet for your body, what ye shall put on. Is not the life more than food, and the body than raiment? Behold the birds of the heaven: for they sow not, neither do they reap, nor gather into barns; yet your heavenly Father feeds them. Are ye not much better than they? Which of you by taking thought can add one cubit unto his stature? And why take ye thought for raiment? Consider the lilies of the field, how they grow; they toil not, neither do they spin: and yet I say unto you, that even Solomon in all his glory was not arrayed like one of these. If then God so clothe the grass of the field, which today is, and tomorrow is cast into the oven, shall He not much more clothe you, O ye of little faith? Therefore take no thought, saying: What shall we eat? or, What shall we drink? or, Wherewithal shall we be clothed? For after all these things do the Gentiles seek; for your heavenly Father knows that ye have need of all these things. But seek ye first the kingdom of God, and His righteousness; and all these things shall be added unto you.

## SUNDAY EVENING VESPERS (TONE 8)

[f. 136A] *On Sunday Evening, at "Lord, I have cried," Penitential Stichera, Tone 8:*

*Tone 8.*

*Podoben: "O Lord, though Thou didst stand before the tribunal" (Господи, аще и на судищи):*

*Sticheron 1:*

O Lord, Who camest into the world to call sinners: the robbers, the publican, and the harlot, and Who didst receive the prodigal: call me also, who have sinned against Thee more than all, O my Christ, and who nowise repent, O Lover of mankind!

*Sticheron 2:*

O Lord, Who didst appoint a pastor for Thy Church from among publicans, and didst make him who before was a persecutor to be now an intercessor: by their prayers, show me also to be Thine own sheep, O Savior, that being unprofitable I may not become food for strangers!

*Sticheron 3:*

Weep, O wretched soul, before the end, and cleave unto God, crying unto Him from the heart with groanings, O all-defiled one: I have sinned against Thee, O Christ! Despise me not, I pray Thee, but choose good things, as Thou art merciful, and make me worthy of forgiveness!

*Other Stichera, to the Bodiless Powers, the same Tone:*

(see fol. 142)[^160]

*Sticheron 4:*

O Lord, the assemblies of the Bodiless Powers unceasingly glorify Thee, delighting in Thy beauty and ineffable loveliness, richly enlightened with thrice-sunlike rays: by their prayers and intercessions, O Savior, save our souls!

*Sticheron 5:*

O Lord, Thou hast established the manifold angelic choirs by Thy most powerful Word and by Thy Holy Spirit, having granted them unfailing radiance: by their supplications and intercessions, O Savior, save our souls!

*Sticheron 6:*

O Lord, the Seraphim hymn Thee, and the Authorities, and the Angels, Principalities, Dominions, Thrones, and Powers, and Archangels, and the fearsome Cherubim offer praise unto Thee: by their supplications and intercessions, O Savior, save our souls! Glory... Both now...

*Theotokion:*

Ever doing [evil], I anger and grieve the Creator; grant me correction, O Maiden, beforehand make Him propitious, and by thy help raise me up unto acceptable deeds, that I may obtain salvation and remission!

*O Gladsome Light. Prokeimenon: "Behold now, bless the Lord, all ye servants of the Lord..." Verse: "Ye that stand in the temple of the Lord..."*

*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, the same Tone:*

[f. 136A] *Sticheron 1:*

The Angels unceasingly hymn Thee as King and Master; and I fall down before Thee, crying like the publican: O God, cleanse me and save me!

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven.*

*Sticheron 2:*

Being immortal, O my soul, be not overwhelmed by the waves of life; but arise, crying unto thy Benefactor: O God, cleanse me and save me!

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are utterly filled with contempt.*

*Sticheron 3:*

O Martyrs of the Lord, ye sanctify every place and heal every sickness; and now pray ye that our souls may be delivered from the snares of the enemy, we beseech you! Glory... Both now...

*Theotokion:*

Having received the voice of the Archangel Gabriel, let us say: Rejoice, O Mother of God, who didst bear the Giver of life unto the world!

*Troparion to the Angels, Tone 8:*

Commanders of the heavenly hosts, we the unworthy now beseech you, that by your prayers you may protect us under the shelter of the wings of your immaterial glory, preserving us who fall down diligently and cry: Deliver us from perils, as commanders of the higher Powers! Glory... Both now...

*Theotokion:*

An invincible rampart art thou unto us Christians, O Virgin Mother of God...

(see fol. 143)[^161]

## MONDAY MATINS: REPENTANCE AND THE BODILESS POWERS (TONE 8)

*On Monday at Matins, after the first Kathisma, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 8:*

*Sessional Hymn 1:*

With a tender-hearted eye, O Lord, behold my lowliness, for shortly my life is ending, and from works there is no salvation for me; therefore do I pray: With a tender-hearted eye, O Lord, behold my lowliness, and save me! Glory...

*Sessional Hymn 2:*

Since the Judge is at hand, take thought, O my soul, and consider the hour of the fearful day; for judgment is without mercy unto those who have not shown mercy: therefore before the end cry aloud: Spare me, O Savior, for Thou alone art without sin! Both now...

*Tone 8.*

*Podoben: "That which was commanded mystically" (Повелѣ́нное та́инственно):*

*Theotokion:*

Having mystically received the command in his understanding, with haste the Bodiless One stood before the chamber of Joseph, saying unto the unwedded one: He Who bowed the heavens by His descent is contained inalterably whole in thee; and beholding Him in thy womb, having received the form of a servant, I am astonished to cry unto thee: Rejoice, O Bride unwedded!

*After the second Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 8:*

*Sessional Hymn 3:*

Considering that fearful day, O my soul, watch, kindling thy lamp and enlightening it with oil; for thou knowest not when the voice shall come unto thee, saying [f. 136A] Behold, the Bridegroom! See then, O my soul, that thou slumber not, and remain outside knocking, like the five virgins; but watchfully wait, that thou mayest meet Christ God with rich oil, and He shall grant thee the divine bridal chamber of His glory!

*Sessional Hymn 4:*

Like the harlot I fall down before Thee, that I may receive remission; and instead of myrrh, I offer tears from the heart unto Thee, that even as her Thou mayest show mercy unto me, O Savior, and grant me cleansing of sins: for like her do I cry unto Thee: Deliver me from the mire of my deeds!

*Martyricon:*

With heavenly light is this temple enlightened today, for in it the angelic hosts rejoice, and with them the choirs of the righteous make merry in the memory of the passion-bearers: by their supplications send down upon Thy world peace, O Christ, and great mercy! Glory... Both now...

*Theotokion:*

Rejoice, O Gate of the King of glory, through which the Most High alone passed, and was well-pleased that it should remain sealed, unto the salvation of our souls!

*Penitential Canon, Creation of Joseph. Ode 1, Tone 8. Heirmos: Thy victorious right hand is divinely glorified in power...*

*Troparion:*

Blot out the multitude of my sins according to the multitude of Thy compassions, O Christ, and grant...

(see fol. 144)[^162]

...me a thought of return, that I may glorify Thy goodness which is beyond understanding!

*Troparion:*

Through the tender mercy of Thy compassions Thou wast manifested, incarnate on earth as man: therefore, O Word of God, receive me now who have sinned more than man, falling down before Thy compassions in repentance!

*Martyricon:*

Imitating the mortality and death of Christ Who suffered of His own will, ye endured manifold sufferings and death, O ever-glorious ones: therefore have ye obtained immortal life, O Martyrs!

*Martyricon:*

Brightly bearing within yourselves the fire of divine love, O Martyrs, ye feared not the fire, but fervently endured pain, and burned up all the deceit of polytheism!

*Theotokion:*

The ranks of the Bodiless Powers with us hymn thee, O all-hymned Virgin; for the Uncircumscribed One, incarnate in thy womb, hast thou borne, remaining a Virgin, even as before birthgiving, O Bride of God!

*Another Canon, to the Bodiless Powers. Ode 1, Tone 8. Heirmos: Traversing the untrodden, unaccustomed, unwetted [path]...*

[f. 136A] *Troparion 1:*

As God-bearing coals kindled with the radiance of Thine essence, Thou hast shown forth the choirs of the Bodiless Powers, glorifying Thee, O Christ, as the Almighty!

*Troparion 2:*

Having acquired the power of incorruption and the granted glory of immortality, O Christ, the Angels are enlightened through drawing near unto Thee!

*Troparion 3:*

The Angels were shown forth as light-bearers, displaying immaterial purity according to their nature, O Christ, sensory-wise portraying it in bodily forms!

*Theotokion:*

The angelic ranks, rejoicing, minister unto thy birthgiving beyond nature, O pure Virgin; for thou didst bear their God and Lord!

*Ode 3. Heirmos: Thou Who alone knowest [the weakness of] human nature...*

*Troparion:*

Arise, come, O my soul, and cry aloud unto thy Creator, Who alone knows all thy secret things, and bring forth fruits of repentance, that the compassionate Lord may show tender mercy unto thee and deliver thee from eternal fire!

*Troparion:*

Cleanse, O only Good One, cleanse and save me! Like the publican with fear do I cry unto Thee, dragging with myself a multitude of sins; and I am bowed down under the weight of transgressions, filled with immeasurable shame!

(see fol. 145)[^163]

*Martyricon:*

Filled with true wisdom and understanding, the Martyrs turned Greek fury to foolishness, destroyed subtle malice, and having suffered valiantly, worthily received crowns of victory, rejoicing!

*Martyricon:*

Confessing a Unity in essence and a Trinity in Persons, O passion-bearers, by divine faith ye destroyed the delusion of polytheism, and appeared as luminaries, enlightening the hearts of all with the rays of grace!

*Theotokion:*

O holy Bride of God, pure one: thou didst bear the Holy One Who rests in the saints, the Son and Word co-unoriginate with the Father, Who sanctifies by the Holy Spirit those who piously worship Him!

*Another Heirmos: The bow of the mighty is broken...*

*Troparion:*

God, Who is immortal by nature, having all-wisely fashioned immortal hosts by His goodness, manifests them!

*Troparion:*

Now standing before Christ with utmost desire, O Angels, pray that all of us may be saved!

*Theotokion:*

The Creator of the ages truly received a beginning in time from thee, O Mother, Ever-Virgin!

*Ode 4. Heirmos: Habakkuk, beholding thee with prophetic eyes as a mountain overshadowed by divine grace...*

*Troparion:*

Unto whom shall I liken thee, O my wretched soul, working wicked deeds and not loving good things? Turn back [f. 136A] cry aloud unto Him Who of His own will became poor for thy sake: O Knower of hearts, cleanse me and save me!

*Troparion:*

Thou didst proclaim repentance, O Savior, unto those who return; grant this unto me, O Good One, before the end of life; give me compunction and sighing, even as formerly unto the harlot who kissed Thy feet, O Master!

*Martyricon:*

Filled with spiritual waters, O Martyrs, ye were shown to be rivers of living water by the divine nod; and by the Spirit ye dried up the muddy torrents of deceit, O Christ, and watered the hearts of the faithful!

*Martyricon:*

Mightily contending, O divine Martyrs, ye endured fire and sword and every cruel torment: by their supplication, O great Word of God, deliver from torments, even eternal ones, those who hymn Thee with faith!

*Theotokion:*

The Son Who was begotten of old of the unbegotten Father accepted birth in time, having become incarnate of thee, O Virgin: wishing, as He is tender-hearted, to take away the ancient warfare of earthly ones, He Who is transcendent over days and years!

*Another Heirmos: I have heard, O Lord, the report of Thy dispensation...*

*Troparion:*

I bring unto Thee the Bodiless Powers as intercessors: receiving them, O Compassionate One, deliver me from sins, as Thou art tender-hearted!

(see fol. 146)[^164]

*Troparion:*

Drawing near unto the Cause of all, the divine minds, being cleansed, are enlightened with supreme understandings!

*Troparion:*

The divine adornments of the heavenly hierarchy, being adorned by the Holy Spirit, are preserved immutably!

*Theotokion:*

Perceiving thee from afar, O Virgin Maiden, bearing in thine arms God incarnate, Isaiah foretold!

*Ode 5. Heirmos: Enlightened by the radiance of Thy coming, O Christ...*

*Troparion:*

I have fallen into corrupt passions, and I fear Thy righteous judgment seat, O righteous Lord; therefore I pray Thee: Strengthen me to do good deeds that shall justify me!

*Troparion:*

The unknown and secret things of my heart Thou knowest, O my God, Creator, and Lord; condemn me not therefore in the hour of judgment, when Thou shalt come to judge all things!

*Martyricon:*

Partaking of the fire, the saints showed forth the kindling of their love for God: therefore, refreshed, the God-bearers rejoiced in the expectation of future blessings!

*Martyricon:*

Strengthened by the hope of future blessings, the Martyrs [f. 136A] the tearing of their limbs most patiently, and with their sinews, through unflagging endurance, choked the crafty one.

*Theotokion:*

God-speaking lips cannot tell the ineffable mystery of thy divine birthgiving, O Bride of God: for thou bearest the Uncircumscribed One, and in thy hands dost hold Him Who holds all things in His hand!

*Another Heirmos: The ember showed forth unto Isaiah...*

*Troparion:*

Divinely shining near the divine Source in brightness, the Cherubim and Seraphim, together with the exalted Thrones, godlike enlighten the rest!

*Troparion:*

As the Giver of all light-outpouring, O Word of God, Thou hast brought forth radiant mirrors receiving Thine illumination with gladness and unwavering understanding!

*Theotokion:*

Rejoicing, which destroys the mourning of the forebears, did the sacred Archangel Gabriel, having flown down from heaven, announce unto thee, O pure Bride of God!

*Ode 6. Heirmos: The nethermost abyss has surrounded us, and there is none...*

*Troparion:*

Being the Physician, O Christ, heal the passions of my heart, and wash me from every defilement with the streams of Thy mercies, O my Jesus, that I may hymn and magnify Thy tender mercy!

(see fol. 147)[^165]

*Troparion:*

Turn me back, O Christ, who wander upon the paths of perdition and fall into the pit of sins, and guide me unto the unerring path of Thy venerable ordinances, that I may glorify Thee!

*Martyricon:*

Truly precious stones pelted with stones, the glorious crown-bearing Martyrs of the Lord rejected not the Rock of life, nor sacrificed unto a carved stone!

*Martyricon:*

Having renewed their souls with the plow of faith, the Martyrs, through the divine ear of grain of their passions, made a hundredfold wheat to grow, and were accounted worthy of blessed delight!

*Theotokion:*

Having borne the Fire of the fiery ministers, thou wast shown forth, O all-immaculate, all-pure Virgin, truly excelling all creation, blessed among women, O thou who art full of God's grace!

*Another Heirmos: A voice of tumult...*

*Troparion:*

The divine places of the glory of Thy primordial Light, O Master Christ, kindled with light-beaming radiances, eternally abide in light-like splendor!

*Troparion:*

Strengthened by divine power, the Seraphim, crying out with unceasing voices the Thrice-Holy hymn, lead us to venerate the three-hypostatic Nature!

[f. 136A] *Theotokion:*

As the Lord swore in truth of old unto David, He fulfilled the request from thy womb: for thou, O Maiden, hast borne Him Who reigns over heaven and earth!

*Kontakion to the Angels, Tone 2:*

Commanders of God, ministers of divine glory, leaders of Angels and guides of men: ask that which is profitable for us, and great mercy, as commanders of the Bodiless Hosts!

*Ikos:*

Thou didst say, O Lover of mankind, in Thy Scriptures: A multitude of Angels rejoice in heaven over one man who repents, O Immortal One! Therefore we who are in iniquities dare ever to entreat Thee, O sinless, only Knower of hearts, as Thou art tender-hearted, to have compassion and send down compunction upon us, the unworthy, O Master; grant us forgiveness, for on behalf of all of us the commanders of the Bodiless Hosts make supplication unto Thee!

*Ode 7. Heirmos: We perceive thee, O Mother of God, as a spiritual furnace...*

*Troparion:*

Daniel tamed the lions of old, girded with virtue; emulate him, O my soul, and make inactive him who roars ever like a lion, wishing to seize thee, by ever looking unto God!

*Troparion:*

Having defiled my soul with a multitude of fornication, O Lord...

(see fol. 148)[^166]

...yet having exceedingly great grace, O Christ, receive me even as the prodigal, and have compassion on me who sing: Praised and supremely glorified is the God of our fathers!

*Martyricon:*

Firmly strengthened by the law of Christ, the invincible Martyrs courageously cast down the evil wiles of the lawless; and lawfully having ended their course in success, [they cried]: Praised and supremely glorified is the God of our fathers!

*Martyricon:*

God-radiant Martyrs of the Lord, manifestly kindled with the light of the Trinity, ye dispersed the darkness of torment and the gloom of deceit, singing: Praised and supremely glorified is the God of our fathers!

*Theotokion:*

The laws of nature are renewed in thee: for beyond the laws of the flesh hast thou borne the Lawgiver, Christ, O all-immaculate one, Who establishes the deliverance of the law for all who sing: Praised and supremely glorified is the God of our fathers!

*Another Heirmos: Into the fiery furnace...*

*Troparion:*

Around the throne of Thine ineffable glory, the heavenly minds, ever dancing, hymn with unceasing lips: Supremely divine God of our fathers, blessed art Thou!

*Troparion:*

Beholding Thee being taken up unto [f. 136A] the angelic ranks opened the heavenly gates unto Thee, singing: Supremely divine God of our fathers, blessed art Thou!

*Theotokion:*

Manifesting thee as the fulfillment of the Law and the Prophets, O Maiden, he cried aloud: Behold, thou alone, O all-hymned one, dost bear the supremely divine and blessed God of our fathers and of us!

*Ode 8. Heirmos: In the furnace the children of Israel, as in a crucible...*

*Troparion:*

O my Redeemer, compassionate Christ, deliver me who cry aloud from the gloom of sin that now encompasses me and from all temptations: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, hymn, and supremely exalt Him unto ages!

*Troparion:*

When Thou shalt come, O Christ, in glory to judge the world, number me also among the standing of Thine elect, who cry aloud and say: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, hymn, and supremely exalt Him unto ages!

*Martyricon:*

Wisely did ye pass over unto the land of Christ, O holy Martyrs: for having contended mightily upon earth, ye received heavenly life, singing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, hymn, and supremely exalt Him unto ages!

*Martyricon:*

Having put off the mortal body, ye were manifestly clothed from above in the garment of immortality as victors, O Martyrs of Christ, crying aloud: Bless the Lord...

(see fol. 149)[^167]

...all ye works of the Lord, hymn, and supremely exalt Him unto ages!

*Theotokion:*

The voices of the holy [prophets] from of old foretold thee, O all-pure one, to be the Mother of God Who made all things; unto Whom we sing, O all-pure one: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, hymn, and supremely exalt Him unto ages!

*Another Heirmos: We praise [the Lord]...*

*Troparion:*

In the likeness of Thy goodness hast Thou fashioned the Angels, who fulfill Thy commandments with might, O Word, and guide all the faithful to cry: Let all creation hymn the Lord of all essence, and supremely exalt Him!

*Troparion:*

With divine virtues hast Thou adorned the heavenly life, the hierarchy of holy Angels, enlightening them as they cry unto Thee, O Christ: Let all creation hymn the Lord of all essence, and supremely exalt Him unto ages!

*Troparion:*

Rejoicing, let us brightly sing the divine hymn of the Bodiless Hosts unstintingly, and glorifying the neighboring ones, with them let us cry aloud: Let all creation hymn the Lord of all essence, and supremely exalt Him unto ages!

*Theotokion:*

Righteousness, which guides the universe, worthily loved thee, O all-immaculate and all-pure Virgin; ineffably, O most pure Mother of God, did It take up Its abode in thee; Which glorify: Let all creation hymn the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

[f. 136A] *Ode 9. Heirmos: An image of thy pure birthgiving, in the burning [furnace]...*

*Troparion:*

Like the Canaanite woman I cry unto Thee: Have mercy on me, O Word! For I have a soul distressed by demonic assaults, acting foolishly in unfitting ways, and insensible of Thy divine fear, O Long-suffering One!

*Troparion:*

Establish, O Lord, the soles of my soul upon the rock of Thy commandments; trip up the serpent who shamelessly desires to trip me up, and deliver me from his evil-doing, as Thou art good and plenteous in mercy!

*Martyricon:*

Having crossed the unstable waters of temptations and the surges of cruel wounds, O Martyrs, ye manifestly reached the harbor of the supreme Kingdom, enjoying divine stillness!

*Martyricon:*

Having become radiant, O Martyrs, ye were accounted worthy of the never-setting Light, to dwell in the Church of the firstborn; and ye rejoice with the angelic choirs: with them pray unto the Giver of life on our behalf!

*Theotokion:*

Thou bearest Him Who bears all things, and sucklest Him Who gives food unto all; great and fearful beyond understanding is thy mystery, O Ark of venerable sanctification, Virgin Mother of God...

(see fol. 150)[^168]

...therefore with faith do we call thee blessed!

*Another Heirmos: Thou art all desire, all sweetness...*

*Troparion:*

Assuming bodily forms, the Angels appeared light-like unto Thy divine disciples, telling of Thy second coming, O Christ: with them all of us, glorifying Thee as God, magnify Thee!

*Troparion:*

As the grace of every rational nature, Thou makest a second brightness unto Thy primordial ray on account of Thine extreme goodness: therefore all of us, singing hymns unto Thee, magnify Thee!

*Theotokion:*

Perceiving thy light-like virginity, O all-pure one, the divine Archistrategos was astonished, and brought joy unto thee: therefore all of us magnify thee as the Mother of God!

*Exapostilarion to the Angels:*

Archangels, Angels, Principalities, Dominions, Powers, Authorities, and Thrones, many-eyed Cherubim, and six-winged Seraphim: pray now for us, that we may be delivered from perils and from unquenchable fire!

*Glory...*

Thou Who adornedst heaven with stars as God, and through Thine Angels guidest the whole earth in light: O Creator of all, save those who hymn Thee!

*Both now...*

[f. 136A] *Theotokion:*

Thou art my protectress, O all-pure one, and a strong rampart, and intercessor for the world: and unto thee do I fall down crying: Deliver me from cruel evils, and snatch me from eternal fire, O Virgin, who place my hope in thee!

*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Monday, Tone 8:*

*Sticheron 1:*

Another age awaits thee, O soul, and the Judge desires to convict thy secret and grievous deeds; abide not then in the things of this world, but anticipate [the end], crying unto the Judge: O God, cleanse me and save me!

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord...*

*Sticheron 2:*

Reject me not, O my Savior, who am held fast by sinful slothfulness; but arouse my thought unto repentance, and show me to be a skilled laborer in Thy vineyard, granting me the reward of the eleventh hour, and great mercy!

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us...*

*Sticheron 3, Martyricon:*

Passion-bearers of Christ! Come, all ye people, let us honor with songs and spiritual hymns the luminaries of the world and preachers of the faith, gushing fountains from which healings flow forth unto the faithful: by their prayers, O Christ our God, grant peace unto Thy world, and...

(see fol. 151)[^169]

...unto our souls great mercy!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*

Receiving the prayers of sinners, and despising not the groanings of the afflicted: pray unto Him Who came forth from thy pure womb, that we may be saved, O most holy Virgin!

*At the Liturgy, The Beatitudes, Tone 8:*

*Troparion 1:*

Through food the enemy led Adam out of Paradise, but by the Cross Christ brought the Thief therein, who cried aloud: Remember me when Thou comest in Thy kingdom!

*Troparion 2:*

Grant me a fountain of compunction by Thy tender-heartedness, O Christ God, cleansing me from all defilements of measureless evils, and make me a partaker of Thy kingdom by grace!

*Troparion 3:*

Bringing the ranks of Thine Angels unto supplication before Thee, O Christ: save and have compassion on us for their sake, as Thou art supremely good, overlooking all our sins committed in knowledge and in ignorance!

*Troparion 4, Martyricon:*

By the shedding of your blood, O holy ones, ye manifestly drowned the spiritual Pharaoh, and now ye pour forth streams of miracles, drying up the deep of [f. 136A] illnesses: therefore are ye called blessed!

*Glory... Trinity Hymn:*

With the voice of Angels, ye faithful, let us cry aloud the Thrice-Holy song: Holy art Thou, O Father, with the Son and the Spirit! Save us who cry unto Thee, O consubstantial Trinity, One God!

*Both now... Theotokion:*

Thou who didst bear the unwaning Light, enlighten my soul that is ever darkened by demonic assaults, O all-immaculate one, and snatch me from the future fire by thy divine prayers!

*Prokeimenon, Tone 4:*

Who makes His Angels spirits, and His ministers a flame of fire. (Ps. 103:4)

*Verse: Bless the Lord, O my soul!*

*Epistle to the Hebrews, Section 303 (Heb. 2:2–10):*

Brethren: If the word spoken by Angels was steadfast, and every transgression and disobedience received a just recompense of reward, how shall we escape if we neglect so great a salvation; which at the first began to be spoken by the Lord, and was confirmed unto us by those who heard Him; God also bearing witness, both with signs and wonders, and with diverse miracles, and distributions of the Holy Spirit, according to His own will? For not unto Angels did God put in subjection the world to come, whereof we speak...

(see fol. 152)[^170]

...but one in a certain place testified, saying: What is man, that Thou art mindful of him, or the son of man, that Thou visitest him? Thou madest him a little lower than the Angels; Thou crownedst him with glory and honor, and didst set him over the works of Thy hands; Thou hast put all things in subjection under his feet. For in that He put all in subjection under him, He left nothing that is not put under him. But now we see not yet all things put under him. But we see Jesus, Who was made a little lower than the Angels, for the suffering of death crowned with glory and honor, that by the grace of God He might taste death for all. For it became Him, for Whom are all things, and by Whom are all things, in bringing many sons unto glory, to make the Captain of their salvation perfect through sufferings.

*Alleluia, Tone 4.*

*The Gospel according to Luke, Section 51 (Luke 10:16–21):*

The Lord said unto His disciples: He that hears you hears Me; and he that rejects you rejects Me; and he that rejects Me rejects Him Who sent Me. And the seventy returned with joy, saying: Lord, even the demons are subject unto us through Thy name! And He said unto them: [f. 136A] I beheld Satan as lightning fallen from heaven. Behold, I give unto you power to tread on serpents and scorpions, and over all the power of the enemy; and nothing shall by any means hurt you. Notwithstanding, in this rejoice not, that the spirits are subject unto you; but rather rejoice, because your names are written in heaven. In that hour Jesus rejoiced in spirit, and said: I thank Thee, O Father, Lord of heaven and earth, that Thou hast hid these things from the wise and prudent, and hast revealed them unto babes: even so, Father; for so it seemed good before Thee!

## MONDAY EVENING VESPERS (FOR TUESDAY) (TONE 8)

*On Monday Evening, at "Lord, I have cried," Penitential Stichera, Tone 8:*

*Tone 8.*

*Podoben: "When from the Tree" (Е҆гда̀ ѿ дре́ва):*

*Sticheron 1:*

I have surpassed all men in sin: to what manner of repentance shall I school myself? If I sigh like the publican, I think to burden heaven; if I weep like the harlot, with my tears I defile the earth: but grant me remission of sins, O God, and have mercy on me!

*Sticheron 2:*

I have committed every sin: I have surpassed all in...

(see fol. 153)[^171]

...fornication; if I desire to repent, I have no streams of tears; if I live slothfully even now, I shall be condemned unto torment: but grant me amendment, O only Good God, and have mercy on me!

*Sticheron 3:*

Grant me a shower of tears of divine compunction, O Christ, as Thou art compassionate, that I may weep and wash away the defilements of my transgressions born of pleasures, and appear purified before Thee when Thou, the Judge, shalt come from heaven, O Lord, to judge men, as the only righteous Judge!

*Other Stichera, to the Holy Forerunner John.*

*Podoben: "The same Tone and Podoben" (Гла́съ и҆ подо́бенъ то́йже):*

*Sticheron 4:*

Preacher of Christ and Baptist, Angel, Apostle, Martyr, Prophet, Forerunner, Lamp, close Friend, Seal of the Prophets, most venerable among those born [of women], mediator between the Old and New grace, radiant voice of the Word: by thy sacred intercessions direct our supplications unto the compassionate God!

*Sticheron 5:*

Behold the Lamb of God Who takes away the sins of the world! didst thou cry aloud, O blessed Forerunner, when thou sawest Christ walking upon the earth; entreat Him therefore to grant remission of sins unto those who honor thee: for thou hast truly great boldness before Him, O Baptist, as mediator [f. 136A] between the Law and grace!

*Sticheron 6:*

Make haste to deliver me from temptations, O glorious Prophet of the Lord, I pray thee: for the bitter demons that war against me have risen up entirely against me, seeking to seize the soul of thine unworthy servant like a bird; forsake me not to the end, that from my deeds they may know yet more, O all-blessed one, that thou art my refuge!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*

As ye are like-minded in natural kinship and harmonious agreement of prayer, O Mother of the King of all, and divine Forerunner: make supplication together for the servant who has greatly angered the merciful One, that He may have compassion and heal me, being moved by your intercessions, and deliver me from the fiery lot!

*Prokeimenon, Tone 8:*

The Lord will hear me when I cry unto Him. (Ps. 4:4)

*Verse: When I called, the God of my righteousness heard me.*

*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 8:*

*Sticheron 1:*

I have sinned against Thee, O Savior, like the prodigal son; receive me, O Father, who repent, and have mercy on me, O God!

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, O Thou that dwellest in heaven!*

*Sticheron 2:*

I cry unto Thee, O Christ Savior, with the voice of the publican: Cleanse me even as him, and have mercy on me, O God!

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are utterly filled with contempt.*

(see fol. 154)[^172]

*Sticheron 3, Martyricon:*

When the holy Martyrs pray for us and hymn Christ, all deceit ceases, and the human race is saved by faith!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*

Impassable Gate, mystically sealed, blessed Mother of God: receive our prayers, bring them unto Thy Son and our God, that for thy sake He may save our souls!

*Troparion to the Forerunner, Tone 2:*

The memory of the righteous is celebrated with hymns of praise, but the testimony of the Lord is sufficient for thee, O Forerunner: for thou wast shown forth truly more honorable than the Prophets, in that thou wast accounted worthy to baptize in the streams Him Whom thou didst preach; therefore having suffered for the truth with rejoicing, thou didst announce good tidings even unto those in Hades: of God manifested in the flesh, Who takes away the sin of the world, and grants us great mercy!

*Theotokion:*

We became partakers of the divine nature through thee, O Virgin Mother of God: for thou hast borne God incarnate unto us: therefore, as is our duty, all of us piously magnify thee!

## TUESDAY MATINS: REPENTANCE AND ST. JOHN THE FORERUNNER (TONE 8)

*On Tuesday at Matins, after the first Kathisma, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 8:*

*Sessional Hymn 1:*

Like the waves of the sea my transgressions have risen up against me; like a ship in the deep, I alone am sinking through many sins; but guide me unto the calm [f. 136A] harbor of repentance, O Lord, and save me!

*Sessional Hymn 2:*

I am a barren tree, O Lord, bearing no fruit of repentance at all, and I fear the hewing down, and I dread that unquenchable fire: therefore I pray Thee: Before that calamity, turn me back and save me!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion: "We became partakers of the divine nature..." (see after the Troparion).*

*After the second Kathisma, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 8:*

*Sessional Hymn 3:*

Have mercy on me, said David, and I also cry unto Thee: I have sinned, O Savior! Cleanse my sins through repentance and have mercy on me! Have mercy on me, O God, have mercy on me! Over two sins David wept, but I cry unto Thee over myriads of transgressions: he bedewed his couch with tears, but I have acquired not even a single drop; I despair and pray: Have mercy on me, O God, according unto the mandate of Thy mercy!

*Martyricon:*

Apostles, Prophets, and Martyrs, holy, Venerable, and Righteous ones, who have fought the good fight and kept the faith, having boldness before the Savior: entreat Him, as He is good, on our behalf, we pray, that our souls may be saved!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*

O Mother of God, despise me not who need protection and the help that is from thee: for in thee has my soul hoped; have mercy on me!

(see fol. 155)[^173]

*After the third Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 8:*

*Sessional Hymn 5:*

For the sake of the mercy of Thy compassion, O Master, save Thine own creature, O Christ; come, having bowed the heavens by Thy descent: therefore singing of Thy fearful dispensation, we cry unto Thee: By the supplications of Thy most pure Mother, grant us cleansing of sins, as Thou alone art tender-hearted!

*Sessional Hymn 6:*

Thou Who hast enlightened Thy saints more than gold, and glorified Thy venerable ones as God: being entreated by them, O Christ God, give peace unto our life, as Thou art the Lover of mankind, and direct our prayers like incense, O Thou Who alone restest among the saints!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*

Fervent protectress of Christians: pray ever unto thy Son, O Mother of God, to deliver us from every malice and vexation of the adversary, and to grant us forgiveness wherein we have sinned, according to the tender mercy of His compassions, through thy prayers, O Virgin Mother!

*Then Psalm 50, and the Polyeleos.*

*Penitential Canon, Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos: Taking up the song of Moses, cry aloud, O my soul: A helper and protector was He unto me for salvation; this is my God, and I will glorify Him!*

*Troparion:*

Let us bitterly weep for ourselves, brethren, before our departure, that through good tears we may escape the tears of torment, which then possess nothing profitable!

[f. 136A] *Troparion:*

Myriads of times, O Christ, I promised to repent; yet having a soul like a purchased slave, I fall into transgressions: have compassion, O Savior, on my weakness!

*Martyricon:*

Having endured the fire of torment through divine bedewing, O passion-bearers of Christ, deliver me who lie in cruel passions from the fire of Gehenna!

*Martyricon:*

Shown forth mighty against the enemies through divine power, ye cast down their powerless strength, O worthily blessed Martyrs of Christ!

*Theotokion:*

O fiery tongs, which Isaiah saw of old: burn up the material passions of my heart, O Mother of God, and destroy them utterly!

*Another Canon, to the Forerunner, Tone the same. Heirmos: In the deep...*

*Troparion 1:*

O Baptist and Forerunner of Christ, strengthen my mind that is grievously submerged by bodily pleasures, and calm the waves of passions, that having come unto divine stillness, I may hymn thee!

*Troparion 2:*

Enlightened with incomprehensible illumination, like a most luminous star thou didst run before the spiritual Orient: pray, O Baptist, that my heart may be illuminated thereby, which is darkened by all demonic assaults!

(see fol. 156)[^174]

*Glory... Troparion 3:*

In the river of old, O all-wise one, didst thou immerse the Abyss of mercy, Which by ensuing grace drowns all transgression; dry up, I pray thee, O blessed one, the torrents of my transgressions by thy sacred intercession!

*Both now... Theotokion:*

A kinsman wast thou, O blessed Forerunner, unto the pure Virgin who made God incarnate; with her we honor and entreat thee: make us who now dwell in thy divine temple to be temples of the Holy Spirit!

*Ode 3. Heirmos: Show forth my barren mind as fruitful, O God, Husbandman of souls and Planter of good things, by Thy tender mercy!*

*Troparion 1:*

With slothful slumber have I burdened my soul: arouse me, O Christ, unto the vigilance of repentance, to do Thy commandments!

*Troparion 2:*

Show me not to be put to shame, O Jesus, in the fearful day, but having turned me back before the end, deliver me from cruel torments!

*Troparion 3, Martyricon:*

Having well conformed yourselves unto the sufferings of Christ by divine images, O passion-bearers of Christ, heal the bitter passions of my soul!

*Troparion 4, Martyricon:*

Accounted worthy of ever-existing blessings in the heavens, O passion-bearers, ye firmly endured every temptation of cruel afflictions upon earth!

*Theotokion:*

[f. 136A] *Theotokion:*

In motherly manner dost Thou nourish with milk the Nourisher of all, and barest Him in thine arms, O pure one, Who ever upholds all things by His hand!

*Another Heirmos: Establish me upon the rock of faith, widening my mouth against mine enemies, for my spirit rejoices when I sing: None is holy as our God, and none is righteous save Thee, O Lord!*

*Troparion 1:*

Heal the wounds of my soul, and by divine intercession enlighten my mind that is darkened by slothfulness, O Forerunner of the Lord, and deliver me from every adverse circumstance, I pray thee!

*Troparion 2:*

Thou didst loose the barrenness of her that bore thee, having been born by the providence of God, O most wise Forerunner; make therefore my barren heart fruitful unto me, O Forerunner of the Lord, by thine intercession, that it may bring forth the shoots of virtues!

*Troparion 3:*

Entreat [God] that the builders of this divine temple may through thy love attain unto the life on high; and those who serve in faith in thy temple, make them to be temples unto the Divine Spirit, O Baptist and Forerunner, by thy prayers!

*Theotokion:*

The Forerunner leaped with joy while yet carried in his mother's womb, and bowed down before the Lord Who was carried in thy womb, O woman endowed with divine grace; entreat Him to deliver us from every tribulation!

*Ode 4. Heirmos: Foreseeing Thy birth from the Virgin, the Prophet proclaimed, crying aloud: I heard report of Thee and was afraid; for from the south, from the holy shadowed mountain, art Thou come, O Christ!*

*Troparion 1:*

Seeing me despoiled on every side and impoverished, O Word, the crafty enemy...

(see fol. 157)[^175]

...rejoices over my perdition; O Lord of glory, Who enrichest the poor: deliver me from his wickedness!

*Troparion 2:*

I have defiled hands and eyes, O Lord, doing what is not seemly to do, and I have provoked Thy compassions unto wrath and exhausted Thy long-suffering; yet have compassion and save me, as Thou art God!

*Martyricon 1:*

How marvelous is our God in His saints, who believed in Him, broke down the idols of perdition, and inherited the expanse of Paradise, whence Adam of old was cast forth!

*Martyricon 2:*

By the shedding of your blood, O blessed ones, ye brought to an end the bloodshed once offered unto the demons, which was destructive unto all, bringing destruction unto deceived men: therefore are ye ever called blessed!

*Theotokion:*

Instructed by the Spirit, the most glorious choir of the Prophets prefigured in diverse manners by sacred symbols thy mystery which is beyond understanding, O Mother of God, beholding the fulfillment thereof from afar!

*Another Heirmos: Thou art come forth from the Virgin, not an intercessor, nor an angel, but Thyself, O Lord, having become incarnate, and hast saved all of me, a man; therefore I cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!*

*Troparion 1:*

Unto Him Who bowed the heavens and mingled with men, thou didst bow thy head [under] thine own right hand, O most bountiful one; by it preserve me, keeping my heart in peace!

*Troparion 2:*

A citizen of the wilderness did the untrodden desert possess in thee, O blessed Forerunner [f. 136A] therefore I cry unto thee: Make my soul, which is utterly desolate of divine deeds, to be fruitful!

*Troparion 3:*

Vindicating the divine Law, thou wast unlawfully slaughtered; therefore I pray thee: Amend me who ever transgress the law and am drowned by demonic deceptions!

*Theotokion:*

Look upon me who am sick, O all-immaculate one, and loose my cruel passions which are difficult to heal, that I may magnify thee who hast magnified all mankind!

*Ode 5. Heirmos: Having driven away the gloom of my soul, O my Savior, enlighten me with the light of Thy commandments, as the only King of peace!*

*Troparion 1:*

In my folly I heap sins upon sins, and there is no respite in my death; woe is me! How shall I appear before Christ?

*Troparion 2:*

Suffering shipwreck like a vessel, I have lost my cargo which Thou gavest me, O Bountiful One; and now, having become impoverished, I cry: Despise me not, O Christ!

*Martyricon 1:*

Having despised the glory that is below and trodden under foot, O passion-bearers, ye were accounted worthy of heavenly glory, abiding with Christ!

*Martyricon 2:*

Having through faith separated your mind from love of the flesh, O passion-bearers, ye willingly embraced torments, ever cleaving unto Christ!

(see fol. 158)[^176]

*Theotokion:*

Beholding thee by the Spirit as a great mountain, O Mother of God, whence a stone was cut without hands, Daniel foresaw Thee shattering the idols of the demons!

*Another Heirmos: The enlightenment of those lying in darkness, the salvation of the despairing, O Christ my Savior, unto Thee do I rise early, O King of peace: enlighten me with Thine illumination, for other than Thee I know no God!*

*Troparion 1:*

Having baptized Christ in the streams of Jordan, O Forerunner, stream of incorruption: pray that the outflowings of my passions may be dried up, and that I may inherit the torrents of delight and the comely rejoicing of the righteous!

*Troparion 2:*

Setting forth the saving law for the people who were in the dissolution of transgressions, O Forerunner, thou didst stand between the Law and grace; therefore we pray thee: Enlighten us with the model of repentance!

*Troparion 3:*

Grant me time for repentance, O Benefactor, who have spent all the past time in despondency, having to entreat Thee on our behalf, O Word, John the great Forerunner and universal preacher of repentance!

*Theotokion:*

I have been slain by the snares and huntings of the deceitful one; quicken me, O all-immaculate Maiden, who didst bear, O Mother of God, the essential Life of all, that I may piously hymn thee, the all-praised one!

*Ode 6. Heirmos: I am held fast in the depth of sin, O Christ, and tempest-tossed in the sea of life; but even as the Prophet Jonah, lead me up from the depth of passions and save me, O Savior!*

[f. 136A] *Troparion 1:*

Even as the Canaanite woman of old, I cry unto Thee, O Savior, Son of David: Have mercy and compassions on me! For I have a soul suffering grievously, and of itself it has no desire to receive understanding!

*Troparion 2:*

The measureless waves of passions trouble me; even as Thou didst rebuke the sea of old, O Jesus, and didst save Thy holy disciples, so also raise me up and save me!

*Martyricon 1:*

The angelic and incorporeal choirs marveled at your bodily endurance, and praised Him Who granted you power, O honorable sufferers, and recompense for your deeds!

*Martyricon 2:*

Drenched with the streams of your blood, having your eyes gouged out, O Martyrs, and freezing from cruel frost: ye passed over unto the warmth of life, singing of Christ!

*Theotokion:*

As a table containing the mystical Bread, whereof eating we hunger no more, O all-praised one, we truly acknowledge thee to be the Mother and nurturer of Christ, the God of all!

*Another Heirmos: Drowned by the tempest of sins, as in the sea am I tossed by the storm; but lead me up from corruption, O my God, as the Lover of mankind!*

*Troparion 1:*

Thou who didst proclaim the Word of the Father, pray Him to receive all voices of supplication, and to grant forgiveness of sins unto those who sing of thee in faith!

*Troparion 2:*

Heal the contrition of my soul, loose the burden of my sins, and beyond hope save me by thy prayers, O blessed Forerunner; entreat Jesus, Whom thou didst baptize with thine own hand, O Forerunner, to deliver me from the hand of sin, lifting up...

(see fol. 159)[^177]

...my hands ever unto Him, O most glorious one!

*Theotokion:*

Slothful slumber holds me fast, and the sleep of sin weighs down my heart; by thy vigilant intercessions, O all-pure one, raise me up and save me!

*Kontakion of the Forerunner, Tone 6:*

The light-manifesting and divine pillar in the world, the lamp of the Sun, the Forerunner, having shown forth in the end his divine and luminous memory, sanctifies those who faithfully bow down before it, crying aloud: O Prophet and Baptist of Christ, save us all!

*Ikos:*

Pouring forth streams of grace like the fountain of Eden, the God-loving memorial of the Forerunner pours forth gifts unto all of us in need; come therefore, let us all eagerly draw from these streams of divine healings; for it refreshes souls with unceasing gifts, drowns bodily sicknesses, and soothes every infirmity in season; let us therefore sing of it in love, and kiss it in faith, venerating it honorably, and crowning it with hymns like wreaths, O ye faithful; cleanse our defilements of thought, and adorn our souls with divine virtues, that we may firmly cry: O all-wise Baptist of Christ, save us all!

*Ode 7. Heirmos: Emulating the Cherubim, the Children danced in the furnace, crying aloud: Blessed art Thou, O God, for in truth and judgment hast Thou brought all these things upon us because of our sins, O Thou Who art all-praised and all-glorified unto all ages!*

[f. 136A] *Troparion 1:*

I have forsaken Thy laws, and having subjected myself like a brute beast unto desires, I work unseemly deeds, O Christ, and have become vain in my folly as no other among men upon earth; therefore leave me not, O Savior, to perish, for the sake of Thy love of mankind!

*Troparion 2:*

In transgressions, O Lord, behold, I was conceived, as David I cry, and as the harlot I shed tears, and as a provoking servant I have provoked Thee, the only truly Good God; leave me not therefore, O Savior, to perish, for the sake of Thy love of mankind!

*Martyricon 1:*

Manfully did the assembly of sufferers contend, and was crowned with martyr's wreaths by the life-bearing right hand; for in truth they loved God Who created all things by His Word, and now rejoicing in the heavens they delight in divine delight!

*Martyricon 2:*

Having their eyes gouged out and their hands and feet severed, the glorious martyrs speeded their course unto the heavenly journey, tripping up the assaults of the adversary; by their prayers, O Word, save all who glorify Thee!

*Theotokion:*

The Cherubim glorify, the Seraphim hymn, Thrones, Authorities, and Dominions ever sing of thy birthgiving which is beyond understanding, O all-praised Mary; for thou alone barest God in the flesh: entreat Him, O pure one, to save all of us who honor thee in love!

*Another Heirmos: The God-opposing decree of the lawless tyrant raised a high flame; but Christ spread a spiritual dew upon the God-fearing children, He Who is blessed and all-glorified!*

*Troparion 1:*

Root up with the axe of thy prayer, cutting down through repentance the instigator of the passions of my heart, and plant therein, O Forerunner...

(see fol. 160)[^178]

...divine dispassion and the purest fear of God, alienating me from every malice!

*Troparion 2:*

Since thou didst baptize in the streams of the Jordan the Lord Who covers His upper chambers with waters, pray Him, O glorious Forerunner, ever to grant unto mine eyes a fountain of divine compunction!

*Troparion 3:*

Having proclaimed the Lamb of God Who takes away the sins of the world, O Forerunner: entreat Him, O glorious one, to show me alien unto the portion of the goats, and to unite me unto the sheep on His right hand!

*Theotokion:*

A barren womb bore thee, O Virgin, who barest in thy womb the Incarnate Word, Whom the great Forerunner, having recognized Him by divine leaping as the all-holy Offspring of the barren, adored with rejoicing!

*Ode 8. Heirmos: He Who of old prefigured on Mount Sinai the virgin miracle unto Moses: sing, bless, and exalt Him supreme unto all ages!*

*Troparion 1:*

Thou camest down unto us, having become incarnate of a Mother; yet I, understanding it in no wise, am enslaved by pleasures; but in Thy goodness turn me back, O Christ, Who art the conversion of all!

*Troparion 2:*

Thou Good Shepherd, O Word, having turned back my wretched soul that wanders upon the mountains of transgression, save me, lest the deceitful enemy swallow me up utterly!

[f. 136A] [Tuesday — Duplicate Photographic Exposure of Folios 159v–160r (Leaf p164)][^179]

[This physical leaf in the codex facsimile is an identical secondary photographic exposure of the spread of folios 159v and 160r. The complete liturgical text is transcribed and translated under leaf p164.]

[f. 136A] *Martyricon 1:*

Let us stand manfully together, cried the valiant sufferers one to another, when sorely wounded: Behold, Christ reaches out unto us wreaths of victory unto all ages!

*Martyricon 2:*

With stout sinews and by your firm sufferings ye throttled the dragon who thought to seduce you with food, and were shown forth to be heirs of the delight of Paradise!

*Theotokion:*

That God might make us radiant, He took flesh from thy pure blood and became earthly, O Virgin Mother of God; entreat Him ever on behalf of those who hymn thee!

*Another Heirmos: The fiery furnace of old in Babylon divided its operation by the divine command, burning the Chaldeans, but bedewing the faithful who sang: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!*

*Troparion 1:*

Stretch forth thy right hand unto me who lie upon the earth, O Forerunner, who didst stretch forth thy right hand and wash with waters the Undefiled One; deliver me from bodily defilements, purifying me wholly through repentance, and save me!

*Troparion 2:*

Having opportunity to repent, O my soul, shake off the heavy sleep of slothfulness, make haste to conquer, crying aloud with all unto the Mother of God: O tender-hearted one, have compassion on me by the supplications of thy Baptist!

*Troparion 3:*

The torrents of passions and the waters of malice have entered into my soul, O blessed Forerunner; make haste quickly to deliver me, thou who didst wash in river streams the calm abyss of dispassion!

*Theotokion:*

Having borne in the flesh the Most High God, deliver me from the fire of passions that afflict me, and enrich me who am sorely impoverished with divine virtues...

(see fol. 161)[^180]

...O all-pure one, that being saved I may hymn thee!

*Ode 9. Heirmos: Ye earthborn, who has ever heard the like, or who has ever seen, that a Virgin was found with child and brought forth an infant without pain? Such is thy miracle, and thee, O pure Mother of God Mary, do we magnify!*

*Troparion 1:*

O how fearful is Thy judgment-seat, whereunto I must come for judgment, unable utterly to escape its terror; having fallen into slothfulness, turn me back, O only Creator, Who didst justify Manasseh when he had sinned!

*Troparion 2:*

Stay the streams of my measureless evils, O Christ, as I cry unto Thee, and grant me a drop of tears for the cleansing of defilement; then grant me remission, and in Thine unceasing mercy save my repenting soul!

*Martyricon 1:*

The radiant memory of the divine sufferers, having shone forth like the sun, enlightens all the ends of the earth, and through the Divine Spirit drives away all the darkness of idols and the gloom of the assault of passions!

*Martyricon 2:*

The honorable battalion of victorious soldiers, the chosen assembly of martyrs, the blessed choir, was numbered among the choir of the bodiless powers; by their prayers, O Christ, make all of us partakers of Thy kingdom!

*Theotokion:*

With radiant rays having shone forth from thy womb [f. 136A] unto us, and having destroyed the night of godlessness, O Virgin Mother Mary: enlighten all who honor thee in faith, and deliver us from the inextinguishable darkness of eternal condemnation!

*Another Heirmos: The Son of the Father without beginning, God and Lord, having become incarnate of the Virgin, hath appeared unto us to enlighten the darkened, and to gather the dispersed: therefore do we magnify the all-praised Mother of God!*

*Troparion 1:*

Deliver me from the mire of sin, O Lord, Who alone art sinless and abundantly merciful, by the supplications of the Baptist, who proclaimed Thee unto all the world as the Lamb of God Who takes away the sins of men!

*Troparion 2:*

As a sweet-smelling rose, as a fragrant cypress, as an unfading lily, as honorable myrrh do I have thee, O Forerunner of the Lord; deliver me from the foul stench of my deeds by thy prayers, who flee under thy protection!

*Troparion 3:*

Make me, who am barren through unfruitfulness, O all-blessed one, ever to bring forth a goodly offspring of deeds of virtues, working a wonder in me, O Lord, and making me a partaker and fellow-dweller in the assembly of the saints!

*Troparion 4:*

Unto us who love thee and bless thee in faith, rejoicing in thy divine temple, grant from heaven release from cruel afflictions, O Forerunner of the Lord, amendment of life, and deliverance from transgressions!

*Theotokion:*

Carried in the womb of the Mother of God, thou didst bow down before Him Who upholds all things by His nod; together with her entreat Him, O glorious one, that my humble soul may be saved, which daily falls into many transgressions!

(see fol. 162)[^181]

*Exapostilarion of the Forerunner:*

Most glorious Forerunner and preacher of grace, who didst preach sacred repentance unto all, and having shown forth the Lamb Who takes away the sins of the world, O blessed one: entreat Him now also to have mercy on the world!

*Glory... Another Exapostilarion:*

Let us all praise the Forerunner John, the Baptist of the Savior, a Prophet born of a Prophet, nursling of the wilderness, and offspring of Elizabeth!

*Both now... Theotokion:*

All the Prophets proclaimed thee beforehand, O Virgin: David called thee a Mountain, and an Ark, a Lampstand, and a Ladder, a Bridge, and a Jar carrying Manna; as the Mother of God we honor thee worthily!

*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 8:*

*Sticheron 1:*

Pondering my foolish deeds and the evils I have committed, I flee unto Thy compassions, emulating the publican, the weeping harlot, and the prodigal son; therefore I fall down before Thee, O Merciful One: before Thou condemnest me, spare me, O my God, and have mercy on me!

*Sticheron 2:*

Overlook my transgressions, O Lord born of the Virgin, and cleanse my heart, making it a temple for Thy Holy Spirit; cast me not away from Thy presence, O Thou Who hast measureless and great mercy!

*Martyricon:*

Taking up the Cross of Christ as an unconquerable weapon, the holy Martyrs brought to naught all the power of the devil, received heavenly crowns, and became a wall unto us, praying unto Him.

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*

[f. 136A] Save Thy servants from perils, O Virgin Mother of God, for after God we all flee unto thee as an unbreakable rampart and protectress!

*At the Liturgy, The Beatitudes, Tone 8:*

*Troparion 1:*

We offer unto Thee the voice of the thief, and we pray: Remember us, O Lord, in Thy kingdom!

*Troparion 2:*

Overlook my many transgressions, O Lover of mankind, and make me a partaker of the brightness on high, O Bountiful One!

*Troparion 3:*

Thou who didst immerse the Abyss of tender-heartedness in the waters, O Forerunner: dry up the abyss of my evils by thy prayers!

*Troparion 4, Martyricon:*

Ye who dried up the streams of deceit with the flows of your blood, O passion-bearers of Christ: worthily are ye glorified!

*Glory... Trinity Hymn:*

Trinity without beginning, I bow down before Thee: Father, and Son, and Right Spirit; I acknowledge Thee as one simple and tri-hypostatic essence!

*Both now... Theotokion:*

The Fruit of thy womb, O pure one, has made him who stumbled through the fruit of old once again a dweller in Paradise!

*Prokeimenon, Tone 7: The righteous man shall be glad in the Lord, and shall hope in Him. (Ps. 63:11)*

*Verse: Hear, O God, my prayer, when I make supplication unto Thee.*

*Epistle to the Colossians, Section 258 (Col. 3:12–16):*

Brethren: Put on therefore, as the elect of God, holy and beloved, bowels of mercies, kindness, humbleness of mind, meekness, long-suffering;

(see fol. 163)[^182]

forbearing one another, and forgiving one another, if any man have a quarrel against any: even as Christ forgave you, so also do ye. And above all these things put on love, which is the bond of perfectness. And let the peace of God rule in your hearts, to the which also ye are called in one body; and be ye thankful. Let the word of Christ dwell in you richly in all wisdom; teaching and admonishing one another in psalms and hymns and spiritual songs, singing with grace in your hearts unto the Lord.

*Alleluia, Tone 4.*

*Verse 1: The righteous man shall flourish like a palm tree. (Ps. 91:13)*

*Verse 2: Planted in the house of the Lord... (Ps. 91:14)*

*The Gospel according to Matthew, Section 40 (Matt. 11:2–15):*

At that time: When John had heard in the prison the works of Christ, he sent two of his disciples, and said unto Him: Art Thou He that should come, or do we look for another? Jesus answered and said unto them: Go and show John again those things which ye do hear and see: the blind receive their sight, and the lame walk, the lepers are cleansed, and the deaf hear, the dead are raised up, and the poor have the gospel preached to them. And blessed is he, whosoever shall not be offended in Me. And as they departed, Jesus began to say unto the multitudes concerning John: What went ye out into the wilderness to see? A reed shaken with the wind?

[f. 136A] But what went ye out for to see? A man clothed in soft raiment? Behold, they that wear soft clothing are in kings' houses. But what went ye out for to see? A prophet? Yea, I say unto you, and more than a prophet. For this is he, of whom it is written: Behold, I send My messenger before Thy face, who shall prepare Thy way before Thee. Truly I say unto you, among them that are born of women there has not risen a greater than John the Baptist; notwithstanding, he that is least in the kingdom of heaven is greater than he. And from the days of John the Baptist until now the kingdom of heaven suffers violence, and the violent take it by force. For all the prophets and the law prophesied until John. And if ye will receive it, this is Elijah, who was to come. He that has ears to hear, let him hear.

*Communion Verse: The righteous man shall be in everlasting remembrance. (Ps. 111:6)*

## TUESDAY EVENING VESPERS (FOR WEDNESDAY) (TONE 8)

*On Tuesday Evening, at "Lord, I have cried," Stichera to the Cross, Tone 3.*

*Podoben: "Great is the power of Thy Cross, O Lord" (Ве́лія Крⷭ҇та̀ твоегѡ̀ Гдⷭ҇и):*

*Sticheron 1:*

Creation was sanctified, O only Long-suffering One, by the precious blood dropping from Thy side, and the rivers of polytheism were dried up; and Adam was delivered from condemnation by Thy Crucifixion, O Word!

(see fol. 164)[^183]

*Sticheron 2:*

He Who is the Creator and Lord of all things and the God of all is willing to be numbered among the condemned, and willingly endures suffering for men; lifted up upon the Cross by the curse of the Law, Thou art crucified of Thine own will, O Benefactor and Lord; and being pierced in Thy side with a spear, O Giver of life, Thou didst pour forth two fountains of immortality; therefore unceasingly do we all faithfully glorify Thy sufferings, O Bountiful One!

*Other Stichera, to the Most Holy Mother of God [Staurotheotokia], Tone the same:*

*Sticheron 3:*

Beholding Him Who was born of thee hanging upon the Tree, O all-immaculate one, thou didst cry aloud, exclaiming: O my most beloved Child, whither is Thy light-bearing beauty set, Who hast adorned the nature of mankind?

*Sticheron 4:*

Wounded by the weapon of the evil serpent, and lying half-dead upon the bed of despair: heal me swiftly, O Maiden, with the medicine of thy prayer, and save me, O my hope and protection!

*Sticheron 5:*

Put off, O soul, the heavy burdens of thy former transgressions, and bow thy neck unto the easy yoke of thy Lord, Who shone forth from the Virgin as from a light cloud at the angelic voice!

*Glory... Both now... Staurotheotokion:*

A sword pierced thy heart, O all-pure one, when thou sawest Thy Son upon the Cross, and thou didst cry aloud: Leave me not childless, O my Son and my God, Who didst preserve me a Virgin after childbearing!

[f. 136A] *Prokeimenon, Tone 3: Thy mercy, O Lord, shall pursue me all the days of my life. (Ps. 22:6)*

*Verse: The Lord is my shepherd, and nothing shall fail me.*

*At the Aposticha, Stichera to the Cross, Tone 3:*

*Sticheron 1:*

We bow down before Thy precious Cross, O Christ, which Thou hast preserved for the world, the great cleansing of us sinners, the victory of the Church, and the boast of all the inhabited earth; prefiguring the Cross, Moses on the mountain lifted up his hands on high and conquered Amalek; and Thou, O Savior, having stretched forth Thy palms upon the precious Cross, didst embrace all and save them from the bondage of the enemy, and gavest unto me a life-giving sign to flee from the bow of mine adversaries: therefore, O Word, I bow down before Thy precious Cross!

*Martyricon:*

The Prophets, and Apostles of Christ, and Martyrs taught us to hymn the Consubstantial Trinity; and they enlightened the deceived nations, and made the sons of men partakers with the Angels!

*Glory... Both now... Staurotheotokion:*

When thou sawest the unwaning Sun, Who shone forth from thy womb, setting upon the Cross and darkening the sun's rays, burdened in soul by the darkness of grief, thou didst cry aloud: O Thou Who didst set of Thine own will, shine forth again for my enlightenment and that of all the world!

*Troparion: Save, O Lord, Thy people, and bless Thine inheritance...*

*Glory... Both now... Staurotheotokion: Having acquired thy protection...*

(see fol. 165)[^184]

## WEDNESDAY MATINS: THE PRECIOUS CROSS AND THE THEOTOKOS (TONE 8)

*On Wednesday at Matins, after the first Kathisma, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 3:*

*Sessional Hymn 1:*

The Cross was planted upon the earth and reached unto the heavens, not as a tree attaining unto the height, but because Thou Who wast upon It fillest all things: O Lord, glory to Thee!

*Sessional Hymn 2:*

Lifted up upon the cypress, the pine, and the cedar, O Lamb of God, that Thou mightest save those who in faith bow down before Thy voluntary Crucifixion: O Christ God, glory to Thee!

*Glory... Both now... Staurotheotokion:*

Having acquired the Cross of Thy Son as a rod of strength, O Mother of God, thereby do we cast down the insolence of the enemy, ever magnifying thee in love!

*After the second Kathisma, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 3:*

*Sessional Hymn 3:*

Thou Who didst deign to meet the Cross and death, didst plant It in the midst of creation; when Thou wast well-pleased, O Savior, to have Thy Body nailed thereto, then the sun hid its rays; then also the thief, beholding these things, praised Thee upon the Cross, crying out reverently: Remember me, O Lord! And believing, he was received into Paradise!

*Sessional Hymn 4:*

Beholding Thine unsearchable power and voluntary Crucifixion, the angelic hosts marveled, seeing how He Who is invisible was encompassed in flesh, wishing to deliver mankind from corruption; therefore as unto our Benefactor we cry unto Thee: Glory, O Christ, to Thy tender-heartedness!

*Martyricon:*

Having clad yourselves in the whole armor of Christ, and girded yourselves with the weapon of faith, the hostile arrays [f. 136A] ye cast down idol-worship; with zealous hope of life ye endured all the torturers' threats and wounds of old; therefore ye also received crowns, O patient-souled martyrs of Christ!

*Glory... Both now... Staurotheotokion:*

A reproachful death, O Bountiful One, by crucifixion didst Thou willingly endure; beholding which, she that bare Thee was struck with awe. By her supplications, for the sake of the tender compassion of Thy Mother, O only all-good Lord, Lover of mankind, have mercy and save the world, Thou Who takest away the sins of the world!

*After the third Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 3:*

*Sessional Hymn 1:*

Crucifixion and death didst Thou accept, O Christ Savior, and didst pour forth immortal life for us, delivering the world from corruption; therefore we glorify Thy saving sufferings, O Life-giver and Lover of mankind, whereby we all are saved, having the peace and invincible weapon of Thy precious Cross!

*Glory... Sessional Hymn 2:*

Thou didst endure the Cross in dishonor, O Lord, Who art higher than all creation, that Thou mightest honor me who of old was grievously dishonored; and being pierced in Thy side with a spear, O Long-suffering One, desiring to deliver Thy creature from corruption, I hymn Thy manifold tender mercy and goodness, O Lover of mankind!

*Both now... Staurotheotokion:*

She that knew not wedlock, Thy pure Mother, O Christ, beholding Thee hanging dead upon the Tree, maternally lamenting, cried aloud: What...

(see fol. 166)[^185]

...what hath the lawless and ungrateful Jewish assembly rendered unto Thee, which enjoyed Thy many and great gifts, O my Son? I hymn Thy divine condescension!

*Canon to the Precious and Life-Creating Cross.*

*Ode 1, Tone 3. Heirmos: He Who of old gathered the waters into one gathering by His divine will, and divided the sea for the people of Israel: He is our God and is all-glorified; unto Him alone let us sing, for He hath been glorified!*

*Troparion 1:*

By the sufferings of Thy precious flesh, O Word, Thou didst make the painful passions to cease, and didst save mankind whom of old the adversary had wounded, as they devoutly bow down before Thy dispensation, which is incomprehensible to the mind!

*Troparion 2:*

He who in Eden through deceit bound man by the transgression of the commandment is bound, O Lord, with unbreakable bonds, wherewith by Thy voluntary incarnation Thou wast bound, loosing our transgressions, O Lover of mankind!

*Martyricon 1:*

Beholding the creation in distress and drowning through demonic deceits, the passion-bearers showed themselves to be stormless havens for the faithful; and by the streams of their blood the renowned ones drowned all the power of the evil one!

*Martyricon 2:*

The choir of martyrs, having radiantly joined the choirs on high, is ever illumined round about by the splendors of the uncreated Godhead, and illumines those on earth who faithfully glorify their marvels!

*Theotokion:*

Higher than the ranks on high hast thou shown thyself to be, having borne [f. 136A] upon earth the God-Word, O all-immaculate one, Who for His goodness' sake hath raised us up unto the heavenly realms by His sufferings and precious Cross!

*Another Canon, to the Mother of God. The same Heirmos:*

*Troparion 1:*

Grant unto the furrows of my soul, O Virgin, to be abundantly watered with tears; and vouchsafe me, O Sovereign Lady, to bear fruit a hundredfold, and fill my heart with gladness, that I may glorify thee!

*Troparion 2:*

For the sake of the tender compassion of thy mercy, O Sovereign Lady of all, have mercy upon my wretched soul, and deliver me from the everlasting fire and from demonic assault, who now flee under thy protection, O Mother of God!

*Troparion 3:*

As the undefiled temple of the King, I pray thee, O all-immaculate one, cleanse by thy prayers my heart which has been defiled by unruly passions, and enrich it with deeds of a pure life, O thou who art full of divine grace!

*Troparion 4:*

Why in slothfulness, O passionate soul, dost thou pass through life, and fearest not the day of the dread judgment? But arise at last from the despondency of transgressions, and now cry aloud with zeal and fervor: Save me, O pure Sovereign Lady!

*Ode 3. Heirmos: O Thou Who from non-existence didst bring all things into being, creating them by Thy Word and perfecting them by Thy Spirit, O Almighty Lord: establish me in Thy love!*

*Troparion 1:*

Thee Who art impassible by the nature of Thy Godhead, yet didst become passible in the flesh for Thy goodness' sake, the Jewish assembly put to death upon the Tree, O Thou Who makest us immortal!

(see fol. 167)[^186]

*Troparion 2:*

O Word Who didst hang the earth upon the waters, Thou wast suspended upon the Tree of Thine own will, raising me up unto the heavens, who had fallen into the malice of the adversaries!

*Martyricon 1:*

Dappled all over with wounds, O all-praised martyrs of Christ, ye stand before the Lord, receiving recompense from His rich hand!

*Martyricon 2:*

With a joyful soul the saints departed unto the contests, making an end to the sorrow of our bondage, and filling the angelic choirs with joy!

*Theotokion:*

Beholding Christ lifted up upon the Cross, she that knew not wedlock, weeping, cried aloud: The Sun of glory has set from mine eyes, He Who illumines those in darkness!

*Another Heirmos, the same:*

*Troparion 1:*

Deliver thy servant from Gehenna and from all other torments in the hour of judgment, O all-immaculate one, and make me a partaker of the Kingdom of thy Son and God!

*Troparion 2:*

Heal the wounds of my soul, and calm the tumultuous surging of my thoughts, O Sovereign Lady, and grant unto me a peaceful state!

*Troparion 3:*
