Fiercely inflamed now by passions, there is no healing for my soul, O all-immaculate one; yet leave me not utterly forsaken, O Virgin, thy servant!

*Troparion 4:*

Being dead in soul from the multitude of the malice of passions [f. 136A] quicken me, O Sovereign Lady, who didst ineffably give birth to Christ the Life-giver!

*Ode 4. Heirmos: Thou hast established for us a firm love, O Lord: for Thine Only-begotten Son didst Thou give unto death for us; therefore thankfully we cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!*

*Troparion 1:*

In truth, O Master, Who art higher than all honor, Thou didst endure reproach, O Christ, being struck upon the head with a reed, that Thou mightest honor me who was dishonored by transgression, O Lover of mankind!

*Troparion 2:*

With a crown of thorns wast Thou crowned of Thine own will, O Long-suffering One, as a true King, and Thou didst cut down the thorn-bearing desire of sin; I hymn Thy sufferings, O Savior!

*Martyricon 1:*

Uncrushed did ye remain by the torment of wounds, O Martyrs, crushing the enemy's deceit, and trampling underfoot him who boasted beyond measure and exalted himself in utter pride!

*Martyricon 2:*

In corruptible bodies having acquired divine incorruption through suffering, having steadfastly imitated the honorable sufferings of the Immortal One, O all-praised Martyrs, ye are numbered with all the bodiless hosts!

*Theotokion:*

Of old the Prophet beheld thee as a scroll, O Maiden, wherein by the Father's finger was written the incarnate Word; and He tore up the ancestral handwriting with the spear, O all-immaculate one!

*Another Heirmos:*

When the Bridegroom shall come in the night to judge the earth, then grant me, O all-immaculate one, to go forth with a shining lamp to meet Him...

(see fol. 168)[^187]

...and to bow down before His coming!

*Troparion 1:*

The dark night of passions holds me fast, thy servant; but by thy light, O good one, dispel the cloud of my soul, and guide me unto the light of the divine commandments, O all-immaculate one!

*Troparion 2:*

Drowned am I wholly by a tempest of temptations and by the surging of evil thoughts, and ever dragged down into the abyss of despair: save me, O Sovereign Lady, thy weeping servant!

*Troparion 3:*

When my soul is about to be separated from the body, then stand by me mercifully, and snatch me away from the assault of the demons who unceasingly seek my destruction, O Maiden!

*Ode 5. Heirmos: He Who is invisible appeared upon earth and willingly conversed with mortals, the Incomprehensible One; and rising early unto Him, we praise Thee, O Lover of mankind!*

*Troparion 1:*

As a Lamb wast Thou lifted up upon the Tree, and offered as a sacrifice unto the Father for our sake, O Good One; and Thou didst bring an end to idolatrous sacrifices, O Almighty One!

*Troparion 2:*

Pierced in Thy side with a spear, O Life-giver, Thou didst pour forth two fountains of salvation, proclaiming Thee to be One of the Trinity, bearing two energies!

*Martyricon 1:*

A firm foundation and an immovable stone have the valiant passion-bearers found in faith, building themselves up as precious stones!

*Martyricon 2:*

Strengthened by the power of God through faith, ye conquered all the cruel might of the deceiver, O passion-bearing Martyrs [f. 136A] and ye are radiantly crowned!

*Theotokion:*

Having incorruptibly given birth to Christ, thou didst renew our nature which had become corrupt through transgression of old, O all-immaculate Sovereign Lady, protectress of our souls!

*Another Heirmos, the same:*

*Troparion 1:*

Behold, O pure one, the affliction of my humble and passionate soul; behold also the uprising of the bodiless enemies, and deliver me from harm at their hands!

*Troparion 2:*

Leave not my mind to be dragged continually into unruly thoughts by demonic temptations, O Sovereign Lady, but establish it immovably!

*Troparion 3:*

Thy Son was shown eating with sinners for the sake of His great mercy; having been made like unto them, O pure one, have mercy on me also, who have sinned more than all!

*Troparion 4:*

Let me not be rebuked in the wrath of thy Son at the time of His coming, nor let me be condemned in His anger, O all-hymned one, but save me by thy prayers!

*Ode 6. Heirmos: The lowest abyss of sins hath encompassed me, and my spirit faints; but stretching forth Thy high arm, O Master, as Peter do Thou save me, O Helmsman!*

*Troparion 1:*

Through the serpent was man brought forth from Eden, when the deceiver deceived him with enticing food; but Christ, lifted up upon the Tree of His own will, grants unto me again the ancient entrance!

*Troparion 2:*

He who wounded us was wounded, and remained incurable when Thy side was wounded, O Benefactor and Lord;

(see fol. 169)[^188]

...and we the faithful were healed by Thy wounds wherewith Thou wast wounded willingly!

*Martyricon 1:*

Ye appeared in the midst of the fire, O all-wise armor-bearers of Christ, roasted like lambs, and offered for the banquet of God the King of all, inheriting unceasing gladness!

*Martyricon 2:*

Ye pour forth rivers of healings flowing from an inexhaustible fountain, O passion-bearers, drying up the currents of passions and bedewing the gatherings of the faithful!

*Theotokion:*

Beholding the death of the Word Who was voluntarily incarnate from thy blood, O all-hymned one, maternally didst thou cry aloud and magnify Him Who is the author of life, O Virgin Mother and Sovereign Lady!

*Another Heirmos, the same:*

*Troparion 1:*

By the worldly waves to the harm of my mind, I, a wretched one, am continually drowned, and am ever driven by the winds of the enemy; now do I cry unto thee: O Mother of God, help me!

*Troparion 2:*

Grant unto me streams of spiritual tears, O all-immaculate Maiden, that I may wash away the mire of my transgressions, and the tumult of passions, and the defilements of the body!

*Troparion 3:*

I have fallen asleep unto spiritual death, and lie in the grave of despair; but give me thy hand, and raise me up, I pray thee, guiding me unto repentance of life!

[f. 136A] Dry up the muddy rivers of mine evil deeds, O Mother of God, by streams of compunction, and direct me unto waters of relief in the day of judgment!

*Kontakion: Thou Who wast lifted up upon the Cross of Thine own will, O Christ God, grant Thy compassions unto Thy new community that bears Thy name; make glad Thy faithful people by Thy power, granting them victories over their adversaries, having Thine aid as a weapon of peace, an invincible trophy!*

*Ikos:*

He who was caught up unto the third heaven into Paradise, and heard divine and ineffable words which it is not lawful for human tongues to utter—what doth he write to the Galatians, which ye as lovers of the Scriptures have read and understood? God forbid, he says, that I should boast, save only in the Cross of the Lord, whereon having suffered He slew the passions! Having Him therefore, let us firmly hold fast to the Cross of the Lord, the boast of all; for this is our saving Tree, a weapon of peace, an invincible victory!

*Ode 7. Heirmos: Unto the golden image of old, with Persian honor the three children would not bow down, singing to the Lord in the midst of the furnace: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!*

*Troparion 1:*

By Thy sufferings Thou didst make our afflictions to cease, O Lover of mankind, and hast now brought us over unto painless life, as we devoutly bow down before Thy precious passions, O God of all!

*Troparion 2:*

When creation beheld Thee crucified, O Christ, it was transformed and trembled; the earth was shaken, the rocks were split asunder, and the course of the sun hid its light!

*Martyricon 1:*

Obeying the law of Christ, the Martyrs turned away from unlawful...

(see fol. 170)[^189]

...flatteries; and suffering lawfully in the midst of the tribunal, they were gloriously crowned!

*Martyricon 2:*

Ye were not consumed by the fire, having acquired a purpose hotter than fire, O crowned passion-bearers of the Lord, crying aloud: Blessed art Thou, O God!

*Theotokion:*

Standing and beholding Christ lifted up upon the Cross, Whom thou hadst borne, O all-immaculate one, thou didst cry aloud: Leave me not childless, O Thou Who didst preserve me pure even after childbearing!

*Another Heirmos, the same:*

*Troparion 1:*

As the merciful Mother of God, vouchsafe Thy servants mercy, O Sovereign Lady, who in faith cry unto Thy Son: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Troparion 2:*

Blot out the remembrance of my sins and vile deeds, O pure one, that in faith I may cry aloud unto Thy Son: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Troparion 3:*

The choir of martyrs entreats thee, O Mother of God and Sovereign Lady: have mercy upon thy servants who in faith cry unto Thy Son: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Troparion 4:*

At the dread judgment seat of Christ, when I am about to be judged as guilty, appear unto me, O all-immaculate one, delivering me from every torment by thy fervent intercession!

*Ode 8. Heirmos: The Babylonian furnace burned not the Children, nor did the fire of the Godhead harm the Virgin; therefore with the Children we the faithful cry aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and exalt Him above all forever!*

[f. 136A] When Thou wast crucified, Paradise was opened again, and the revolving sword turned its back on us, put to shame by the spear that pierced Thy holy side, O Christ, greatly merciful!

*Troparion 2:*

By Thy spear was the adversary wounded and fell; and Adam who had fallen returned unto life, crying aloud: I honor Thee Who wast slain of Thine own will, O Christ, and I bless and glorify Thee, O greatly merciful One!

*Martyricon 1:*

The world is enlightened by your contests and the courage of your suffering, and by unnumbered marvels it is delivered from heavy afflictions, faithfully crying: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and exalt Him above all forever!

*Martyricon 2:*

He who before boasted that he would consume the earth and the sea is seen as a laughingstock, lying beneath your feet, O holy ones; and Christ adorns you with His life-bearing right hand and with unfading crowns, O ever-glorious ones!

*Theotokion:*

In time thou didst bear Him Who is above all times, Who looses temporal bonds by His own bonds, O all-immaculate one, binding the first-created Adam with the sweet bonds of His love!

*Another Heirmos:*

*Troparion 1:*

Unceasingly, O pure one, show tender mercy unto those who in compunction honor thee, that they may be delivered from the snares of the devil, crying unto Thy Son: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and exalt Him above all forever!

(see fol. 171)[^190]

*Troparion 2:*

When by God's command my soul is about to depart from life, snatch me away from the hunting predators, O Mother of God, as I sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and exalt Him above all forever!

*Troparion 3:*

Dark night holds me fast, for I have been enslaved to dark deeds; but do thou thyself, the gate of the Light, illumine me with thy light, as I sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and exalt Him above all forever!

*Troparion 4:*

Grant thy mercy, O Sovereign Lady, unto the world, and prayer unto thy servant; deliver thy people from impending threat, O all-honorable one, as we cry aloud giving thanks: Glory to thee, O Queen of all!

*Ode 9. Heirmos: Her that was prefigured on Sinai in the bush and fire to Moses the Lawgiver, and who conceived the divine Fire in her womb without being burned—the all-radiant and unquenchable lamp, the holy Mother of God—in hymns unceasingly honoring, we magnify!*

*Troparion 1:*

That Thou mightest restore the others whom Thou hadst lost, O Christ, Thou didst lift up Thy flesh upon the Cross, O Good One, and makest partakers of joy Thine angelic hosts on high, O Life-giver, with whom honoring Thee in hymns, O Benefactor, we magnify Thee!

*Troparion 2:*

As Thou didst stretch forth Thy hands upon the Cross, O Christ, Thou didst take up my weakened hands, vanquished by many passions, by Thy power, and didst strengthen my knees unto true healing; therefore thankfully we magnify Thee!

[f. 136A] In the flame of boundless wounds, Thy valiant and marvelous champions found Thee to be cooling dew, O Christ; therefore rejoicing they ran the race for the desired rewards, unceasingly magnifying Thee in hymns!

*Martyricon 2:*

The multitude of sufferers, the holy choir, entreats Thee, O Christ, for the assembly of the people who provoke Thee much: by the multitude of Thy compassions, O Bountiful One, cleanse the multitude of our iniquities, as the Lover of mankind!

*Theotokion:*

Bearing human likeness, O Virgin, thou didst bear the Radiance of the Father; and when He was lifted up upon the Cross, the sun, enduring not to see it, was darkened, and the gloom of idolatry departed: therefore with Him we magnify thee!

*Another Heirmos: In the shadow of the law and in the scriptures, an image let us the faithful discern...*

*Troparion 1:*

The end draws nigh, O soul, judgment is at the doors; turn away from shameful deeds, take hold of the good life, for thou hast the Mother of God as a helper, delivering thee from every malice!

*Troparion 2:*

Among mental thieves have I fallen, wretched one, and through their wiles have I been stripped of the robe of my virtues to the harm of my mind, and filled with soul-corrupting passions; I cry unto thee: O all-immaculate Sovereign Lady, despise me not!

*Troparion 3:*

My whole life, O all-immaculate one, is consumed in despondency, and through despair I have now drawn nigh unto hades; but grant me amendment...

(see fol. 172)[^191]

...O pure one, and leave me not to be afflicted therein, who flee under thy protection!

*Troparion 4:*

Thou knowest the weakness of my humble body, the wounds of my soul, the sighing of my heart, the wandering and deceit of my mind; therefore by thy tender-heartedness grant healing unto my wandering!

*Exapostilarion of the Cross, on Wednesday:*

The divine Cross, an invincible weapon and confirmation of the faith, do we hymn and bow down before in faith: the great refuge and boast of Christians, the guardian of the Orthodox, the glory of passion-bearers; worthily do we praise Thee!

*Glory... Both now... Staurotheotokion:*

Standing by the Cross, the all-immaculate one, seeing her Son hanging crucified upon the Tree, poor one, wept, shedding tears maternally, and cried aloud: What marvelous wonder is this, that the Creator of creation submits to be nailed fast!

*At the Aposticha, Stichera to the Cross, Tone 3:*

*Sticheron 1:*

The tree of disobedience budded forth death unto the world; but the tree of the Cross sprouted life and incorruption; therefore we pray Thee, the crucified Lord, that the light of Thy countenance may be signed upon us!

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord.*

*Sticheron 2:*

Through longing for pleasure was I cast out, having fallen by a cruel fall; yet despising me not, O Master, for my sake Thou didst take upon Thyself crucifixion like unto mine, and savest me and leadest me into glory, O [f. 136A] my Deliverer, glory to Thee!

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us.*

*Martyricon:*

The hosts of the holy Angels were amazed at the struggles of the martyrs, how, though compassed with flesh, they set at naught the torments, having become imitators of the sufferings of Christ the Savior; and they pray for our souls!

*Glory... Both now... Staurotheotokion:*

When the all-hymned Ewe-lamb, the Virgin Thy Mother, beheld Thee lifted up upon the Cross, O my Christ, weeping with lamentation she cried aloud: O my Son and my God, leave me not childless, Thou Who didst preserve me pure even after childbearing!

*At the Divine Liturgy, the Beatitudes, Tone 3:*

*Troparion 1:*

When forefather Adam cast off Thy commandment, O Christ, Thou didst cast him out of Paradise; but the thief who confessed Thee upon the cross, O Bountiful One, Thou didst settle therein, as he cried: Remember me, O Savior, in Thy Kingdom!

*Troparion 2:*

Crucified for my sake and pierced in Thy side with a spear, O my Jesus, Thou didst pour forth two fountains of salvation; therefore, saved by Thy suffering, O Christ, I hymn and glorify Thy tender compassion: Remember me, O Savior, in Thy Kingdom!

*Troparion 3:*

Thou wast numbered with the transgressors, and didst take away the iniquities of all, O Jesus; and crowned with thorns as King of all, Thou didst cut down from the root the thorny sin of the forefather, O Christ; therefore in faith we now glorify Thy sufferings!

(see fol. 173)[^192]

*Martyricon:*

Having been made like unto the sufferings of Christ, O valiant sufferers, all-praised Martyrs, ye cast down the enemy's deceit by the power of Christ, and obtained eternal glory; pray ye for all of us, O holy ones!

*Glory... Trinity Hymn:*

O Tri-hypostatic Unity and undivided all-effecting Trinity, one essence and power: protect Thy singers from every harm of the enemy, and vouchsafe them the Kingdom, which they that lived well have obtained!

*Both now... Theotokion:*

Beholding Him upon the Cross Whom thou didst incarnate of thy pure blood, grieving she cried aloud, O Virgin Mother of God: What is this, O Child, that the evil assembly hath rendered unto Thee, putting Thee to death, Who art the life and resurrection of all the faithful!

*Prokeimenon, Tone 3 (Song of the Theotokos): My soul doth magnify the Lord. (Luke 1:46) Verse: For He hath regarded the lowliness of His handmaiden. (Luke 1:48)*

*The Epistle of the Holy Apostle Paul to the Philippians, Section 240 (Phil. 2:5–11):*

Brethren: Let this mind be in you, which was also in Christ Jesus: Who, being in the form of God, thought it not robbery to be equal with God: but made Himself of no reputation, and took upon Him the form of a servant, and was made in the likeness of men: and being found in fashion as a man, He humbled Himself, and became obedient unto death, even the death of the Cross. Therefore God also hath highly exalted Him, and given Him a name which is above every name: that at the name of Jesus every knee should bow, of things in heaven, and things in earth, and things under the earth; and that every tongue should [f. 136A] confess that Jesus Christ is Lord, to the glory of God the Father.

*Alleluia, Tone 8 (Tone [N]): Verse: Give ear, O daughter, and see... (Ps. 44:11) Verse: The rich among the people shall entreat thy countenance... (Ps. 44:13)*

*The Gospel according to Luke, Section 54 (Luke 10:38–42, 11:27–28):*

At that time: Jesus entered into a certain village: and a certain woman named Martha received Him into her house. And she had a sister called Mary, who also sat at Jesus' feet, and heard His word. But Martha was cumbered about much serving, and came to Him, and said: Lord, dost Thou not care that my sister hath left me to serve alone? Bid her therefore that she help me. And Jesus answered and said unto her: Martha, Martha, thou art careful and troubled about many things: but one thing is needful: and Mary hath chosen that good part, which shall not be taken away from her. And it came to pass, as He spake these things, a certain woman of the company lifted up her voice, and said unto Him: Blessed is the womb that bare Thee, and the paps which Thou hast sucked. But He said: Yea rather, blessed are they that hear the word of God, and keep it.

*Communion Verse: I will take the cup of salvation, and call upon the name of the Lord. (Ps. 115:4)*

(see fol. 174)[^193]

[An ornamental woodcut headpiece with foliate strapwork and the Christogram monogram 'IXC'.]

## WEDNESDAY EVENING VESPERS (FOR THURSDAY) (TONE 8)

*On Wednesday Evening, at "Lord, I have cried," Stichera to the Apostles, Tone 4.*

*Podoben: "As one valiant among martyrs" (Я҆́кѡ до́бля):*

*Sticheron 1:*

With the most valiant mind making war, O glorious ones, ye set yourselves in array against the evil enemy; having armed yourselves with the whole armor of the Spirit, bravely casting down the strong demonic wiles, ye snatched away the souls of men like spoils; therefore unto ages do we honor you!

*Sticheron 2:*

O Apostles of Christ, cross-wise spreading forth the wings of faith, the divine twelve of Thine Apostles, O Christ, caught all the nations in the net unto the clothing of Thy vesture, and dried up the salty sea of passions; therefore I pray Thee: call me up from the depth of transgressions by their most acceptable supplications!

*Sticheron 3:*

Let the God-chosen, apostolic and all-honorable twelve be praised today with divine hymns: Peter and Paul, James, Luke, John, and Matthew, Thomas, Mark, Simon, and Philip, and the most glorious other Andrew, with Matthias, and divine Bartholomew, together with the rest of the Seventy!

[f. 136A] *Other Stichera to Saint Nicholas, Tone 4:*

*Sticheron 1:*

Having gazed steadfastly upon the height of understanding, and clearly beheld the mysteries of wisdom, by thy teachings hast thou enriched the world, O father; pray unceasingly for us unto Christ God, O Hierarch Nicholas!

*Sticheron 2:*

O man of God and faithful servant, steward of His mysteries and man of spiritual desires, living pillar and inspired icon: as a divine treasure the church of Myra received thee in wonder, and an intercessor for our souls!

*Sticheron 3:*

Flying about the flowers of the Church like a bird of the heavenly nest of the Angels, O thrice-blessed Nicholas, thou ever criest unto God for all of us who are in dire distress and temptations, and deliverest us by thy prayers!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*

Rejoice, O seal of the Prophets and preaching of the God-proclaiming Apostles; for in truth thou, O pure one, beyond mind and word didst give birth to God incarnate, whereby we receive again our primal nobility and enjoy the delight of Paradise; honoring thee in hymns as the acceptable dispenser of grace, enriched through thee, O all-immaculate one, we are accounted worthy of the eternal life of thy Son, Who richly grants great mercy!

(see fol. 175)[^194]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera to the Apostles, Tone 4:*

*Sticheron 1:*

The marvelous choir hast Thou enlightened by the Holy Spirit, O Christ, cleansing our defilements; for their sake have mercy on us!

*Sticheron 2:*

The unlettered disciples did the Holy Spirit reveal as teachers, O Christ God; and by the widespread nets of the nations Thou didst abolish deceit, as the Almighty!

*Martyricon:*

Living sacrifices, rational whole-burnt offerings, O Martyrs of the Lord, perfect creations of God: sheep that know God and are known of God, whose fold cannot be penetrated by wolves: pray ye that we also may be pastured with you beside the water of rest!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*

Having thee, O Mother of God, as our hope and protectress, we fear not the enemy's snares, for thou savest our souls!

*Dismissal Troparion of the Apostles: O holy Apostles, entreat the merciful God to grant remission of transgressions unto our souls...*

*Dismissal Troparion of Saint Nicholas: The rule of faith and icon of meekness, a teacher of temperance did the reality of things show thee forth unto thy flock; therefore by humility thou didst acquire exalted things, by poverty riches: O Hierarch and Father Nicholas, entreat Christ God that our souls be saved!*

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*

We have come to know the Word of the Father, Christ our God, Who was incarnate of thee, O Virgin Mother of God: entreat Him that our souls may be saved!

## THURSDAY MATINS: THE HOLY APOSTLES AND ST. NICHOLAS (TONE 8)

*On Thursday at Matins, after the first Kathisma, Sessional Hymns of the Apostles, Tone 4:*

*Sessional Hymn 1:*

As luminaries throughout the universe hast Thou revealed Thy disciples, O Christ, shining with preaching, and illumining souls in darkness [f. 136A] and having thereby refuted the deceit of idols, by the teachings of piety Thou didst enlighten the world: by their supplications save our souls!

*Glory... Sessional Hymn 2:*

As Moses, having led the people out of bondage through the Red Sea, by Thy right hand didst drown Pharaoh, so by miracles Thy wise disciples parted the sea of cruel godlessness, and guided the people unto Thee, O Word all-lauded, only Lover of mankind!

*Both now... Theotokion:*

A refuge unto those in cruel afflictions, and reconciliation unto God for sinners, O all-immaculate Sovereign Lady: save me from every adversity, and from the malice of men, and from terrible torment, and dishonorable passions, as with undoubting faith I ever call upon thee, O all-hymned Maiden!

*After the second Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 4:*

*Sessional Hymn 1:*

As preachers of the faith, O Savior, Thou hast shown forth Thy disciples unto the world, thereby guiding the nations unto Thy knowledge; for by the rays of the Word they enlightened all, having truly sown the seed of piety: by their prayers save our souls!

*Glory... Sessional Hymn 2:*

Into all the earth is gone out your sound, and made foolish the unwise wisdom of deceit, O glorious Apostles; ye raised up men from the depth of deceit and showed unto all the path of salvation: therefore now worthily do we call you blessed!

(see fol. 176)[^195]

*Martyricon:*

Manfully having endured torments, ye worthily received the unfading crown of Christ's Kingdom; for ye led each one unto ageless life, having endured sufferings in the flesh, O passion-bearers: therefore pray ye that our souls may be shown mercy!

*Both now... Theotokion:*

We magnify thee, O Mother of God, crying aloud: Thou art alone blessed, from whom ineffably Christ our God was born!

*After the third Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 4:*

*Sessional Hymn of the Apostles:*

Rays, like the Sun of righteousness in the comeliness of Christ, to enlighten all the earth, the glorious Apostles with divine lightnings illumine all with unwaning divine light, and ye enlighten those who in faith celebrate your holy memory!

*Glory... Sessional Hymn of Saint Nicholas:*

Quickly anticipate, O Father Nicholas, and save thy servants from the calamities and sorrows that come upon us; for thou hast boldness toward the Creator and God: draw near therefore quickly, and appear as a protection and swift giver unto those who faithfully call upon thee!

*Both now... Theotokion:*

O unabashed hope of those who trust in thee, who alone above nature didst bear in the flesh Christ our God: with the holy Apostles pray Him to grant unto the universe cleansing of transgressions, and unto all of us amendment of life before the end!

[f. 136A] *Canon to the Holy Apostles, Tone 4.*

*Ode 1. Heirmos: The depth of the Red Sea...*

*Troparion 1:*

The divine Teacher's instruments sounding aloud, ever breathing with His divine breathings, sang a song for us that is truly saving, the all-glorious Apostles of Christ!

*Troparion 2:*

Sleeping in the poverty of slothfulness, and fiercely weighed down in soul by deadly sin in truth, make me worthy of visitation, O eyewitness Apostles, make me worthy!

*Troparion 3:*

Ye who with the word of reason adorn the nations, O Apostles, illumine my heart, which is fiercely darkened by irrational deeds, by the grace of the Comforter, O Apostles!

*Theotokion:*

As even after childbirth thou didst remain incorrupt, having given birth beyond word unto Him Who appeared upon earth for our sake, entreat Him diligently to enlighten our souls!

*Another Canon to Saint Nicholas, Tone 2.*

*Ode 1. Heirmos: Horse and rider...*

*Troparion 1:*

In faith and love having drawn nigh unto God, O Father, thou didst fully accomplish His desires; obeying His commands, thou wast holy, O God-bearing Nicholas!

*Troparion 2:*

Having thee as an intercessor in mercies, O blessed one, surrounded by afflictions and sorrows, we flee unto thee:

(see fol. 177)[^196]

...stretch forth a hand that saves us from every distress!

*Troparion 3:*

Christ anointed thee as Hierarch of Myra, perfuming us with the sweet fragrance of marvels; therefore we pray thee: deliver us from the foul stench of sin, O Nicholas!

*Theotokion:*

Of old the choir of Prophets called thee the divine mountain, O Virgin, and the impassable gate; therefore we pray thee: open unto us the doors of repentance as divine!

*Ode 3. Heirmos: Thy Church rejoices in Thee, O Christ...*

*Troparion 1:*

The streams of Thy spiritual rivers, O God, ever savingly make glad Thy city alone!

*Troparion 2:*

Citizens of heaven, fellow-ministers with the ranks of the bodiless hosts, O all-glorious Apostles, deliver us from every affliction!

*Troparion 3:*

Thou Who didst establish Thine intelligible weights, O Christ, establish us by their prayers in the instruction of Thy desires, as Thou art tender-hearted!

*Theotokion:*

Many a time Thy Mother entreats Thee together with the choir of Thy Disciples, O Lord, she that purely gave birth to Thee: grant unto us a fountain of mercies!

*Another Heirmos: Establish...*

*Troparion 1:*

Thou didst blunt the arrows of the evil one, O Venerable One, by the counter-image of thy divine labors; by thy most wise supplications, O great Nicholas, preserve us unharmed from his malice and violence!

[f. 136A] *Troparion 2:*

Having shown forth an angelic life upon earth, now with the saints thou ever standest holily before the Throne of the Trinity, asking remission for our transgressions and temptations, O holy Hierarch Nicholas!

*Troparion 3:*

Drive away all the dark obscurity of my mind by thy light-bearing prayers, O Nicholas; calm the tempest of passions, guide me unto the haven of dispassion, O father, I pray thee, that praising I may glorify thee!

*Theotokion:*

Standing at the right hand of Christ as a Queen, in golden vesture, the Maiden full of divine grace was arrayed in truth: mediate heavenly kingdom for us, O Maiden, mediating by thy prayers!

*Kontakion of Saint Nicholas, Tone 3.*

*Podoben: "Today the Virgin" (Дѣ́ва дне́сь):*

In Myra, O holy Nicholas, thou wast shown forth as a sacrificer: having fulfilled the Gospel of Christ, O venerable one, thou didst lay down thy life for thy people, and didst save the innocent from death; therefore thou wast sanctified as a great initiate of the grace of God!

*Ikos:*

Let us now praise the Hierarch in hymns, the pastor and teacher of the people of Myra, that by his prayers we may be enlightened; for behold, he was shown forth all-immaculate and incorrupt in the Spirit, offering unto Christ an undefiled and pure sacrifice, well-pleasing unto God, as a sacrificer purified both in soul and in body; therefore thou art the true protector and defender...

(see fol. 178)[^197]

...of this Church, as a great initiate of the grace of God!

*Ode 4. Heirmos: Beholding Thee lifted up...*

*Troparion 1:*

Thou didst bring upon the sea Thy chosen horses, O Lover of mankind, troubling the waters of evil faith, and proclaiming unto all Thy true knowledge!

*Troparion 2:*

As stars having enlightened with piety the intelligible firmament of the Church, O glorious Apostles, deliver me from the night of ignorance and transgressions!

*Troparion 3:*

Having appeared as sharpened arrows, O Apostles, extinguish now the enemy's fiery darts of my malice, and establish my thoughts!

*Theotokion:*

Heal my soul, embittered by the venom of the adversary's biting, through effectual cure, by the prayers of her that bare Thee, O Christ, and of Thy holy Apostles!

*Another Heirmos: Having perceived Thy divine [dispensation]...*

*Troparion 1:*

By passing things thou didst purchase the future things; make us also inheritors of them by thy holy prayers, delivering us from all the temptations of life, O Nicholas!

*Troparion 2:*

Having been the Primate of the people of Myra, O Nicholas, perfume all the senses of my heart with myrrh, ever driving away foul-smelling passions from it!

*Troparion 3:*

Having crushed the enemy's horses, [crush] our visible and invisible enemies [f. 136A] and send unto final destruction those who ever rage, O Nicholas, visible and invisible enemies!

*Theotokion:*

O Holy Mother of God, help me who am ever drowned by bodily passions, and continually lie upon the bed of slothfulness and groan!

*Ode 5. Heirmos: Thou, O Lord, my light into the world...*

*Troparion 1:*

O branches of the Vine of Life, blossoming forth clusters of grapes, ye give all to drink of the wine of spiritual gladness, O glorious Apostles!

*Troparion 2:*

Guide me unto the light of God's commandments, O Apostles, who foolishly abide in the darkness of the soul's despondency; deliver me from the future departure of soul-corrupting transgressions, and from passions and perils, O blessed Apostles!

*Theotokion:*

O God of the saved, as Lover of mankind do Thou save, by the supplications of her that ineffably gave birth to Thee, and of all Thy divine Apostles!

*Another Heirmos: Thou Who clothest Thyself with light...*

*Troparion 1:*

Thou didst cast down idol temples, O holy one; thou didst show forth the counsels of the heretics to be of no avail, O Nicholas, and didst snatch away those who were being led unto death!

*Troparion 2:*

Thou didst rise early unto the Lord from childhood, O Venerable One, therefore from celestial illuminations thou wast wholly illumined; therefore I pray thee: dispel the clouds of my soul!

*Troparion 3:*

We pray thee, O Father Nicholas, at this hour be in the midst of all who call upon thee, granting us our petitions unto salvation!

(see fol. 179)[^198]

*Theotokion:*

Incomprehensible to the mind, O full of God's grace, He Who took flesh accepted to be held by thee, delivering men from the sorrows that encompass them!

*Ode 6. Heirmos: Thou Who hast reached unto the end of...*

*Troparion 1:*

God-chosen sheep of the Good Shepherd, scattered throughout the world, ye converted all the savagery of wolves into sheep-like meekness through faith!

*Troparion 2:*

O goodly-fruited trees of the divine Paradise, bring all the barrenness of my wretched soul into the good fruit of a virtuous life, O Apostles!

*Troparion 3:*

Wounded by the weapon of pleasures and struck down, ye who received from Christ the glorious grace to raise the dead: quicken my dead and wretched soul!

*Theotokion:*

Cause the fierce tempest of my soul to cease, O Bountiful God of all, by the supplications of the Mother of God who bare Thee, and of Thine Apostles and Martyrs!

*Another Heirmos: Surging with the tempest...*

*Troparion 1:*

Make the Master merciful by thy prayers, O Nicholas, unto all who honor thee, that He may grant remission of our transgressions!

*Troparion 2:*

Deliver from diseases and worldly stumbling-blocks, and from temptations and sorrows, O Nicholas, those who have acquired thee as an intercessor before the Lord!

[f. 136A] *Troparion 3:*

A supreme physician did Christ the Master show thee forth; therefore heal the infirmities of those who piously draw near unto thee, O Nicholas!

*Theotokion:*

O wedlock-free Mother, pure Mother of God, therefore in faith I pray thee: dispel the despondency of my soul!

*Kontakion of the Apostles, Tone 2:*

The firm and God-proclaimed preachers, the pinnacle of Thy disciples, O Lord, Thou hast received into the enjoyment of Thy good things and rest; for Thou didst accept their labors and death above every whole-offering, Thou Who alone knowest what is in the heart!

*Ikos:*

Make plain my tongue, O my Savior, open wide my mouth and fill it; touch my heart with compunction, that I may follow what I say, and do that which I teach others beforehand; for every one that does and teaches, He says, the same is great. For if I speak and do not, I am become as sounding brass. Therefore grant me to speak what is meet and do what is profitable, O Thou Who alone knowest what is in the heart!

*Ode 7. Heirmos: In the furnace, the children of Abraham...*

*Troparion 1:*

By the power of the all-holy preaching, the guileless Apostles of Christ destroyed the winter of deceit, and enlightened the thoughts of the faithful with the knowledge of God!

*Troparion 2:*

Ever pouring forth fragrant myrrh, O divine Apostles, fill those who flee unto you with the fragrance of spiritual sweet perfume, and drive away foul-smelling passions!

(see fol. 180)[^199]

*Troparion 3:*

Paralyzed as I am by carnal improprieties, O glorious disciples of the incorrupt Word, save me who sing: Blessed art Thou in the temple of Thy glory, O Lord!

*Theotokion:*

Choirs of Angels, choirs of Martyrs and Apostles ever pray Thee, O Word: in Thy manifold tender mercy, O Lover of mankind, have compassion on all for the sake of the Mother of God, as Thou art merciful!

*Another Heirmos: Exalted above all...*

*Troparion 1:*

Having wholly committed thyself unto God, O Nicholas, save me wholly, who wretchedly and ever fall into worldly passions, O God-wise one!

*Troparion 2:*

O most God-radiant beacon, enlighten my mind which is ever darkened by the gloom of passions, and grant that I may walk decorously in life!

*Troparion 3:*

Every deceitful mouth opened against me, O Nicholas, stop by thy prayers, and deliver me from visible and invisible enemies!

*Theotokion:*

From thee did the unwaning Sun shine forth unto us, O all-radiant Cloud, Christ our God, enlightening those who were in the darkness of ignorance, O Mother of God!

*Ode 8. Heirmos: Daniel, stretching forth his hands...*

*Troparion 1:*

O fire-breathing mouths of Christ, ye stopped shameless mouths and scattered the saving preaching everywhere [f. 136A] from the mouth of the intelligible wolf deliver those who cry: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and supremely exalt Him!

*Troparion 2:*

Shake off the slumber of my deadened soul, O sweet-sounding trumpets of Christ, glorious Apostles; and from the grave of despair and cruel despondency raise her up, that she may sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

*Troparion 3:*

Despise me not, who by perversion of mind have trampled upon Thy laws, O Christ—me, O Master, the prodigal, who have slipped into passions and continually work for evil habits: despise me not, by the supplications of Thy disciples!

*Theotokion:*

O Mary, Sovereign Lady of all, deliver me by thy prayers from being held captive by the serpent and continually sinning, and enslave me unto Christ in a pure life, that I may cry: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

*Another Heirmos: Unto Thee, the Creator in the furnace...*

*Troparion 1:*

Having been the great Primate in Myra, O Nicholas, perfume the senses of my soul with myrrh, that I may escape the foul stench of passions, and receive the grace of the Comforter!

*Troparion 2:*

By the streams of thy holy tongue, O most wise one, thou didst stop the blasphemous streams of Arius; therefore I cry unto thee:

(see fol. 181)[^200]

...dry up the torrents of my passions by thy prayers, O all-blessed Nicholas!

*Troparion 3:*

Deliver us from transgressions by thy prayers, and from demonic malice, and from barbarian captivity, and from all tribulations and evil harm, that we may praise thee as our deliverer!

*Trinity Hymn:*

Unceasingly in faith glorifying the Monarchical Trinity, let us cry aloud: O Father, Word, and all-holy Spirit, we praise Thee in hymn unto all ages!

*Theotokion:*

For our sake God was clothed in flesh from thee, and showed thee forth, O pure and all-immaculate Virgin, as the divine protectress of our whole race; therefore we the faithful with loud voice sing unto thee!

*Ode 9. Heirmos: A stone cut without hands...*

*Troparion 1:*

As chosen stones of the Corner-stone that was laid at the head of the corner, ye built up the hearts of all the faithful, having overturned the enemy's buildings with the stone of faith, O Apostles!

*Troparion 2:*

Having received power from Christ to loose and to bind, loose the bonds of mine evils, and bind me unto the love of God, and make me a partaker of the divine Kingdom, O Apostles!

*Troparion 3:*

O divine clouds of the Master, with divine showers water now my heart that has been dried up by every wicked deed, and show it forth fruitful, O God-blessed Apostles!

[f. 136A] *Theotokion:*

Together with the holy Angels, with the divine Apostles, and with the glorious Martyrs, pray Thy Son and God, O all-immaculate Mother of God, to deliver our souls from afflictions!

*Another Heirmos: Rejoice, O Isaiah...*

*Troparion 1:*

Having become a divine Hierarch, thou didst keep all the commandments of Christ, therefore thou didst become a divine guardian of the faithful; from every calamity and affliction protect us, O Father Nicholas!

*Troparion 2:*

As of old in time of famine thou didst nourish thy city in scarcity of bread, as a good and venerable pastor, so also nourish my soul with spiritual bread, O Father Nicholas, who have thee as an intercessor!

*Troparion 3:*

Thee, the great sun placed on high, ever of the Church of Christ, O venerable pastor, we faithfully pray: by the light-bearing rays of light drive away the deep darkness of sin from our souls!

*Troparion 4:*

The most excellent day of Christ's coming has drawn near, as it is written: strive, O barren soul, cast off slothfulness, and earnestly cry unto Christ: Save me, O Lord, by the prayers of Nicholas!

*Theotokion:*

A radiant lampstand did the Prophet foresee thee to be, O all-immaculate one, bearing Christ the spiritual candle, whereby we who lay in the darkness of evils are enlightened, calling thee blessed, O Mother of God, ever-Virgin!

(see fol. 182)[^201]

*Exapostilarion of the Apostles:*

Peter and Paul, all of us, Andrew, James, Bartholomew, and Matthew, James, and Philip, Thomas, and Simon, Luke, John, and Mark: let us faithfully praise the Apostles of the Lord!

*Glory... Of Saint Nicholas:*

O holy twelve, glory of the Apostles: with Nicholas the venerable Hierarch of Myra, unceasingly pray our Savior!

*Both now... Theotokion:*

O unwedded Virgin: unceasingly pray Christ together with the wise Apostles, and with the multitude of Martyrs, and with wise Nicholas, to deliver from every calamity those who have set their hope upon thee, O Maiden!

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Apostles, Tone 4:*

*Sticheron 1:*

The apostolic choir hast Thou enlightened by the Holy Spirit, O Christ; for their sake wash away our sinful defilement, O God, and have mercy on us!

*Sticheron 2:*

The unlettered disciples did the Holy Spirit reveal as teachers, O Christ God; and by their eloquent sweet-sounding speech Thou didst abolish the deceit of the nations, as Thou art almighty!

*Martyricon:*

Precious is the death of Thy saints, O Lord: for by sword and fire, and with a contrite soul, they poured forth their blood, having hope in Thee to receive the reward of their labors; and having endured, they received from Thee great mercy, O Savior!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*

Thee have we acquired as a rampart, O all-immaculate Mother of God, and a calm haven [f. 136A] and a confirmation; therefore I pray thee: being tossed about by the waves of life, guide me and save me!

*At the Divine Liturgy, the Beatitudes, Tone 4:*

*Troparion 1:*

Through the tree was Adam exiled from Paradise, but through the tree of the Cross the thief dwelt in Paradise: for the one, having tasted of it, cast aside the commandment of Him that made him; but the other, being crucified with Him, confessed Thee, the hidden God: Remember me, he cried, in Thy Kingdom!

*Troparion 2:*

Being nurslings of the Shepherd and Lamb, spiritual lambs, ye were sent into the midst of wolves as wise lambs, O Apostles, by your divine preaching converting their savagery into meekness, crying in faith with unswerving purpose: Remember us in Thy Kingdom!

*Troparion 3:*

Having traversed the whole breadth of the earth as radiant stars, O Apostles of the Lord, ye destroyed the deceit of the darkened, and shone forth the saving light upon the deceived; therefore we call you blessed, O preachers of Christ, asking: Pray for us ever unto the Lord!

*Martyricon:*

Slain by the world and burned with material fire, ye burned up the bitter matter of polytheism, O God-blessed ones, and now ye pour forth streams of healings unto those who come unto you with fervent faith, calling upon you and crying unto Christ:

(see fol. 183)[^202]

...Remember us in Thy Kingdom!

*Glory... Trinity Hymn:*

With unoffending thoughts, being sober in mind, let us speak unto Him Who sits on high together with the Father and the Spirit, the undivided Trinity, Who by His Word established all things, and enlightens all that call upon Thee in faith: Remember us also in Thy Kingdom!

*Both now... Theotokion:*

The rejoicing of the Apostles, and the unfading crown of the passion-bearers art thou in truth, O all-immaculate Virgin Mother of God: with them ask for us, O Sovereign Lady, deliverance from transgressions and amendment of life, who in faith ask thee and faithfully cry: Rejoice, O all-true treasury of good things!

*Prokeimenon, Tone 4: Their sound is gone out into all the earth, and their words unto the ends of the world. (Ps. 18:4) Verse: The heavens declare the glory of God. (Ps. 18:1)*

*The Epistle of the Holy Apostle Paul to the Corinthians, Section 131 (1 Cor. 4:9–16):*

Brethren: God has set forth us the apostles last, as it were appointed to death: for we are made a spectacle unto the world, and to angels, and to men. We are fools for Christ's sake, but ye are wise in Christ; we are weak, but ye are strong; ye are honorable, but we are despised. Even unto this present hour we both hunger, and thirst, and are naked, and are buffeted, and have no certain dwellingplace; and labor, working with our own hands: being reviled, we bless; being persecuted, we suffer it; being defamed, we entreat: we are made as the filth of the world, and are the offscouring of all things unto this day. I write not these things to shame [f. 136A] you, but as my beloved sons I warn you. For though ye have ten thousand instructors in Christ, yet have ye not many fathers: for in Christ Jesus I have begotten you through the Gospel. Therefore I beseech you, be ye followers of me, even as I also am of Christ.

*Alleluia, Tone 4.*

*The Gospel according to Matthew, Section 34 (Matt. 9:36–10:8):*

At that time: when Jesus saw the multitudes, He was moved with compassion on them, because they fainted, and were scattered abroad, as sheep having no shepherd. Then said He unto His disciples: The harvest truly is plenteous, but the laborers are few; pray ye therefore the Lord of the harvest, that He will send forth laborers into His harvest. And when He had called unto Him His twelve disciples, He gave them power against unclean spirits, to cast them out, and to heal all manner of sickness and all manner of disease. Now the names of the twelve apostles are these: The first, Simon, who is called Peter, and Andrew his brother; James the son of Zebedee, and John his brother; Philip, and Bartholomew; Thomas, and Matthew the publican; James the son of Alphaeus, and Lebbaeus, whose surname was Thaddaeus; Simon the Canaanite, and Judas Iscariot, who also betrayed Him. These twelve Jesus sent forth, and commanded them, saying: Go not into the way of the Gentiles, and into any city of the Samaritans enter ye not: but go rather to the lost sheep of the house of Israel. And as ye go, preach, saying: The Kingdom of heaven is at hand. Heal the sick, cleanse the lepers, raise the dead, cast out devils: freely ye have received, freely give.

*Communion Verse: Their sound is gone out into all the earth, and their words unto the ends of the world. (Ps. 18:4)*

(see fol. 184)[^203]

[An ornamental woodcut headpiece with foliate strapwork and central floral emblem.]

## THURSDAY EVENING VESPERS (FOR FRIDAY) (TONE 8)

*On Thursday Evening, at "Lord, I have cried," Stichera of the Cross, Tone 6:*

*Sticheron 1:*

The forefather, tripped up because of the tree, fell, and received cruel condemnation; but Christ, nailed upon the Tree, loosed it: He renewed the crushed one, and taking him upon His shoulders, made him enthroned together with Himself and the Father with the honorable Spirit!

*Sticheron 2:*

Grant me a thought of compunction, O my Christ, that in waking and in heart I may ever bear the precious sufferings which Thou didst endure in the flesh for our sake, receiving tears and weeping over them, thinking always to stand by Thy Cross, and to behold Thee taken up upon it like a Lamb!

*Sticheron 3:*

The earth was terrified, the air was darkened, beholding Christ God insulted by a servant—He Who dissolves mountains with a rebuke, and by His look alone empties the abysses, closing His eyes upon the Tree, Who with His breath holds everything in the palm of His hand, becoming breathless and dead in the flesh!

*Other Stichera of the Mother of God, Tone 6:*

*Sticheron 1:*

Beholding the Lamb stretched out upon the Tree, between two thieves, the undefiled Ewe-lamb and Virgin: Woe is me [f. 136A] cried she, O my sweetest Child! What strange and most glorious mystery is this? How has the lawless assembly lifted Thee up upon the Cross and given Thee gall to drink, Who didst satisfy men with manna?

*Sticheron 2:*

When the Virgin and Thy Mother beheld Thee nailed upon the Cross, O my Christ, by the judgment of the lawless Jews at the Place of the Skull, she cried aloud: Woe is me, O my most beloved Son! What strange sight is this? How do the senseless children of the Hebrews nail Thee, the Lord of all, unto the Cross?

*Sticheron 3:*

All of us have come to know thee alone as all-holy Sovereign Lady, shown forth as a Virgin after childbirth; seeing Him Whom thou didst bear without seed willingly stretch forth His hands nailed upon the Tree, the all-pure one wept and cried aloud: Willingly, O Long-suffering One, dost Thou die, delivering from death all that hymn Thee!

*Glory... Both now... Staurotheotokion:*

O all-holy Sovereign Lady, Virgin who didst bear the Lord, Who for His tender-heartedness was nailed upon the Cross and poured forth streams of life for the world: pray Him that our souls may be saved; for thee alone, we the faithful have as a refuge, rampart, and protection: therefore do we flee under thy Protection!

*Prokeimenon: My help comes from the Lord, Who made heaven and earth. (Ps. 120:2) Verse: I have lifted up mine eyes unto the hills, from whence comes my help. (Ps. 120:1)*

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 6:*

*Sticheron 1:*

(see fol. 185)[^204]

Thy Cross, O Lord, is life and protection unto Thy people; and trusting in it, we hymn Thee Who wast crucified in the flesh, our God: have mercy on us!

*Sticheron 2:*

Thy Cross, O Lord, has opened Paradise unto the human race; and having been delivered from corruption, we hymn Thee Who wast crucified in the flesh, our God: have mercy on us!

*Martyricon:*

Thy Martyrs, O Lord, denied Thee not, nor did they depart from Thy commandments: by their prayers have mercy on us!

*Glory... Both now... Staurotheotokion:*

Beholding Thee crucified, O Christ, she that bare Thee cried aloud: What strange mystery is this that I behold, O my Son? How dost Thou die upon the Tree, suspended in the flesh, O Giver of life?

## FRIDAY MATINS: THE PRECIOUS CROSS AND THE THEOTOKOS (TONE 8)

*On Friday at Matins, after the first Kathisma, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 6:*

*Sessional Hymn 1:*

Thy Cross, O Lord, has been sanctified, for thereby healings are granted unto those who are sick in sins; therefore we fall down before Thee: have mercy on us!

*Sessional Hymn 2:*

No sooner was the wood of Thy Cross set up, O Christ, than the foundations of death were shaken, O Lord: for him whom Hades swallowed with longing, it released with trembling; Thou hast shown forth Thy holy salvation, and we glorify Thee, O Son of God: have mercy on us!

[f. 136A] Together with the virgin disciple, the Virgin Mother, standing by the Cross of Him Who came forth from her, unable to bear seeing Him lifted up on the Cross, cried aloud, held by weenings: How dost Thou suffer, Who art by nature impassible? I glorify Thine ineffable goodness, O my Son, that surpasses every mind!

*After the second Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 6:*

*Sessional Hymn 1:*

Today the prophetic word is fulfilled: for behold, we worship at the place where Thy feet stood, O Lord; and having tasted of the tree of salvation, we have obtained freedom from sinful passions, by the prayers of the Mother of God, O Thou Who alone art the Lover of mankind!

*Sessional Hymn 2:*

O Lord, the Jews condemned Thee unto death, the Life of all: they that passed through the Red Sea with a rod brought Thee unto the Cross; and they that sucked honey from the rock brought Thee gall. But Thou didst endure it willingly, that Thou mightest free us from the slavery of the enemy. O Lord, glory to Thee!

*Martyricon:*

Unto the righteous Thou art ever a light, O Lord; for Thy saints, being enlightened through Thee, shine ever as luminaries, having quenched the lamps of the ungodly: by their prayers enlighten my lamps, O our Savior, and save me!

*Glory... Both now... Staurotheotokion:*

O holy, all-immaculate Virgin, glory of Angels: when thou didst stand by the Cross of thy Son and God, not bearing to behold the reproach of the enemies, thou didst cry aloud, weeping maternally: How dost Thou...

(see fol. 186)[^205]

...endure all threatenings, O Lover of mankind! I glorify Thy long-suffering.

*After the third Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 6:*

*Sessional Hymn 1:*

Upon the wood of the Cross, for the world's sake, O Word, Thou wast lifted up; when Thy side was pierced with a spear, Thou didst tear up the cruel handwriting of sins, as almighty God; therefore we piously praise Thine ineffable dispensation, O Word. Glory.

*Sessional Hymn to the Cross:*

O Cross, dispeller of demons, physician of the sick, strength and protector of the faithful, victory of kings, praise of the Orthodox in truth, confirmation of the Church of Christ: be unto us a confirmation, and rampart, and protector, O blessed Wood! Both now.

*Staurotheotokion:*

O sole immaculate Ewe-lamb, Virgin Mother of God: beholding Him Who took flesh from thy pure blood hanging willingly upon the Cross, the Maiden cried aloud with bitter tears: O my guileless Son, I praise Thy dispassionate dispensation!

*Canon to the Precious and Life-creating Cross, Ode 1, Tone 6. Heirmos:*

With a wave of the sea He covered of old the pursuing tyrant; under the earth the saved children hid Him: but let us, like the maidens, sing unto the Lord; for gloriously has He been glorified.

*Troparion 1:*

By Thy precious Passion Thou hast honored the nature of man which had become dishonored; therefore, reverencing Thee with fear, we magnify Thy dominion, and faithfully glorify Thee; for gloriously has He been glorified.

[f. 136A] *Troparion 2:*

By Thy blood, O Word, Thou didst stop the shedding of unrighteous blood, and didst cleanse from evil defilements the nature of mankind, O Almighty One; therefore, being saved, we glorify Thy dominion.

*Martyricon 1:*

The praiseworthy resistance of the martyrs truly conquered blood-eating beasts, and subdued the fiery nature through the bedewing of divine grace, and stopped the raging tempest of polytheism.

*Martyricon 2:*

Having dried up the rain of deceit by showers of blood, ye pour forth showers of healings unto the world, and drive away the kindling of passions, O divine martyrs; therefore are ye called blessed.

*Staurotheotokion:*

Having no form or comeliness in Thy suffering, Thou Who art fair in comeliness: the unwedded Maiden, the adornment of the faithful, looking upon Thee, cried out maternally, and with love glorified Thee.

*Another Canon to the Mother of God, Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos: Unto God Who shook off Pharaoh...*

*Troparion 1:*

Him that had fallen into corruption through disobedience, the forefather Adam, thou hast renewed, O all-immaculate one, having borne God in the flesh.

*Troparion 2:*

With joy I cry unto thee the voice of Gabriel, O pure one: Rejoice, all hope of the all-immaculate; Rejoice, O Bride of God!

*Troparion 3:*

My deadened mind do thou quicken, O all-immaculate Virgin, through the operation of life, Him Who was truly born of thee!

*Troparion 4:*

They that bless thee in faith are manifestly...

(see fol. 187)[^206]

...blessed of God, for thou hast borne the fountain of blessing, O only all-hymned one!

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

Seeing Thee hanging at the Place of the Skull, Who without restraint didst hang the whole earth upon the waters, creation was seized with trembling, crying: There is none holy save Thee, O Lord, Lover of mankind!

*Troparion 1:*

Seeing Thee Who didst hang the whole earth without restraint upon the waters lifted up upon the Tree and pierced in the side, the sun was darkened, knowing Thee to be the Enlightenment of all.

*Troparion 2:*

By Thy nails, O Long-suffering One, the evil one who of old in Paradise wounded Adam is wounded; and being struck down, he remains unhealed unto the ages; but we the faithful have found healing for all wounds.

*Martyricon 1:*

When the angelic choir beheld the assembly of martyrs being slain for the sake of the slaughtered Lamb, they marveled how material beings conquered immaterial enemies, receiving the crowns of victory.

*Martyricon 2:*

Clothed with strength, the saints, by the power of Him Who alone strengthens all things, wrestled with the enemy and trampled down his weak power, receiving crowns from God.

*Staurotheotokion:*

When she that bare Thee beheld Him Who was born of thee wounded by the spear, she was wounded in heart, O all-immaculate, all-pure one; and marveling [f. 136A] she said: What has the lawless assembly rendered unto Thee, O Child?

*Another Heirmos: Established in the faith of Christ...*

*Troparion 1:*

As a figure, God revealed thee unto the lawgiver upon the mountain, O Virgin, as a tabernacle and an ark; for thou wast an abode sanctifying all.

*Troparion 2:*

All of us have recognized thee as holy earth, O Virgin, which without seed put forth the fair Ear of grain, Jesus Christ, Who nourishes those who in faith and love call thee blessed.

*Troparion 3:*

The goings of the Most High God were seen in thee, O Maiden, when He was ineffably incarnate; for thou hast become Mother, O Sovereign Lady, of Him Who reigns over all.

*Troparion 4:*

Guided by the prophets, the Church of Christ glorifies thee as Mother of God; for thou, O Virgin, hast borne the incarnate God, the Savior, who hymn thee.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

Foreseeing Thy divine emptying upon the Cross, Habakkuk cried out in awe: Thou didst break the power of the mighty, O Good One, joining Thyself unto those in Hades, as Almighty!

*Troparion 1:*

Stretching out Thy hands, O Lord, taking away the intemperance of stretching out the hand unto the eating of the tree: dead upon the Tree Thou wast sacrificed, willing to save; and from Thy side Thou dost drop forgiveness unto the prisoners.

*Troparion 2:*

Thou didst deign to suffer, O Most Good One, that Thou mightest loose us from unreasonable passions; Thou didst taste gall, pouring forth divine sweetness unto me, being put to death and giving life unto me, O Master.

(see fol. 188)[^207]

*Martyricon 1:*

Bearing the fire of divine love in their rational soul, they quenched the flame of the lawless, and Christ's passion-bearers were shown forth as illuminating candles.

*Martyricon 2:*

The divine friends of the King of all disburdened themselves of carnal love, and rushed steadfastly into every torture; and having conquered, they were crowned with glory.

*Staurotheotokion:*

Without passions did she bear Thee, and how do I see Thee now partaking of passions? How dost Thou endure this? cried the Virgin: O unoriginate Son, I glorify Thy long-suffering!

*Another Heirmos: He heard Thy report and was afraid...*

*Troparion 1:*

Habakkuk heard of thee, O all-immaculate one, as a mountain overshadowed by the Spirit, being enlightened; and from thee God was manifested incarnate.

*Troparion 2:*

The Orthodox assembly is set upright, hymning thee as the Ark, O Mother of God, crying unto thee with the Archangel: Rejoice!

*Troparion 3:*

Silenced is the countenance of Nestorius, who would not profess thee to be the pure Mother of God; for in truth, O all-immaculate one, thou hast borne God.

*Troparion 4:*

Finding thee fair and radiant in virtues among women, He made His abode in thee, and made thee the all-pure Mother of God.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Unto Thee I rise early, Who for Thy tender-heartedness didst empty Thyself [f. 136A] without mutation, and even unto death didst bow Thyself impassibly, O Word of God: grant peace unto me, O Lover of mankind.

*Troparion 1:*

Dead wast Thou seen at the Place of the Skull, Thou Who didst slay Hades by the passion of Thy flesh; Thou hadst neither form nor comeliness to behold, O Christ, willing to adorn me, as Thou art the Lover of mankind.

*Troparion 2:*

Willing by nails to heal the passionate changes of the first Adam, Thou art nailed with nails and pierced with a spear, O Christ, forbidding the fiery sword to forbid Thine entrance unto Thy servants.

*Martyricon 1:*

Following the footsteps of the Master's Passion, the choirs of martyrs were nailed upon the tree, and their hands were taken together, and their feet were divinely guided unto the paths of honorable martyrdom.

*Martyricon 2:*

By a perfect mind ye cast down utterly the earthly pride of the haughty mind, being humbled in likeness unto Him Who exalted all, through humility of heart, O martyrs of Christ.

*Theotokion:*

From the root of Jesse dost thou bud forth, O Virgin, and as a flower dost thou bring the Planter unto the world, having dried up the shoots of godlessness by the divine garden of the Cross, which He received in the flesh.

*Another Heirmos: My spirit rises early unto Thee...*

(see fol. 189)[^208]

*Troparion 1:*

Having formed in thee, O Virgin, the desirable comeliness of thy godlike beauty and sweetest love, the Master poured Himself into thee.

*Troparion 2:*

All-holy and light-bearing gate of grace: having illumined the universe with light brighter than the sun, O Maiden, enlighten those who sing of thee.

*Troparion 3:*

May they that confess thee to be the Mother of God, O Sovereign Lady and Mother, be accounted worthy through thee, O Mother of God, to obtain the unpassing Kingdom and delight.

*Troparion 4:*

Thou wast revealed as a temple of the All-seeing and Almighty One; for finding thy womb more honorable than the heavens, He took up His dwelling therein, O all-pure Mother of God.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

Jonah was caught, but not held, in the belly of the whale; for bearing the image of Thee Who didst suffer and wast given over unto burial, he came forth from the beast as from a bridal chamber, and called unto the watch: Ye that keep vanities and lies have forsaken your own mercy.

*Troparion 1:*

Changing his hands of old, Jacob foreshadowed the Cross when blessing the children; therefore, O Most High, ascending thereon, Thou dost loose mankind from the ancient curse, and dost now pour forth blessing upon those who bless Thee, O Thou Who alone art blessed, Healer and Most Good.

*Troparion 2:*

Foreshadowing Thy Passion, O Word, great Moses of old lifted up the brazen serpent, delivering those who looked upon it from the bite of the venomous serpent; for being crucified, O Master [f. 136A] when Thou wast crucified, all we the faithful were delivered from the harm of the God-hated serpent.

*Martyricon 1:*

As others suffered of old, so the divine and honorable sufferers were suffering, looking toward eternal rewards and rejoicings: by their prayers, O Bountiful Christ, deliver those who sing of Thee from temptations, and from sins and calamities.

*Martyricon 2:*

The honorable and divine passion-bearers stopped the torrents of deceit by the shedding of their blood, and truly became fountains for the faithful, pouring forth waters of the knowledge of God: by their prayers, O Savior, shower upon the world forgiveness for all, life, and cleansing, and great mercy.

*Staurotheotokion:*

Beholding the Chief Shepherd of all and Master lifted up upon the Tree, the Ewe-lamb, weeping maternally, cried aloud: What is this new mystery, O Child? How dost Thou, Who art immortal by essence, accept death, willing to deliver men from corruption?

*Another Heirmos: Jonah cried from the belly of Hades...*

*Troparion 1:*

Thou hast borne Him Who lived as the God-man among men, O Virgin Mother of God, the only Benefactor, Who grants being unto men.

*Troparion 2:*

Emmanuel, two-fold in nature, hast thou borne, O Mother of God, the pre-eternal, perfect Word of God incarnate, Who grants redemption unto us.

*Troparion 3:*

The ark that received the tables of the Law prefigured thee, O pure one, who didst receive in thy womb the Word of God incarnate for our sake.

*Troparion 4:*

All angelic tongues cannot declare thy praises...

(see fol. 190)[^209]

...O pure one; yet we now, receiving it as servants, offer the "Rejoice" of Gabriel.

*The Kontakion and Ikos are written on Wednesday.*

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

Ineffable wonder! He Who delivered the righteous youths from the flame in the furnace is laid in the tomb dead, without breath, unto our salvation who sing: O God our Redeemer, blessed art Thou!

*Troparion 1:*

When the Hebrew assembly condemned Thee upon the Cross, the earth trembled beholding it, and the sun hid its light, and they that were in darkness saw light, singing: O God our Redeemer, blessed art Thou!

*Troparion 2:*

Thou didst endure insult, being struck with a reed, O Savior, and crowned with thorns as King, willingly, O Christ God, unto our salvation who sing: O God our Redeemer, blessed art Thou!

*Martyricon 1:*

Blessed are ye, O most praiseworthy martyrs, visibly destroying all the devil's malice; ye endure tortures and an unjust death, desiring life on high, and crying: O God our Redeemer, blessed art Thou!

*Martyricon 2:*

Ye stood in the midst of the flame, O most praiseworthy martyrs, visibly burning up deceit and remaining unconsumed by the bedewing of divine grace, crying aloud and singing: O God our Redeemer, blessed art Thou!

*Staurotheotokion:*

Thou art crucified willingly, O unoriginate Son, cried the Mother of God beholding Thee; in sorrow is my soul now submerged, Thou diest [f. 136A] giving life unto those who sing: O God our Redeemer, blessed art Thou!

*Another Heirmos: The youths in the fiery furnace...*

*Troparion 1:*

Gratefully we offer unto thee the "Rejoice", having truly been delivered through thee, O Mother of God, from every distress, and crying in unison: in gladness and joy we magnify thee, O God of our fathers!

*Troparion 2:*

David the forefather wrote of thy glory, O Virgin, and prophesied that thou art manifestly the Queen of creations, standing at the right hand, O all-immaculate one, of the God of our fathers.

*Troparion 3:*

Thou hast renewed the earthly nature, O pure one, by the renewal of thy childbirth; therefore do we cry unto thee: Blessed unto all ages, O Lady, is the fruit of thy womb!

*Troparion 4:*

Adam who had been condemned and held by death, the second Adam, O all-immaculate one, brings up, having had mercy on him through thee, crying: Blessed art Thou, Who wast born and hast renewed me!

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

Be astonished, trembling, O heaven, and let the foundations of the earth be shaken; for behold, He that dwells in the highest is numbered among the dead, and is strangely received in a narrow tomb: Whom ye children bless, ye priests praise, ye people supremely exalt unto all ages!

*Troparion 1:*

Thou drinkest the cup, O Christ, which Thou hast prepared by Thy Passion on the Cross, pouring forth streams of forgiveness from Thy life-creating side unto those who died on account of the rib, singing unto Thee and crying: Whom ye children...

(see fol. 191)[^210]

...bless, ye priests praise, ye people supremely exalt unto all ages.

*Troparion 2:*

When the lawless people condemned Thee to be crucified, O Jesus, in the midst of the lawless, O Life-giving Lord, the earth feared, and all sang in fear: Ye children bless, ye priests praise, ye people supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

*Martyricon 1:*

Precious before God was your death shown to be, O sufferers; for partaking of many sufferings, ye were wisely shown forth as partakers of the honorable Passion, crying: Ye children bless, ye priests praise, ye people supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

*Martyricon 2:*

In truth non-carnal were your weapons against the array of the enemies, O martyrs: hope, faith, and truth, whereby having attained God, ye were joined unto the choirs of the chosen, crying unto the Master: Ye children bless, ye priests praise, ye people supremely exalt Him unto all ages!

*Staurotheotokion:*

O Most High, unoriginate Son, Thou endurest spittings and reproaches and the Cross, being struck mocking-fashion with a reed, cried the Mother of God: I glorify Thy long-suffering, Whom ye children bless, ye priests praise, ye people supremely exalt unto all ages!

*Another Heirmos: The only unoriginate King of glory...*

*Troparion 1:*

The incomprehensible Master of all, Whom the heavens cannot contain, O Mother of God, is contained within thy womb, O Virgin; therefore we the faithful supremely exalt thee with love unto all ages.

[f. 136A] *Troparion 2:*

Him Who in mind is higher than all creation, O Maiden, Whom created nature is not able to behold, thou didst bear in thy pure hands; therefore we the faithful supremely exalt thee with love unto all ages.

*Troparion 3:*

Pray God Whom thou hast borne, O Virgin, to send down salvation unto those who faithfully honor thee, and deliverance from calamities, and speedy alleviation of cruel diseases, and eternal grace.

*Troparion 4:*

Knowing thee, O all-holy Virgin, to be adorned with various virtues and enlightened as a temple of the Most High, we piously sing of thee and glorify thee unto all ages.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*

Lament Me not, O Mother, beholding in the tomb the Son Whom thou didst conceive in the flesh without seed; for I will arise and will be glorified, and will lift up in glory without ceasing, as God, those who with faith and love magnify thee.

*Troparion 1:*

To deliver from corruption him that had decayed through the sweet eating of the tree, O only Savior: as Thou art good, Thou didst deign to be crucified in the flesh and to die; we unceasingly hymn Thy great tender-heartedness and Thy power, O Jesus.

*Troparion 2:*

O how didst Thou endure pains, stretched out upon the Cross, delivering us from cruel pangs, O Master Christ! How, being crowned with thorns, didst Thou root out all passionate briars; how, being given vinegar and gall to drink, didst Thou pour out the cup of gladness for us!

(see fol. 192)[^211]

*Martyricon 1:*

Though distressed, all of you, and delivered unto prisons, and cruelly severed by judgments, O sufferers, and harshly racked upon the wheel, and given as food to be devoured by wild beasts: ye denied not Christ, the Master of all.

*Martyricon 2:*

Brighter than the sun's beams shines the most radiant and celebrated memory of the sufferers, and it ever enlightens the souls of the pious, driving away the gloom of passions and calamities, and destroying the deep demonic darkness.

*Staurotheotokion:*

Thou bearest Him Who bears all things, holding the Infant in thine arms Who delivers us from the hand of the battling enemy, O all-immaculate Sovereign Lady; and thou beholdest Him lifted up upon the Wood of the Cross, Who raised us up from the pit of malice.

*Another Heirmos: Hymned one, higher than the heavens, who...*

*Troparion 1:*

All-perfectly was human nature received from thee, O all-immaculate Maiden, being united unto the Word, flesh ensouled and adorned with a rational soul; therefore we the faithful magnify thee.

*Troparion 2:*

Let the eloquence of rhetors be silenced, but let the divine apostolic trumpet sound forth, praising thee with true voices, O Virgin, and confessing thee to be the true Mother of God.

*Troparion 3:*

Mankind has now found mercy through thee: being united in truth hypostatically unto the Word, and by creation becoming God, O Virgin; therefore do we all unceasingly magnify thee.

[f. 136A] *Troparion 4:*

Foreknown by the sharp-sightedness of the prophets was the mystery of thy childbirth, O Maiden: for thou hast borne the incarnate God unto men, Who delivers us from tribulations, by thy prayers.

*Exapostilarion of the Cross:*

Rejoice, O precious Cross, invincible wall of the world, refuge of the sorrowing and protector of those in needs, victory of kings, preservation of warriors, overcoming of barbarians, crown of the diadem, and salvation of the faithful! The Cross is the guardian of the whole universe, the Cross is the beauty of the Church, the Cross is the might of kings, the Cross is the confirmation of the faithful, the Cross is the glory of Angels, the Cross is the ruin of demons!

*Glory... Both now... Staurotheotokion:*

Beholding Thee nailed upon the Cross, the all-immaculate one, weeping and lamenting, cried aloud: How hast Thou set, O Sun of glory, O Primal Light? How art Thou nailed upon the Cross and pierced in the side with a spear, O my Son and my God, for the sake of man's salvation?

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 6:*

*Sticheron 1:*

Having hope in the Cross and boasting in it, I cry aloud: O Lord, Lover of mankind, cast down the pride of those who do not confess Thee to be God and man!

*Sticheron 2:*

Protected by the Cross, we withstand the enemy, not fearing his wiles and cunning; for the proud one has been brought to naught and trampled down by the power of Christ Who was nailed upon the Tree.

(see fol. 193)[^212]

*Martyricon:*

O Lord, in the memory of Thy saints all creation keeps feast: the heavens rejoice with the Angels, and the earth makes glad with men; by their prayers have mercy on us.

*Glory... Both now... Staurotheotokion:*

When the all-immaculate one beheld Thee suspended upon the Cross, weeping maternally she cried aloud: O my Son and my God, my sweetest Child: how dost Thou endure this reproachful Passion?

*At the Divine Liturgy, the Beatitudes, Tone 6:*

*Troparion 1:*

Remember me, O God, my Savior, when Thou comest in Thy Kingdom, and save me, as Thou art the only Lover of mankind!

*Troparion 2:*

Stretching out Thy palms upon the Tree willingly, Thou didst unite the nations who sing of Thy tender-heartedness, O Christ our God.

*Troparion 3:*

Thou wast nailed upon the Tree willingly, and by Thy power, O Christ, Thou hast utterly crushed the tormentor's sting.

*Martyricon:*

Nourished by torments, O passion-bearers, ye were accounted worthy of the sweetness of Paradise, unceasingly praying for the world.

*Glory... Trinity Hymn:*

Deliver us from every torment, O Father, and Son, and co-enthroned Spirit: save Thy servants, O Lord!

*Both now... Theotokion:*

Beholding Thee stretched out upon the Cross, the Virgin sighed with tears: by her prayers save us, O Lord!

*Prokeimenon, Tone 7: Exalt ye the Lord our God, and worship at His footstool, for He is holy. (Ps. 98:5) Verse: The Lord is King, let the peoples rage. (Ps. 98:1)*

*The Epistle of the Holy Apostle Paul to the Corinthians, Section 125 (1 Cor. 1:18–24).*

[f. 136A] Brethren: The preaching of the Cross is to them that perish foolishness; but unto us which are saved it is the power of God. For it is written: I will destroy the wisdom of the wise, and will bring to nothing the understanding of the prudent. Where is the wise? Where is the scribe? Where is the disputer of this world? Has not God made foolish the wisdom of this world? For after that in the wisdom of God the world by wisdom knew not God, it pleased God by the foolishness of preaching to save them that believe. For the Jews require a sign, and the Greeks seek after wisdom: but we preach Christ crucified, unto the Jews a stumblingblock, and unto the Greeks foolishness; but unto them which are called, both Jews and Greeks, Christ the power of God, and the wisdom of God.

*Alleluia, Tone 4.*

*The Gospel according to Mark, Section 37 (Mark 8:34–9:1):*

The Lord said: Whosoever will come after Me, let him deny himself, and take up his cross, and follow Me. For whosoever will save his life shall lose it; but whosoever shall lose his life for My sake and the Gospel's, the same shall save it. For what shall it profit a man, if he shall gain the whole world, and lose his own soul? Or what shall a man give in exchange for his soul? Whosoever therefore shall be ashamed of Me and of My words in this adulterous and sinful generation, of him also shall the Son of man be ashamed, when He comes in the glory of His Father with the holy angels. And He said unto them: Truly I say unto you, That there be some of them that stand here, which shall not taste of death, till they have seen the Kingdom of God come with power.

*Communion Verse: The light of Thy countenance has been signed upon us, O Lord. (Ps. 4:6)*

(see fol. 194)[^213]

[An ornamental woodcut headpiece with foliate strapwork, foliate motifs, and a central cartouche with folio number 194.]

## FRIDAY EVENING VESPERS (FOR SATURDAY) (TONE 8)

*On Friday Evening, at "Lord, I have cried," Stichera to the Martyrs, Tone 8.*

*Podoben: "What shall we call you, O holy ones" (Что вы наречемъ):*

*Sticheron 1:*

Wounded am I, wretched one, ever by the arrows of the enemy, and what to do I know not; but his arrows have ye crushed, who suffered valiantly; and by the streams of your blood ye sanctify all creation: heal me who am sick, O divine martyrs, as ye have boldness unto the Lord, O right-blessed ones!

*Sticheron 2:*

Wholly held by perplexity am I, when I bring to mind that dreadful hour, and the searching of Thy judgment, O God of all; and I weep, and mourn, and lament, remembering the abyss of mine evils; therefore I reverence Thee: save me, O Lover of mankind, by the supplications of Thy martyrs, and deliver me from torment, as Thou art tender-hearted!

*Sticheron 3:*

For those who celebrate in faith your all-holy memory, pray God, O divine martyrs, that they may be delivered from temptations, and obtain the heavenly mansions of those whom He has translated, as the only Benefactor and Lover of mankind; and that He may appoint their souls where the ranks of the Patriarchs ever make glad, as He is long-suffering.

[f. 136A] *Other Stichera to the Martyrs, the same Tone:*

*Sticheron 1:*

O martyrs of the Lord, ye sanctify every place and heal every sickness; and now pray that our souls may be delivered from the snares of the enemy, we entreat you.

*Sticheron 2:*

O martyrs of the Lord, pray our God, and ask for our souls a multitude of compassions, and cleansing of many transgressions, we entreat you.

*Sticheron 3:*

O invincible martyrs of Christ, having conquered deceit by the power of the Cross, receiving the grace of eternal life, ye feared not the torturers' threatenings; wounded by torments, ye make glad: and now your blood has become healing for our souls; pray that our souls may be saved!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion (Tone 8 Dogmatikon incipit): "The King of heaven, for His love of mankind..."*

*Prokeimenon, Tone 8: O God, Thou art my defender, and Thy mercy shall go before me. (Ps. 58:9b, 10b) Verse: Deliver me from mine enemies, O God, and protect me from them that rise up against me. (Ps. 58:1)*

*At the Aposticha, Stichera to the Martyrs, Tone 8:*

*Sticheron 1:*
